/* ================= FILE: ./src/business/data-plan.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Data Plan & Bandwidth Overuse Monitor
 * Implements Rule BR-06:
 * - Only External/Internet traffic (is_local_lan = 0) is billed
 * - Calculates cycle usage from the configured billing day
 * - Triggers warning alert at warnThresholdPercent (80%)
 * - Triggers critical limit alert at 100%
 */

import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters.js';

export class DataPlanMonitor {
  constructor(trafficRepo, alertsRepo, config = {}) {
    this.trafficRepo = trafficRepo;
    this.alertsRepo = alertsRepo;
    this.config = {
      enabled: config.enabled !== undefined ? config.enabled : true,
      limitBytes: config.limitBytes || (100 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024), // 100 GB
      billingDayOfMonth: config.billingDayOfMonth || 1,
      warnThresholdPercent: config.warnThresholdPercent || 80,
      adapterId: config.adapterId || null,
      lastWarnFired: false,
      lastLimitFired: false
    };
  }

  updateConfig(newConfig) {
    Object.assign(this.config, newConfig);
    // Reset alert flags if limit increased
    this.config.lastWarnFired = false;
    this.config.lastLimitFired = false;
  }

  getBillingCycleStartTimestamp() {
    const now = new Date();
    const cycleStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), this.config.billingDayOfMonth, 0, 0, 0);
    // If today is before billing day in this month, cycle started previous month
    if (now.getDate() < this.config.billingDayOfMonth) {
      cycleStart.setMonth(cycleStart.getMonth() - 1);
    }
    return Math.floor(cycleStart.getTime() / 1000);
  }

  auditCurrentUsage() {
    if (!this.config.enabled) return null;

    const startTsSec = this.getBillingCycleStartTimestamp();
    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    // BR-06: Only count external Internet traffic (is_local_lan = 0)
    const summary = this.trafficRepo.getTotalsForRange(startTsSec, nowSec, {
      isLocalLan: 0,
      adapterId: this.config.adapterId
    });

    const usedBytes = summary.totalBytes;
    const limitBytes = this.config.limitBytes;
    const percentUsed = limitBytes > 0 ? (usedBytes / limitBytes) * 100 : 0;

    // Check warning threshold (80%)
    if (percentUsed >= this.config.warnThresholdPercent && !this.config.lastWarnFired) {
      this.config.lastWarnFired = true;
      this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'DATA_PLAN_WARNING',
        severity: 2, // Warning
        title: `Data Plan Warning: ${percentUsed.toFixed(1)}% Reached`,
        details_json: {
          usedBytes,
          limitBytes,
          formattedUsed: formatBytes(usedBytes),
          formattedLimit: formatBytes(limitBytes),
          threshold: this.config.warnThresholdPercent
        }
      });
    }

    // Check critical limit exceeded (100%)
    if (percentUsed >= 100 && !this.config.lastLimitFired) {
      this.config.lastLimitFired = true;
      this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'DATA_PLAN_EXCEEDED',
        severity: 3, // Critical
        title: 'Data Plan Limit Exceeded!',
        details_json: {
          usedBytes,
          limitBytes,
          formattedUsed: formatBytes(usedBytes),
          formattedLimit: formatBytes(limitBytes),
          exceededBy: formatBytes(usedBytes - limitBytes)
        }
      });
    }

    return {
      usedBytes,
      limitBytes,
      percentUsed: Math.min(100, percentUsed),
      rawPercent: percentUsed,
      isExceeded: percentUsed >= 100,
      isWarning: percentUsed >= this.config.warnThresholdPercent,
      formattedUsed: formatBytes(usedBytes),
      formattedLimit: formatBytes(limitBytes)
    };
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/business/data-plan.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/business/firewall-policy.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Firewall Policy Controller
 * Implements:
 * - BR-01: Applications keyed by normalized lowercase full path
 * - BR-05: Critical system exemptions in BLOCK_ALL (DHCP UDP 67/68, Loopback 127.0.0.1)
 * - Modes: CLICK_TO_BLOCK, ASK_TO_CONNECT, BLOCK_ALL
 * - Profiles: Default, Public Wi-Fi, Work
 *
 * AUDIT FIXES (Batch #2):
 *  - FUNC-03: setProfile() now pushes changed rules to the kernel via the daemon,
 *    instead of only updating the UI state.
 *  - FUNC-07: enforcement failures are reported through onEnforceError
 *    (wired to a critical toast in app.js — Batch #3) instead of being swallowed.
 */
import { FIREWALL_MODES } from '../config/default-settings.js';

export class FirewallPolicyController {
  constructor(appsRepo, alertsRepo, options = {}) {
    this.appsRepo = appsRepo;
    this.alertsRepo = alertsRepo;
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== undefined ? options.enabled : true;
    this.mode = options.mode || FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK;
    this.activeProfile = options.profile || 'default';
    this.onEnforceError = options.onEnforceError || null;
    // Pending connection requests for ASK_TO_CONNECT mode
    this.pendingConnections = new Map(); // key: exe_path -> { app, resolve, reject, timestamp }
    // Multi-profile state storage: profileId -> Set of blocked exe_paths
    this.profileRules = {
      default: new Set(),
        public_wifi: new Set(),
          work: new Set()
    };
  }

  /** Central enforce call with failure reporting (AUDIT FUNC-07) */
  _enforce(payload) {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof fetch !== 'function') return;
    fetch('/api/firewall/enforce', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    }).catch(() => {
      if (this.onEnforceError) this.onEnforceError();
    });
  }

  setEnabled(enabled) {
    this.enabled = !!enabled;
    if (!this.enabled) {
      this._enforce({ unblockAll: true });
    } else {
      for (const app of this.appsRepo.getAll()) {
        if (app.firewall_state === 1) {
          this._enforce({ exePath: app.exe_path, blocked: true });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  isEnabled() {
    return this.enabled;
  }

  setMode(mode) {
    if (Object.values(FIREWALL_MODES).includes(mode)) {
      this.mode = mode;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  getMode() {
    return this.mode;
  }

  setProfile(profileId) {
    if (this.profileRules[profileId]) {
      this.activeProfile = profileId;
      // Sync applications firewall_state to match current profile
      const blockedSet = this.profileRules[profileId];
      const apps = this.appsRepo.getAll();
      for (const app of apps) {
        const isBlocked = blockedSet.has(app.exe_path);
        const changed = (app.firewall_state === 1) !== isBlocked;
        this.appsRepo.setFirewallState(app.app_id, isBlocked ? 1 : 0);
        // AUDIT FUNC-03: profile switch must reach the kernel, not just the UI
        if (changed) {
          this._enforce({ exePath: app.exe_path, blocked: isBlocked && this.enabled });
        }
      }
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  getProfile() {
    return this.activeProfile;
  }

  initFromRepo() {
    const blockedSet = this.profileRules[this.activeProfile];
    for (const app of this.appsRepo.getAll()) {
      if (app.firewall_state === 1) {
        blockedSet.add(app.exe_path);
        if (this.enabled) {
          this._enforce({ exePath: app.exe_path, blocked: true });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  isApplicationBlocked(exePath, portNumber = 0, ipProtocol = 6, destinationIp = '') {
    // If master firewall is turned off, all apps are allowed
    if (!this.enabled) return false;
    // BR-05: Exemption for critical system networking in BLOCK_ALL
    if (this.mode === FIREWALL_MODES.BLOCK_ALL) {
      // Loopback traffic is never blocked
      if (destinationIp && destinationIp.startsWith('127.')) return false;
      // DHCP (UDP 67/68) is exempt
      if (ipProtocol === 17 && (portNumber === 67 || portNumber === 68)) return false;
      // All other traffic is blocked in BLOCK_ALL
      return true;
    }
    // Check specific application rule by normalized lowercase path (BR-01)
    const normalizedPath = exePath ? exePath.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
    const app = this.appsRepo.getByPath(normalizedPath);
    if (!app) return false;
    return app.firewall_state === 1; // 1 = Blocked
  }

  toggleBlock(appId) {
    const app = this.appsRepo.getById(appId);
    if (!app) return null;
    const newState = app.firewall_state === 1 ? 0 : 1;
    this.appsRepo.setFirewallState(appId, newState);
    // Save to current profile set
    const blockedSet = this.profileRules[this.activeProfile];
    if (newState === 1) {
      blockedSet.add(app.exe_path);
    } else {
      blockedSet.delete(app.exe_path);
    }
    // Enforce live OS network & process block in Rust backend
    this._enforce({ exePath: app.exe_path, blocked: newState === 1 && this.enabled });
    return newState;
  }

  /**
   * Handle incoming connection attempt from an application.
   * If in ASK_TO_CONNECT mode and app is unknown/unapproved, holds the request and triggers a prompt.
   */
  async evaluateConnection(appInfo, connectionInfo) {
    if (!this.enabled) return { allowed: true };
    const normalizedPath = appInfo.exe_path.trim().toLowerCase();
    let app = this.appsRepo.getByPath(normalizedPath);
    // If application is newly seen
    const isFirstTime = !app;
    if (!app) {
      app = this.appsRepo.register(appInfo);
      this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'FIRST_NETWORK_ACTIVITY',
        severity: 1, // Info
        app_id: app.app_id,
        title: `First network activity for ${app.app_name}`,
        details_json: { path: app.exe_path, destination: connectionInfo.destination_ip }
      });
    }
    // BLOCK_ALL check with BR-05 exemptions
    if (this.mode === FIREWALL_MODES.BLOCK_ALL) {
      const isExempt = (connectionInfo.ip_protocol === 17 && (connectionInfo.port === 67 || connectionInfo.port === 68)) ||
      (connectionInfo.destination_ip && connectionInfo.destination_ip.startsWith('127.'));
      if (!isExempt) {
        return { allowed: false, reason: 'BLOCK_ALL_LOCKDOWN' };
      }
      return { allowed: true };
    }
    // If app already blocked in database
    if (app.firewall_state === 1) {
      return { allowed: false, reason: 'EXPLICITLY_BLOCKED' };
    }
    // ASK_TO_CONNECT Mode
    if (this.mode === FIREWALL_MODES.ASK_TO_CONNECT && isFirstTime) {
      // Mark as pending ask
      this.appsRepo.setFirewallState(app.app_id, 2);
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        this.pendingConnections.set(normalizedPath, {
          app,
          connectionInfo,
          resolve,
          timestamp: Date.now()
        });
      });
    }
    return { allowed: true };
  }

  resolvePendingConnection(exePath, allow) {
    const normalizedPath = exePath.trim().toLowerCase();
    const pending = this.pendingConnections.get(normalizedPath);
    if (!pending) return false;
    const newState = allow ? 0 : 1;
    this.appsRepo.setFirewallState(pending.app.app_id, newState);
    const blockedSet = this.profileRules[this.activeProfile];
    if (newState === 1) {
      blockedSet.add(normalizedPath);
    } else {
      blockedSet.delete(normalizedPath);
    }
    this.pendingConnections.delete(normalizedPath);
    pending.resolve({ allowed: allow, userDecision: true });
    return true;
  }

  getPendingConnections() {
    return Array.from(this.pendingConnections.values());
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/business/firewall-policy.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/business/security-score.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Security Score & Statistical Anomaly Detector
 * Implements the hybrid local evaluation engine documented in Section 7.7 & FUNC-05
 */

export class SecurityScoreEngine {
  constructor(appsRepo, alertsRepo) {
    this.appsRepo = appsRepo;
    this.alertsRepo = alertsRepo;

    // Moving statistics cache for Anomaly Detection: appId -> { samples: number[], mean: number, stdDev: number }
    this.trafficHistory = new Map();
  }

  calculateSecurityScore(app) {
    let score = 50; // Neutral baseline

    // 1. Digital Signature Check (+40 pts)
    if (app.is_signed) {
      score += 40;
    } else {
      score -= 20; // Unverified publisher penalty
    }

    // 2. Protected OS / System Directory Check (+20 pts)
    const normalized = (app.exe_path || '').toLowerCase();
    if (
      normalized.startsWith('/usr/bin/') ||
      normalized.startsWith('/usr/lib/') ||
      normalized.startsWith('/usr/sbin/') ||
      normalized.startsWith('/bin/') ||
      normalized.startsWith('/sbin/') ||
      normalized.startsWith('/opt/')
    ) {
      score += 20;
    } else if (normalized.includes('/tmp/') || normalized.startsWith('/tmp/') || normalized.includes('/downloads/')) {
      score -= 25; // High-risk execution location penalty
    }

    // 3. VirusTotal Result Check (+30 pts for clean, penalty for positives)
    if (app.vt_total > 0) {
      if (app.vt_positives === 0) {
        score += 30;
      } else {
        score -= (app.vt_positives * 15);
      }
    }

    // Clamp score strictly between 0 and 100
    const finalScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));
    this.appsRepo.setSecurityScore(app.app_id, finalScore);
    return finalScore;
  }

  getScoreRating(score) {
    if (score >= 80) return { label: 'Safe', color: '#2ED573', cssClass: 'score-safe' };
    if (score >= 50) return { label: 'Moderate', color: '#FFA502', cssClass: 'score-warning' };
    return { label: 'Low / Suspicious', color: '#FF4757', cssClass: 'score-danger' };
  }

  /**
   * Evaluates real-time traffic volume against historical baseline for anomalies (Z-Score > 3)
   */
  checkTrafficAnomaly(appId, currentRateBytesPerSec) {
    const app = this.appsRepo.getById(appId);
    if (!app) return { isAnomaly: false };

    let hist = this.trafficHistory.get(appId);
    if (!hist) {
      hist = { samples: [], sum: 0, sumSq: 0 };
      this.trafficHistory.set(appId, hist);
    }

    hist.samples.push(currentRateBytesPerSec);
    hist.sum += currentRateBytesPerSec;
    hist.sumSq += (currentRateBytesPerSec * currentRateBytesPerSec);

    // Keep window of last 60 samples (1 minute at 1Hz)
    if (hist.samples.length > 60) {
      const removed = hist.samples.shift();
      hist.sum -= removed;
      hist.sumSq -= (removed * removed);
    }

    // Need at least 10 baseline samples before flagging anomalies
    if (hist.samples.length < 10) {
      return { isAnomaly: false };
    }

    const n = hist.samples.length;
    const mean = hist.sum / n;
    const variance = Math.max(0, (hist.sumSq / n) - (mean * mean));
    const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);

    // Flag anomaly if rate exceeds mean + 3 * stdDev and is an absolute surge (> 100KB/s)
    const threshold = mean + (3 * stdDev);
    if (currentRateBytesPerSec > threshold && currentRateBytesPerSec > 100 * 1024) {
      const zScore = stdDev > 0 ? ((currentRateBytesPerSec - mean) / stdDev).toFixed(1) : '3.0+';

      // Log security alert for anomaly
      this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'NETWORK_ANOMALY',
        severity: 2, // Warning
        app_id: appId,
        title: `Unusual network activity detected for ${app.app_name}`,
        details_json: {
          rate: currentRateBytesPerSec,
          baselineMean: Math.round(mean),
          zScore: zScore
        }
      });

      return {
        isAnomaly: true,
        zScore,
        rate: currentRateBytesPerSec,
        threshold
      };
    }

    return { isAnomaly: false };
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/business/security-score.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/business/security-watchdog.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Security Watchdogs & System Monitors
 * Implements Section 5.7 and FUNC-05 / FUNC-07:
 * - ARP Spoofing State Machine
 * - Wi-Fi Evil Twin Detector
 * - Binary Integrity Checker (BR-02)
 * - Hosts File & DNS Server Watcher
 * - Remote Desktop & Proxy Watchdogs
 *
 * AUDIT FIXES (Batch #2):
 *  - FUNC-05: baselines (gateway MAC, DNS servers, hosts-file hash, Wi-Fi BSSID)
 *    are now LEARNED from the live system on first sight and persisted to
 *    localStorage, instead of hardcoded values that fired false-positive
 *    critical alerts on every real network.
 */
export class SecurityWatchdog {
  constructor(alertsRepo, appsRepo, devicesRepo) {
    this.alertsRepo = alertsRepo;
    this.appsRepo = appsRepo;
    this.devicesRepo = devicesRepo;
    // Baselines start empty and are learned from live kernel data (AUDIT FUNC-05)
    this.gatewayState = { ip: null, mac: null };
    this.dnsServers = [];
    this.hostsFileHash = null;
    this.currentWifi = { ssid: null, bssid: null, security: null };
    this.proxyEnabled = false;
    this.isOnline = true;
    this.restoreBaseline();
  }

  /** Store a trusted baseline learned from the live system */
  learnBaseline({ gatewayIp, gatewayMac, dnsServers, wifi } = {}) {
    if (gatewayIp && gatewayMac && gatewayMac !== 'UNKNOWN') {
      this.gatewayState = { ip: gatewayIp, mac: gatewayMac.toUpperCase() };
    }
    if (Array.isArray(dnsServers) && dnsServers.length > 0) {
      this.dnsServers = dnsServers.slice();
    }
    if (wifi && wifi.ssid && wifi.bssid) {
      this.currentWifi = { ssid: wifi.ssid, bssid: wifi.bssid, security: wifi.security || null };
    }
    this._persistBaseline();
  }

  _persistBaseline() {
    if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('cachywire_watchdog_baseline', JSON.stringify({
        g: this.gatewayState,
        d: this.dnsServers,
        w: this.currentWifi
      }));
    } catch {}
  }

  restoreBaseline() {
    if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cachywire_watchdog_baseline') || 'null');
      if (parsed && parsed.g && parsed.g.ip && parsed.g.mac) this.gatewayState = parsed.g;
      if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.d) && parsed.d.length) this.dnsServers = parsed.d;
      if (parsed && parsed.w && parsed.w.ssid) this.currentWifi = parsed.w;
    } catch {}
  }

  // 1. ARP Spoofing State Machine (Section 5.7 #1)
  checkArpTable(arpEntries) {
    const entries = Array.isArray(arpEntries) ? arpEntries : [];
    // No trusted baseline yet => learn silently from live kernel data (prefer the .1 router)
    if (!this.gatewayState.mac) {
      const gw = entries.find(e => e.ip && e.ip.endsWith('.1') && e.mac && e.mac !== 'UNKNOWN') || entries[0];
      if (gw && gw.mac && gw.mac !== 'UNKNOWN') {
        this.learnBaseline({ gatewayIp: gw.ip, gatewayMac: gw.mac });
      }
      return null;
    }
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.ip === this.gatewayState.ip) {
        if (entry.mac && entry.mac !== 'UNKNOWN' &&
          entry.mac.toUpperCase() !== this.gatewayState.mac.toUpperCase()) {
          return this.alertsRepo.create({
            alert_type: 'ARP_SPOOF',
            severity: 3, // Critical
            title: 'ARP Spoofing Detected!',
            details_json: {
              gatewayIp: this.gatewayState.ip,
              originalMac: this.gatewayState.mac,
              spoofedMac: entry.mac,
              description: 'Gateway MAC address has changed or is being intercepted by an attacker.'
            }
          });
          }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 2. Wi-Fi Evil Twin Detector (Section 5.7 #2)
  checkNearbyWifi(beacons) {
    if (!Array.isArray(beacons) || beacons.length === 0) return null;
    // First run: learn the current network as the trusted baseline silently
    if (!this.currentWifi.ssid) {
      this.learnBaseline({ wifi: beacons[0] });
      return null;
    }
    for (const b of beacons) {
      if (b.ssid === this.currentWifi.ssid && b.bssid !== this.currentWifi.bssid) {
        if (b.security === 'Open' || b.security === 'None' || b.security !== this.currentWifi.security) {
          return this.alertsRepo.create({
            alert_type: 'WIFI_EVIL_TWIN',
            severity: 3, // Critical
            title: `Wi-Fi Evil Twin Warning: ${b.ssid}`,
            details_json: {
              ssid: b.ssid,
              fakeBssid: b.bssid,
              realSecurity: this.currentWifi.security,
              fakeSecurity: b.security,
              description: 'An untrusted access point with matching SSID and weaker encryption was detected.'
            }
          });
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 3. Binary Integrity & Modification Check (BR-02, Section 5.7 #3)
  verifyBinaryIntegrity(appId, newSha256, newVersion = null) {
    const app = this.appsRepo.getById(appId);
    if (!app) return null;
    if (app.sha256_hash && app.sha256_hash !== newSha256) {
      const oldHash = app.sha256_hash;
      this.appsRepo.update(appId, {
        sha256_hash: newSha256,
        file_version: newVersion || app.file_version,
        last_seen_ts: Date.now()
      });
      return this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'APP_CHANGED',
        severity: 2, // Warning
        app_id: appId,
        title: `Application binary modified: ${app.app_name}`,
        details_json: {
          path: app.exe_path,
          previousSha256: oldHash,
          newSha256: newSha256,
          publisher: app.publisher
        }
      });
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 4. Hosts File Watcher
  verifyHostsFile(currentContentHash) {
    if (!this.hostsFileHash) {
      this.hostsFileHash = currentContentHash; // learn baseline silently
      return null;
    }
    if (this.hostsFileHash !== currentContentHash) {
      this.hostsFileHash = currentContentHash;
      return this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'HOSTS_CHANGED',
        severity: 3, // Critical
        title: 'System Hosts file (/etc/hosts) was modified',
                                    details_json: {
                                      path: '/etc/hosts',
                                      action: 'DNS redirection / domain override detected'
                                    }
      });
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 5. DNS Server Watcher
  verifyDnsServers(newDnsList) {
    if (!Array.isArray(newDnsList) || newDnsList.length === 0) return null;
    if (this.dnsServers.length === 0) {
      this.learnBaseline({ dnsServers: newDnsList }); // learn silently on first run
      return null;
    }
    const isDifferent = newDnsList.length !== this.dnsServers.length ||
    newDnsList.some((ip, idx) => ip !== this.dnsServers[idx]);
    if (isDifferent) {
      const previous = [...this.dnsServers];
      this.dnsServers = newDnsList;
      return this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'DNS_CHANGED',
        severity: 2, // Warning
        title: 'Network DNS servers changed',
        details_json: {
          previousServers: previous,
          newServers: newDnsList
        }
      });
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 6. RDP Remote Connection Detector
  notifyRdpSession(clientIp, username = 'Administrator') {
    return this.alertsRepo.create({
      alert_type: 'RDP_CONNECTION',
      severity: 2, // Warning
      title: `Remote Desktop connection from ${clientIp}`,
      details_json: { clientIp, username, port: 3389 }
    });
  }

  // 7. Proxy Settings Watcher
  verifyProxySettings(isProxyActive, proxyUrl = '') {
    if (this.proxyEnabled !== isProxyActive) {
      this.proxyEnabled = isProxyActive;
      return this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: 'PROXY_SETTINGS_CHANGED',
        severity: 2, // Warning
        title: isProxyActive ? 'System Proxy enabled' : 'System Proxy disabled',
        details_json: { proxyUrl, active: isProxyActive }
      });
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 8. Internet Connection Loss / Restore
  setInternetStatus(online) {
    if (this.isOnline !== online) {
      this.isOnline = online;
      return this.alertsRepo.create({
        alert_type: online ? 'INTERNET_RESTORED' : 'INTERNET_LOST',
        severity: online ? 1 : 2,
        title: online ? 'Internet access restored' : 'Internet access lost / offline',
        details_json: { isOnline: online, timestamp: Date.now() }
      });
    }
    return null;
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/business/security-watchdog.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/business/traffic-classifier.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Network Traffic Classifier
 * Implements Rule BR-03: Strict LAN vs Internet/External Segregation
 * RFC 1918, RFC 3927 (Link-Local), Loopback
 */

export function isLocalLanAddress(ip) {
  if (!ip) return false;
  const cleanIp = ip.trim().toLowerCase();

  // IPv6 Loopback & Link-local
  if (cleanIp === '::1' || cleanIp.startsWith('fe80:')) {
    return true;
  }

  // IPv4 Loopback (127.0.0.0/8)
  if (cleanIp.startsWith('127.')) {
    return true;
  }

  // RFC 1918 Class A: 10.0.0.0/8
  if (cleanIp.startsWith('10.')) {
    return true;
  }

  // RFC 1918 Class B: 172.16.0.0 - 172.31.255.255
  if (cleanIp.startsWith('172.')) {
    const parts = cleanIp.split('.');
    if (parts.length >= 2) {
      const secondOctet = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
      if (secondOctet >= 16 && secondOctet <= 31) {
        return true;
      }
    }
  }

  // RFC 1918 Class C: 192.168.0.0/16
  if (cleanIp.startsWith('192.168.')) {
    return true;
  }

  // Link-Local: 169.254.0.0/16
  if (cleanIp.startsWith('169.254.')) {
    return true;
  }

  // Otherwise, public Internet traffic
  return false;
}

export function classifyTrafficFlow(flow) {
  const isLan = isLocalLanAddress(flow.destination_ip);
  return {
    ...flow,
    is_local_lan: isLan ? 1 : 0
  };
}

/* ================= END: ./src/business/traffic-classifier.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/business/virustotal.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - VirusTotal Integration
 * Implements Section 2 (FUNC-08): VirusTotal API v3 hash inspection,
 * caching results, handling rate limits (4 req/min for free tier),
 * and offline fallback.
 */

export class VirusTotalClient {
  constructor(appsRepo, options = {}) {
    this.appsRepo = appsRepo;
    this.apiKey = options.apiKey || '';
    this.autoScan = options.autoScan || false;
    this.cache = new Map(); // sha256 -> { positives, total, lastScan }
  }

  setApiKey(key) {
    this.apiKey = (key || '').trim();
  }

  setAutoScan(enabled) {
    this.autoScan = !!enabled;
  }

  async scanApplication(appId) {
    const app = this.appsRepo.getById(appId);
    if (!app) return { error: 'Application not found' };

    const hash = app.sha256_hash;
    if (this.cache.has(hash)) {
      const cached = this.cache.get(hash);
      this.appsRepo.setVirusTotalResult(appId, cached.positives, cached.total);
      return cached;
    }

    // If no API key is provided, provide deterministic offline simulation
    if (!this.apiKey) {
      // Deterministic simulation based on app characteristics
      let positives = 0;
      const total = 72;

      if (!app.is_signed && (app.exe_path.includes('temp') || app.exe_path.includes('updater_temp'))) {
        positives = 5; // Matches Figure 2-4 in PDF: updater_temp.exe has 5 / 72 detections
      } else if (app.app_name.includes('Malware') || app.app_name.includes('Suspicious')) {
        positives = 14;
      }

      const result = {
        positives,
        total,
        permalink: `https://www.virustotal.com/gui/file/${hash}`,
        simulated: true
      };

      this.cache.set(hash, result);
      this.appsRepo.setVirusTotalResult(appId, positives, total);
      return result;
    }

    // Real API v3 call if key is provided and online
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`https://www.virustotal.com/api/v3/files/${hash}`, {
        headers: {
          'x-apikey': this.apiKey
        }
      });

      if (response.status === 429) {
        return { error: 'VirusTotal API quota exceeded (free tier limit 4 requests/min)' };
      }
      if (response.status === 404) {
        return { positives: 0, total: 72, notFound: true, message: 'Hash not seen on VirusTotal' };
      }
      if (!response.ok) {
        return { error: `VirusTotal HTTP Error: ${response.status}` };
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      const stats = data.data?.attributes?.last_analysis_stats || {};
      const positives = (stats.malicious || 0) + (stats.suspicious || 0);
      const total = Object.values(stats).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 72;

      const result = {
        positives,
        total,
        permalink: `https://www.virustotal.com/gui/file/${hash}`,
        simulated: false
      };

      this.cache.set(hash, result);
      this.appsRepo.setVirusTotalResult(appId, positives, total);
      return result;
    } catch (e) {
      return { error: 'Network error connecting to VirusTotal (working offline)' };
    }
  }
}

/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Security Alerts Repository
 */

export class AlertsRepository {
  constructor(db) {
    this.db = db;
  }

  create(alertData) {
    return this.db.createAlert(alertData);
  }

  getAll(category = 'ALL') {
    return this.db.getAllAlerts(category);
  }

  getUnreadCount() {
    return this.db.getUnreadAlertsCount();
  }

  markAsRead(alertId) {
    return this.db.markAlertAsRead(alertId);
  }

  markAllAsRead() {
    return this.db.markAllAlertsAsRead();
  }

  clear() {
    return this.db.clearAlerts();
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/data/alerts-repo.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/data/applications-repo.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Applications Repository
 */

export class ApplicationsRepository {
  constructor(db) {
    this.db = db;
  }

  register(data) {
    return this.db.registerApplication(data);
  }

  getById(appId) {
    return this.db.getApplication(appId);
  }

  getByPath(path) {
    return this.db.getApplicationByPath(path);
  }

  getAll() {
    return this.db.getAllApplications();
  }

  update(appId, fields) {
    return this.db.updateApplication(appId, fields);
  }

  setFirewallState(appId, state) {
    const res = this.db.updateApplication(appId, { firewall_state: state });
    if (this.db.saveToStorage) {
      this.db.saveToStorage();
    }
    return res;
  }

  setSecurityScore(appId, score) {
    return this.db.updateApplication(appId, { security_score: score });
  }

  setVirusTotalResult(appId, positives, total) {
    return this.db.updateApplication(appId, {
      vt_positives: positives,
      vt_total: total,
      vt_scan_date: Date.now()
    });
  }

  toggleAlertsMuted(appId) {
    const app = this.getById(appId);
    if (!app) return null;
    return this.db.updateApplication(appId, { alerts_muted: app.alerts_muted ? 0 : 1 });
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/data/applications-repo.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/data/db.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Local Storage Engine (SQLite 3 Specification)
 * Reference: Section 5.4, 7.3 (DDL & ER Diagram), 7.5, and 9.5
 *
 * Implements the full 6-table relational schema with string interning,
 * time-series bucketing/downsampling, memory-batch aggregation,
 * and persistent storage with backup/restore capabilities.
 */

export class CachyWireDatabase {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.storageKey = options.storageKey || 'cachywire_database_v3';
    this.isIncognito = false;
    this.dbPath = options.dbPath || '~/.local/share/cachywire/cachywire_db.json';

    // In-memory 6-table tables conforming strictly to Section 7.3 DDL
    this.tables = {
      applications: new Map(),   // key: app_id -> row
      remote_hosts: new Map(),   // key: host_id -> row
      traffic_types: new Map(),  // key: proto_id -> row
      traffic_stats: new Map(),  // key: composite primary key -> row
      lan_devices: new Map(),    // key: device_id -> row
      security_alerts: new Map() // key: alert_id -> row
    };

    // Auto-increment counters
    this.autoIncs = {
      app_id: 1,
      host_id: 1,
      proto_id: 1,
      device_id: 1,
      alert_id: 1
    };

    // Lookup indexes
    this.indexes = {
      appByPath: new Map(),     // normalized lowercase path -> app_id (BR-01)
      hostByIp: new Map(),      // ip_address -> host_id
      protoByKey: new Map(),    // `${ip_protocol}_${port_number}` -> proto_id
      deviceByMac: new Map()    // mac_address -> device_id
    };

    // Memory batch aggregation buffer (Scenario 6 / High Throughput)
    this.batchBuffer = [];
    this.isDirty = false;
  }

  async initialize() {
    const isDesktopGui = typeof window !== 'undefined';

    await this.loadFromStorage();

    if (this.tables.traffic_types.size === 0) {
      this._seedInitialTrafficTypes();
    }

    if (isDesktopGui) {
      return;
    }

    // Headless / Automated Test Suite Seeding (Node.js)
    if (this.tables.remote_hosts.size === 0) {
      this._seedDefaultRemoteHosts();
    }
    if (this.tables.applications.size === 0) {
      this._seedDefaultApplications();
    }
    let hasValidTraffic = false;
    for (const row of this.tables.traffic_stats.values()) {
      if (Number.isFinite(row.bucket_ts) && row.bucket_ts > 0) {
        hasValidTraffic = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!hasValidTraffic) {
      this.tables.traffic_stats.clear();
      this._seedHistoricalTraffic();
    }
    if (this.tables.lan_devices.size === 0) {
      this._seedDefaultLanDevices();
    }
    if (this.tables.security_alerts.size === 0) {
      this._seedDefaultAlerts();
    }
  }

  _seedDefaultAlerts() {
    const now = Date.now();
    const alerts = [
      {
        alert_type: 'FIRST_NETWORK_ACTIVITY',
        severity: 1,
        title: 'First Network Activity: Google Chrome',
        details: { app_name: 'Google Chrome', remote_ip: '142.250.180.14', domain: 'google.com', country: 'US' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 2
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'NEW_DEVICE_ON_NETWORK',
        severity: 2,
        title: 'New Device on LAN: DESKTOP-DEV-RIG',
        details: { hostname: 'DESKTOP-DEV-RIG', ip: '192.168.1.105', mac: '00:15:5D:89:AB:CD', vendor: 'Microsoft' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 3
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'DNS_CHANGED',
        severity: 2,
        title: 'DNS Server Changed',
        details: { old_dns: '192.168.1.1', new_dns: '1.1.1.1', adapter: 'Wi-Fi' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 5
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'APP_CHANGED',
        severity: 3,
        title: 'Application Hash Modified: suspicious_daemon',
        details: { app_name: 'suspicious_daemon', reason: 'SHA-256 binary hash altered on disk' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 8
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'SUSPICIOUS_HOST',
        severity: 3,
        title: 'Suspicious Remote Host Connected',
        details: { remote_ip: '185.220.101.5', reputation: 'Tor Exit Node / Blacklisted' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 10
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'ARP_SPOOF',
        severity: 3,
        title: 'ARP Cache Inconsistency Detected',
        details: { gateway_ip: '192.168.1.1', spoofed_mac: 'DE:AD:BE:EF:00:01' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 14
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'DATA_PLAN_WARNING',
        severity: 2,
        title: 'Bandwidth Plan 75% Warning',
        details: { usage: '7.5 GB', quota: '10.0 GB', percentage: '75%' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 18
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'FIRST_NETWORK_ACTIVITY',
        severity: 1,
        title: 'First Network Activity: Microsoft Teams',
        details: { app_name: 'Microsoft Teams', remote_ip: '52.114.132.88', country: 'US' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 20
      },
      {
        alert_type: 'FIRST_NETWORK_ACTIVITY',
        severity: 1,
        title: 'First Network Activity: bdservicehost (Bitdefender)',
        details: { app_name: 'bdservicehost', remote_ip: '64.233.160.100', country: 'US' },
        time: now - 3600000 * 22
      }
    ];

    alerts.forEach(a => {
      this.createAlert({
        alert_type: a.alert_type,
        severity: a.severity,
        title: a.title,
        details_json: JSON.stringify(a.details),
        app_id: 1
      });
    });
  }

  _seedHistoricalTraffic() {
    const now = Date.now();
    const apps = Array.from(this.tables.applications.values());
    if (apps.length === 0) return;

    // Seed 144 data points (every 10 minutes over the last 24 hours)
    // Generating the exact peak pattern seen in CachyWire 3.x reference
    const tenMins = 10 * 60 * 1000;
    const start24h = now - (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    for (let t = start24h; t <= now; t += tenMins) {
      const hoursAgo = (now - t) / (60 * 60 * 1000);
      let multiplier = 0.05; // baseline night / quiet hours

      // Peak around 16 hours ago (~8:40 AM)
      if (hoursAgo >= 15 && hoursAgo <= 17) {
        multiplier = 0.45;
      }
      // Major peaks between 11:00 AM and 1:00 PM (~12 to 14 hours ago)
      else if (hoursAgo >= 12 && hoursAgo <= 14) {
        multiplier = 1.0; // peak spike (matching Image 2)
      }
      // Afternoon moderate activity
      else if (hoursAgo >= 6 && hoursAgo <= 11) {
        multiplier = 0.25;
      }
      // Recent activity
      else if (hoursAgo < 6) {
        multiplier = 0.15;
      }

      const totalPointBytesIn = Math.round((7.5 * 1024 * 1024) * multiplier * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4));
      const totalPointBytesOut = Math.round((6.8 * 1024 * 1024) * multiplier * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4));

      // Distribute across top applications
      const chrome = apps.find(a => a.app_name.includes('Chrome')) || apps[0];
      const langSrv = apps.find(a => a.app_name.includes('language_server')) || apps[0];
      const teamViewer = apps.find(a => a.app_name.includes('TeamViewer')) || apps[0];
      const firefox = apps.find(a => a.app_name.includes('Firefox')) || apps[0];

      this.recordTrafficFlow({
        timestamp: t,
        bucket_ts: Math.floor(t / 1000),
        resolution_sec: 60,
        adapter_id: 'Ethernet0',
        app_id: chrome.app_id,
        host_id: 1,
        proto_id: 1,
        bytes_in: Math.round(totalPointBytesIn * 0.4),
        bytes_out: Math.round(totalPointBytesOut * 0.1),
        is_local_lan: 0
      });

      this.recordTrafficFlow({
        timestamp: t,
        bucket_ts: Math.floor(t / 1000),
        resolution_sec: 60,
        adapter_id: 'Ethernet0',
        app_id: langSrv.app_id,
        host_id: 2,
        proto_id: 1,
        bytes_in: Math.round(totalPointBytesIn * 0.05),
        bytes_out: Math.round(totalPointBytesOut * 0.8),
        is_local_lan: 0
      });

      this.recordTrafficFlow({
        timestamp: t,
        bucket_ts: Math.floor(t / 1000),
        resolution_sec: 60,
        adapter_id: 'Ethernet0',
        app_id: teamViewer.app_id,
        host_id: 3,
        proto_id: 2,
        bytes_in: Math.round(totalPointBytesIn * 0.45),
        bytes_out: Math.round(totalPointBytesOut * 0.08),
        is_local_lan: 0
      });

      this.recordTrafficFlow({
        timestamp: t,
        bucket_ts: Math.floor(t / 1000),
        resolution_sec: 60,
        adapter_id: 'Ethernet0',
        app_id: firefox.app_id,
        host_id: 4,
        proto_id: 1,
        bytes_in: Math.round(totalPointBytesIn * 0.1),
        bytes_out: Math.round(totalPointBytesOut * 0.02),
        is_local_lan: 0
      });

      // LAN background heartbeat
      this.recordTrafficFlow({
        timestamp: t,
        bucket_ts: Math.floor(t / 1000),
        resolution_sec: 60,
        adapter_id: 'Ethernet0',
        app_id: (apps[1] || apps[0]).app_id,
        host_id: 6,
        proto_id: 3,
        bytes_in: Math.round(15 * 1024 * multiplier),
        bytes_out: Math.round(12 * 1024 * multiplier),
        is_local_lan: 1
      });
    }
  }

  setIncognito(enabled) {
    this.isIncognito = !!enabled;
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Table 1: applications
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  registerApplication(data) {
    if (!data.exe_path) throw new Error('exe_path is required (BR-01)');
    const normalizedPath = data.exe_path.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (this.indexes.appByPath.has(normalizedPath)) {
      const existingId = this.indexes.appByPath.get(normalizedPath);
      const app = this.tables.applications.get(existingId);
      app.last_seen_ts = Date.now();
      if (data.file_version) app.file_version = data.file_version;
      if (data.sha256_hash && app.sha256_hash !== data.sha256_hash) {
        // Handled in binary integrity checker (BR-02)
      }
      this.isDirty = true;
      return app;
    }

    const appId = this.autoIncs.app_id++;
    const now = Date.now();
    const newApp = {
      app_id: appId,
      exe_path: normalizedPath,
      app_name: data.app_name || normalizedPath.split(/[\\/]/).pop(),
      publisher: data.publisher || 'Unknown Publisher',
      is_signed: data.is_signed !== undefined ? (data.is_signed ? 1 : 0) : 0,
      file_version: data.file_version || '1.0.0.0',
      sha256_hash: data.sha256_hash || '',
      icon_blob: data.icon_blob || null,
      security_score: data.security_score !== undefined ? data.security_score : 50,
      vt_positives: data.vt_positives !== undefined ? data.vt_positives : -1,
      vt_total: data.vt_total !== undefined ? data.vt_total : -1,
      vt_scan_date: data.vt_scan_date || null,
      firewall_state: data.firewall_state !== undefined ? data.firewall_state : 0, // 0: Allowed, 1: Blocked, 2: Pending
      alerts_muted: data.alerts_muted ? 1 : 0,
      first_seen_ts: data.first_seen_ts || now,
      last_seen_ts: now
    };

    this.tables.applications.set(appId, newApp);
    this.indexes.appByPath.set(normalizedPath, appId);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return newApp;
  }

  getApplication(appId) {
    return this.tables.applications.get(appId) || null;
  }

  getApplicationByPath(path) {
    if (!path) return null;
    const id = this.indexes.appByPath.get(path.trim().toLowerCase());
    return id ? this.tables.applications.get(id) : null;
  }

  getAllApplications() {
    return Array.from(this.tables.applications.values());
  }

  updateApplication(appId, fields) {
    const app = this.tables.applications.get(appId);
    if (!app) return null;
    Object.assign(app, fields);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return app;
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Table 2: remote_hosts
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  getOrCreateRemoteHost(ipAddress, domainName = '', countryIso2 = 'GLOBAL', asnOrg = '') {
    if (!ipAddress) return null;
    const cleanIp = ipAddress.trim();

    if (this.indexes.hostByIp.has(cleanIp)) {
      const hostId = this.indexes.hostByIp.get(cleanIp);
      const host = this.tables.remote_hosts.get(hostId);
      host.last_seen_ts = Date.now();
      if (domainName && !host.domain_name) host.domain_name = domainName;
      if (countryIso2 && (!host.country_iso2 || host.country_iso2 === 'GLOBAL')) host.country_iso2 = countryIso2;
      return host;
    }

    const hostId = this.autoIncs.host_id++;
    const now = Date.now();
    const newHost = {
      host_id: hostId,
      ip_address: cleanIp,
      domain_name: domainName,
      country_iso2: countryIso2,
      asn_org: asnOrg,
      first_seen_ts: now,
      last_seen_ts: now
    };

    this.tables.remote_hosts.set(hostId, newHost);
    this.indexes.hostByIp.set(cleanIp, hostId);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return newHost;
  }

  getRemoteHost(hostId) {
    return this.tables.remote_hosts.get(hostId) || null;
  }

  getAllRemoteHosts() {
    return Array.from(this.tables.remote_hosts.values());
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Table 3: traffic_types
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  getOrCreateTrafficType(ipProtocol, portNumber, serviceName = '') {
    const key = `${ipProtocol}_${portNumber}`;
    if (this.indexes.protoByKey.has(key)) {
      return this.tables.traffic_types.get(this.indexes.protoByKey.get(key));
    }

    const protoId = this.autoIncs.proto_id++;
    const determinedName = serviceName || this._guessServiceName(ipProtocol, portNumber);
    const newType = {
      proto_id: protoId,
      ip_protocol: ipProtocol, // 6: TCP, 17: UDP, 1: ICMP
      port_number: portNumber,
      service_name: determinedName
    };

    this.tables.traffic_types.set(protoId, newType);
    this.indexes.protoByKey.set(key, protoId);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return newType;
  }

  getAllTrafficTypes() {
    return Array.from(this.tables.traffic_types.values());
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Table 4: traffic_stats (Time-Series Buckets WITHOUT ROWID)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  recordTrafficFlow(stat) {
    // BR-04: In Incognito mode, halt disk persistence
    if (this.isIncognito) return;

    const resolutionSec = stat.resolution_sec || 1;
    const rawSec = Number.isFinite(stat.timestamp)
      ? Math.floor(stat.timestamp / 1000)
      : Number.isFinite(stat.bucket_ts)
        ? Math.floor(stat.bucket_ts)
        : Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    // Align timestamp to bucket resolution
    const bucketTs = Math.floor(rawSec / resolutionSec) * resolutionSec;
    const adapterId = String(stat.adapter_id || 'Ethernet0');
    const appId = stat.app_id;
    const hostId = stat.host_id;
    const protoId = stat.proto_id;
    const isLocalLan = stat.is_local_lan ? 1 : 0; // BR-03

    const key = `${bucketTs}_${resolutionSec}_${adapterId}_${appId}_${hostId}_${protoId}_${isLocalLan}`;

    if (this.tables.traffic_stats.has(key)) {
      const row = this.tables.traffic_stats.get(key);
      row.bytes_in += stat.bytes_in || 0;
      row.bytes_out += stat.bytes_out || 0;
    } else {
      const row = {
        bucket_ts: bucketTs,
        timestamp: bucketTs * 1000,
        resolution_sec: resolutionSec,
        adapter_id: adapterId,
        app_id: appId,
        host_id: hostId,
        proto_id: protoId,
        is_local_lan: isLocalLan,
        bytes_in: stat.bytes_in || 0,
        bytes_out: stat.bytes_out || 0
      };
      this.tables.traffic_stats.set(key, row);
    }
    this.isDirty = true;
  }

  queryTrafficStats({ startTimeSec, endTimeSec, resolutionSec = 1, appId = null, hostId = null, adapterId = null, isLocalLan = null }) {
    const results = [];
    for (const stat of this.tables.traffic_stats.values()) {
      if (resolutionSec !== null && resolutionSec !== 'all' && resolutionSec !== 0 && stat.resolution_sec !== resolutionSec) continue;
      if (startTimeSec && stat.bucket_ts < startTimeSec) continue;
      if (endTimeSec && stat.bucket_ts > endTimeSec) continue;
      if (appId !== null && appId !== undefined && stat.app_id !== appId) continue;
      if (hostId !== null && hostId !== undefined && stat.host_id !== hostId) continue;
      if (adapterId !== null && adapterId !== undefined && stat.adapter_id !== adapterId) continue;
      if (isLocalLan !== null && isLocalLan !== undefined && stat.is_local_lan !== isLocalLan) continue;
      results.push(stat);
    }
    return results.sort((a, b) => a.bucket_ts - b.bucket_ts);
  }

  // Multi-Tier Downsampling Roll-up Engine (Never loses historical network usage)
  runDownsamplingRollup() {
    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    // Tier 1: Roll-up 1s buckets older than 1 hour (3600s) into 60s buckets
    const cutoff1s = nowSec - 3600;
    const oneSecStats = [];
    const keysToDelete1s = [];

    // Tier 2: Roll-up 60s buckets older than 7 days (604800s) into 3600s (1h) buckets
    const cutoff60s = nowSec - (7 * 86400);
    const sixtySecStats = [];
    const keysToDelete60s = [];

    // Tier 3: Roll-up 3600s buckets older than 90 days into 86400s (1d) buckets
    const cutoff3600s = nowSec - (90 * 86400);
    const hourlyStats = [];
    const keysToDelete3600s = [];

    for (const [key, stat] of this.tables.traffic_stats.entries()) {
      const res = stat.resolution_sec || 1;
      if (res === 1 && stat.bucket_ts < cutoff1s) {
        oneSecStats.push(stat);
        keysToDelete1s.push(key);
      } else if (res === 60 && stat.bucket_ts < cutoff60s) {
        sixtySecStats.push(stat);
        keysToDelete60s.push(key);
      } else if (res === 3600 && stat.bucket_ts < cutoff3600s) {
        hourlyStats.push(stat);
        keysToDelete3600s.push(key);
      }
    }

    // 1. Roll-up 1s -> 60s
    if (oneSecStats.length > 0) {
      for (const stat of oneSecStats) {
        const bucket60s = Math.floor(stat.bucket_ts / 60) * 60;
        const key60 = `${bucket60s}_60_${stat.adapter_id}_${stat.app_id}_${stat.host_id}_${stat.proto_id}_${stat.is_local_lan}`;
        if (this.tables.traffic_stats.has(key60)) {
          const row = this.tables.traffic_stats.get(key60);
          row.bytes_in += stat.bytes_in;
          row.bytes_out += stat.bytes_out;
        } else {
          this.tables.traffic_stats.set(key60, {
            ...stat,
            bucket_ts: bucket60s,
            timestamp: bucket60s * 1000,
            resolution_sec: 60
          });
        }
      }
      for (const key of keysToDelete1s) {
        this.tables.traffic_stats.delete(key);
      }
      this.isDirty = true;
    }

    // 2. Roll-up 60s -> 3600s (1h)
    if (sixtySecStats.length > 0) {
      for (const stat of sixtySecStats) {
        const bucket3600s = Math.floor(stat.bucket_ts / 3600) * 3600;
        const key3600 = `${bucket3600s}_3600_${stat.adapter_id}_${stat.app_id}_${stat.host_id}_${stat.proto_id}_${stat.is_local_lan}`;
        if (this.tables.traffic_stats.has(key3600)) {
          const row = this.tables.traffic_stats.get(key3600);
          row.bytes_in += stat.bytes_in;
          row.bytes_out += stat.bytes_out;
        } else {
          this.tables.traffic_stats.set(key3600, {
            ...stat,
            bucket_ts: bucket3600s,
            timestamp: bucket3600s * 1000,
            resolution_sec: 3600
          });
        }
      }
      for (const key of keysToDelete60s) {
        this.tables.traffic_stats.delete(key);
      }
      this.isDirty = true;
    }

    // 3. Roll-up 3600s -> 86400s (1 day)
    if (hourlyStats.length > 0) {
      for (const stat of hourlyStats) {
        const bucket86400s = Math.floor(stat.bucket_ts / 86400) * 86400;
        const key86400 = `${bucket86400s}_86400_${stat.adapter_id}_${stat.app_id}_${stat.host_id}_${stat.proto_id}_${stat.is_local_lan}`;
        if (this.tables.traffic_stats.has(key86400)) {
          const row = this.tables.traffic_stats.get(key86400);
          row.bytes_in += stat.bytes_in;
          row.bytes_out += stat.bytes_out;
        } else {
          this.tables.traffic_stats.set(key86400, {
            ...stat,
            bucket_ts: bucket86400s,
            timestamp: bucket86400s * 1000,
            resolution_sec: 86400
          });
        }
      }
      for (const key of keysToDelete3600s) {
        this.tables.traffic_stats.delete(key);
      }
      this.isDirty = true;
    }

    // High safety ceiling (100,000 rows): only remove daily records older than 2 years if ever exceeded
    if (this.tables.traffic_stats.size > 100000) {
      const twoYearsAgo = nowSec - (730 * 86400);
      for (const [key, stat] of this.tables.traffic_stats.entries()) {
        if (stat.bucket_ts < twoYearsAgo) {
          this.tables.traffic_stats.delete(key);
        }
      }
      this.isDirty = true;
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Table 5: lan_devices ("Things" - Scanner)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  upsertLanDevice(data) {
    if (!data.mac_address) throw new Error('mac_address is required');
    const cleanMac = data.mac_address.toUpperCase().trim();

    if (this.indexes.deviceByMac.has(cleanMac)) {
      const devId = this.indexes.deviceByMac.get(cleanMac);
      const dev = this.tables.lan_devices.get(devId);
      dev.last_seen_ts = Date.now();
      if (data.last_ip) dev.last_ip = data.last_ip;
      if (data.hostname) dev.hostname = data.hostname;
      if (data.custom_name) dev.custom_name = data.custom_name;
      if (data.is_online !== undefined) dev.is_online = data.is_online ? 1 : 0;
      this.isDirty = true;
      return dev;
    }

    const devId = this.autoIncs.device_id++;
    const now = Date.now();
    const newDevice = {
      device_id: devId,
      mac_address: cleanMac,
      last_ip: data.last_ip || '192.168.1.1',
      hostname: data.hostname || 'Device-' + devId,
      custom_name: data.custom_name || '',
      device_type: data.device_type || 'generic',
      vendor_name: data.vendor_name || 'Standard Manufacturer',
      is_online: data.is_online !== undefined ? (data.is_online ? 1 : 0) : 1,
      first_seen_ts: now,
      last_seen_ts: now
    };

    this.tables.lan_devices.set(devId, newDevice);
    this.indexes.deviceByMac.set(cleanMac, devId);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return newDevice;
  }

  getAllLanDevices() {
    return Array.from(this.tables.lan_devices.values());
  }

  updateLanDevice(devId, fields) {
    const dev = this.tables.lan_devices.get(devId);
    if (!dev) return null;
    Object.assign(dev, fields);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return dev;
  }

  deleteLanDevice(devId) {
    const dev = this.tables.lan_devices.get(devId);
    if (!dev) return false;
    this.indexes.deviceByMac.delete(dev.mac_address);
    this.tables.lan_devices.delete(devId);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return true;
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Table 6: security_alerts (Security Event Log)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  createAlert({ alert_type, severity = 1, app_id = null, device_id = null, title, details_json = '{}' }) {
    const alertId = this.autoIncs.alert_id++;
    const now = Date.now();
    const newAlert = {
      alert_id: alertId,
      created_ts: now,
      alert_type,
      severity, // 1: Info, 2: Warning, 3: Critical
      app_id,
      device_id,
      title,
      details_json: typeof details_json === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(details_json) : String(details_json),
      is_read: 0
    };

    this.tables.security_alerts.set(alertId, newAlert);
    this.isDirty = true;
    return newAlert;
  }

  getAllAlerts(category = 'ALL') {
    const alerts = Array.from(this.tables.security_alerts.values());
    if (category === 'ALL') return alerts.sort((a, b) => b.created_ts - a.created_ts);

    return alerts.filter(a => {
      if (category === 'SECURITY') return a.severity === 3 || a.alert_type.includes('SPOOF') || a.alert_type.includes('ANOMALY') || a.alert_type.includes('EVIL_TWIN');
      if (category === 'SYSTEM') return a.alert_type.includes('HOSTS') || a.alert_type.includes('DNS') || a.alert_type.includes('CHANGED') || a.alert_type.includes('RDP');
      if (category === 'NETWORK') return a.alert_type.includes('FIRST_ACTIVITY') || a.alert_type.includes('DEVICE') || a.alert_type.includes('OUTAGE');
      if (category === 'DATA_PLAN') return a.alert_type.includes('DATA_PLAN');
      return true;
    }).sort((a, b) => b.created_ts - a.created_ts);
  }

  getUnreadAlertsCount() {
    let count = 0;
    for (const a of this.tables.security_alerts.values()) {
      if (a.is_read === 0) count++;
    }
    return count;
  }

  markAlertAsRead(alertId) {
    const alert = this.tables.security_alerts.get(alertId);
    if (alert) {
      alert.is_read = 1;
      this.isDirty = true;
    }
  }

  markAllAlertsAsRead() {
    for (const alert of this.tables.security_alerts.values()) {
      alert.is_read = 1;
    }
    this.isDirty = true;
  }

  clearAlerts() {
    this.tables.security_alerts.clear();
    this.isDirty = true;
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Persistence, Clear History, Backup & Restore
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  clearHistory() {
    // Clears time-series and hosts, but preserves Firewall rules & applications list (Scenario 2)
    this.tables.traffic_stats.clear();
    this.tables.remote_hosts.clear();
    this.indexes.hostByIp.clear();
    this.isDirty = true;
    this.saveToStorage();
  }

  /**
   * Complete Clean Slate Reset: Wipes all recorded traffic, apps, hosts, LAN devices, and alerts
   * so CachyWire starts recording fresh from 0 Bytes on CachyOS.
   */
  clearAllData() {
    this.tables.applications.clear();
    this.tables.remote_hosts.clear();
    this.tables.traffic_types.clear();
    this.tables.traffic_stats.clear();
    this.tables.lan_devices.clear();
    this.tables.security_alerts.clear();

    this.indexes.appByPath.clear();
    this.indexes.hostByIp.clear();
    this.indexes.protoByKey.clear();
    this.indexes.deviceByMac.clear();

    this.autoIncs = {
      app_id: 1,
      host_id: 1,
      proto_id: 1,
      device_id: 1,
      alert_id: 1
    };

    this._seedInitialTrafficTypes();
    this.isDirty = true;
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.removeItem(this.storageKey);
      this.saveToStorage();
    }
  }

  async saveToStorage() {
    if (this.isIncognito) return;
    try {
      this.runDownsamplingRollup();
      const dump = {
        version: '3.11.1164',
        autoIncs: this.autoIncs,
        applications: Array.from(this.tables.applications.values()),
        remote_hosts: Array.from(this.tables.remote_hosts.values()),
        traffic_types: Array.from(this.tables.traffic_types.values()),
        traffic_stats: Array.from(this.tables.traffic_stats.values()),
        lan_devices: Array.from(this.tables.lan_devices.values()),
        security_alerts: Array.from(this.tables.security_alerts.values())
      };
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(dump);
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(this.storageKey, serialized);
          const blockedPaths = [];
          for (const a of this.tables.applications.values()) {
            if (a.firewall_state === 1) blockedPaths.push(a.exe_path);
          }
          localStorage.setItem('cachywire_blocked_paths', JSON.stringify(blockedPaths));
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof fetch === 'function') {
        fetch('/api/database/state', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: serialized
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
      this.isDirty = false;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Storage persistence warning:', e);
    }
  }

  async loadFromStorage() {
    try {
      let raw = null;

      // In CachyOS Desktop GUI, load directly from native Rust disk storage (~/.local/share/cachywire/cachywire_db.json)
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof fetch === 'function') {
        try {
          const res = await fetch('/api/database/state', { cache: 'no-store' });
          if (res.ok) {
            const diskText = await res.text();
            if (diskText && diskText.length > 10 && diskText.includes('"applications"')) {
              raw = diskText;
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }

      if (!raw && typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        raw = localStorage.getItem(this.storageKey);
      }

      if (!raw) return;
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!parsed || !parsed.applications) return;

      this.autoIncs = parsed.autoIncs || this.autoIncs;

      for (const app of parsed.applications) {
        this.tables.applications.set(app.app_id, app);
        this.indexes.appByPath.set(app.exe_path, app.app_id);
      }
      for (const host of parsed.remote_hosts) {
        this.tables.remote_hosts.set(host.host_id, host);
        this.indexes.hostByIp.set(host.ip_address, host.host_id);
      }
      for (const proto of parsed.traffic_types) {
        this.tables.traffic_types.set(proto.proto_id, proto);
        this.indexes.protoByKey.set(`${proto.ip_protocol}_${proto.port_number}`, proto.proto_id);
      }
      for (const stat of parsed.traffic_stats) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(stat.bucket_ts)) continue;
        stat.timestamp = stat.timestamp || (stat.bucket_ts * 1000);
        const key = `${stat.bucket_ts}_${stat.resolution_sec}_${stat.adapter_id}_${stat.app_id}_${stat.host_id}_${stat.proto_id}_${stat.is_local_lan}`;
        this.tables.traffic_stats.set(key, stat);
      }
      for (const dev of parsed.lan_devices) {
        this.tables.lan_devices.set(dev.device_id, dev);
        this.indexes.deviceByMac.set(dev.mac_address, dev.device_id);
      }
      for (const alert of parsed.security_alerts) {
        this.tables.security_alerts.set(alert.alert_id, alert);
      }

      // Ensure blocked paths are accurately restored
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        try {
          const savedBlocked = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cachywire_blocked_paths') || '[]');
          if (Array.isArray(savedBlocked) && savedBlocked.length > 0) {
            for (const path of savedBlocked) {
              const appId = this.indexes.appByPath.get(path);
              if (appId) {
                const app = this.tables.applications.get(appId);
                if (app) app.firewall_state = 1;
              }
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      this.runDownsamplingRollup();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Database load corruption recovery triggered:', e);
    }
  }

  exportDatabaseSnapshot() {
    return JSON.stringify({
      schema_version: '3.11.1164',
      export_ts: Date.now(),
      autoIncs: this.autoIncs,
      applications: Array.from(this.tables.applications.values()),
      remote_hosts: Array.from(this.tables.remote_hosts.values()),
      traffic_types: Array.from(this.tables.traffic_types.values()),
      traffic_stats: Array.from(this.tables.traffic_stats.values()),
      lan_devices: Array.from(this.tables.lan_devices.values()),
      security_alerts: Array.from(this.tables.security_alerts.values())
    }, null, 2);
  }

  importDatabaseSnapshot(jsonStr) {
    const data = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
    this.tables.applications.clear();
    this.tables.remote_hosts.clear();
    this.tables.traffic_types.clear();
    this.tables.traffic_stats.clear();
    this.tables.lan_devices.clear();
    this.tables.security_alerts.clear();
    this.indexes.appByPath.clear();
    this.indexes.hostByIp.clear();
    this.indexes.protoByKey.clear();
    this.indexes.deviceByMac.clear();

    this.autoIncs = data.autoIncs || this.autoIncs;
    for (const item of data.applications || []) {
      this.tables.applications.set(item.app_id, item);
      this.indexes.appByPath.set(item.exe_path, item.app_id);
    }
    for (const item of data.remote_hosts || []) {
      this.tables.remote_hosts.set(item.host_id, item);
      this.indexes.hostByIp.set(item.ip_address, item.host_id);
    }
    for (const item of data.traffic_types || []) {
      this.tables.traffic_types.set(item.proto_id, item);
      this.indexes.protoByKey.set(`${item.ip_protocol}_${item.port_number}`, item.proto_id);
    }
    for (const stat of data.traffic_stats || []) {
      const key = `${stat.bucket_ts}_${stat.resolution_sec}_${stat.adapter_id}_${stat.app_id}_${stat.host_id}_${stat.proto_id}_${stat.is_local_lan}`;
      this.tables.traffic_stats.set(key, stat);
    }
    for (const item of data.lan_devices || []) {
      this.tables.lan_devices.set(item.device_id, item);
      this.indexes.deviceByMac.set(item.mac_address, item.device_id);
    }
    for (const item of data.security_alerts || []) {
      this.tables.security_alerts.set(item.alert_id, item);
    }
    this.isDirty = true;
    this.saveToStorage();
    return true;
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Seeders (Initial Baseline)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  _guessServiceName(proto, port) {
    if (proto === 6) { // TCP
      if (port === 443) return 'HTTPS (TLS)';
      if (port === 80) return 'HTTP';
      if (port === 853) return 'DNS-over-TLS (DoT)';
      if (port === 22) return 'SSH';
      if (port === 5938 || port === 5939) return 'TeamViewer Protocol';
      if (port === 9614) return 'Download Accelerator IPC';
      if (port === 3389) return 'RDP';
      if (port === 7010) return 'CachyWire Control';
      return `TCP/${port}`;
    }
    if (proto === 17) { // UDP
      if (port === 443) return 'QUIC / HTTP3 (UDP 443)';
      if (port === 53) return 'DNS (UDP 53)';
      if (port === 67 || port === 68) return 'DHCP';
      if (port === 5353) return 'mDNS (Bonjour/Avahi)';
      if (port === 3478 || port === 19302) return 'WebRTC STUN/TURN';
      return `UDP/${port}`;
    }
    if (proto === 1) return 'ICMP (Ping)';
    return `Proto/${proto}`;
  }

  _seedInitialTrafficTypes() {
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(6, 443, 'HTTPS (TCP 443)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(17, 443, 'QUIC (UDP 443)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(17, 53, 'DNS (UDP 53)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(6, 80, 'HTTP (TCP 80)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(6, 22, 'SSH (TCP 22)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(6, 3389, 'Remote Desktop (RDP)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(17, 67, 'DHCP (UDP 67)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(17, 68, 'DHCP (UDP 68)');
    this.getOrCreateTrafficType(6, 7010, 'CachyWire Control Protocol');
  }

  _seedDefaultRemoteHosts() {
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('142.250.185.14', 'google.com', 'US', 'AS15169 Google LLC');
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('185.199.108.153', 'github.com', 'DE', 'AS36459 GitHub, Inc.');
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('139.162.130.1', 'mirror.cachyos.org', 'DE', 'AS63949 Akamai / CachyOS CDN');
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('104.16.132.229', 'cloudflare.com', 'US', 'AS13335 Cloudflare, Inc.');
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('52.114.132.88', 'teams.microsoft.com', 'US', 'AS8075 Microsoft Corporation');
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('192.168.1.1', 'gateway.local', 'LAN', 'Local Area Network Gateway');
    this.getOrCreateRemoteHost('151.101.1.69', 'static.crates.io', 'GB', 'AS54113 Fastly, Inc.');
  }

  _seedDefaultApplications() {
    const defaultApps = [
      { name: 'NetworkManager', path: '/usr/bin/NetworkManager', pub: 'CachyOS Network Stack', in: 12.5 * 1024 * 1024, out: 2.1 * 1024 * 1024, score: 99, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'systemd-resolved', path: '/usr/lib/systemd/systemd-resolved', pub: 'CachyOS Systemd Core', in: 4.8 * 1024 * 1024, out: 1.2 * 1024 * 1024, score: 99, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'CachyWire Desktop', path: '/usr/bin/cachywire-desktop', pub: 'CachyOS / CachyWire Project', in: 8.2 * 1024 * 1024, out: 1.5 * 1024 * 1024, score: 100, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'Mozilla Firefox', path: '/usr/lib/firefox/firefox', pub: 'Mozilla Foundation', in: 342.1 * 1024 * 1024, out: 48.7 * 1024 * 1024, score: 98, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'Google Chrome', path: '/opt/google/chrome/chrome', pub: 'Google LLC (Linux x86_64)', in: 480.5 * 1024 * 1024, out: 62.3 * 1024 * 1024, score: 97, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'TeamViewer Remote', path: '/opt/teamviewer/tv_bin/teamviewer', pub: 'TeamViewer Germany GmbH', in: 155.3 * 1024 * 1024, out: 24.1 * 1024 * 1024, score: 96, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'CachyOS Package Manager', path: '/usr/bin/pacman', pub: 'CachyOS / Arch Linux Official', in: 124.5 * 1024 * 1024, out: 1.8 * 1024 * 1024, score: 99, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'cURL Data Transfer', path: '/usr/bin/curl', pub: 'Daniel Stenberg / Open Source', in: 18.2 * 1024 * 1024, out: 820 * 1024, score: 99, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'OpenSSH Client', path: '/usr/bin/ssh', pub: 'OpenBSD / Arch Linux', in: 5.2 * 1024 * 1024, out: 4.9 * 1024 * 1024, score: 99, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'Visual Studio Code', path: '/usr/bin/code', pub: 'Microsoft Corporation', in: 68.4 * 1024 * 1024, out: 12.3 * 1024 * 1024, score: 96, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'Brave Browser', path: '/usr/bin/brave-browser', pub: 'Brave Software Inc.', in: 42.7 * 1024 * 1024, out: 6.2 * 1024 * 1024, score: 97, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'Telegram Desktop', path: '/usr/bin/telegram-desktop', pub: 'Telegram Messenger Inc.', in: 35.8 * 1024 * 1024, out: 8.4 * 1024 * 1024, score: 96, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'Discord Linux Client', path: '/usr/bin/discord', pub: 'Discord Inc.', in: 89.2 * 1024 * 1024, out: 14.5 * 1024 * 1024, score: 95, signed: 1, vtPos: 0 },
      { name: 'suspicious_daemon', path: '/tmp/suspicious_daemon', pub: 'Unverified Binary', in: 450 * 1024, out: 12.4 * 1024 * 1024, score: 24, signed: 0, vtPos: 5 }
    ];

    defaultApps.forEach(item => {
      this.registerApplication({
        exe_path: item.path,
        app_name: item.name,
        publisher: item.pub,
        is_signed: item.signed !== undefined ? item.signed : 1,
        file_version: '1.0.0.0',
        sha256_hash: item.vtPos > 0
          ? 'a1b2c3d4e5f60718293a4b5c6d7e8f90123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0'

        security_score: item.score,
        vt_positives: item.vtPos !== undefined ? item.vtPos : 0,
        vt_total: 72,

      });
    });
  }

  _seedDefaultLanDevices() {
    this.upsertLanDevice({
      mac_address: '70:4F:57:12:34:56',
      last_ip: '192.168.1.1',

      is_online: 0
    });
  }
}

/* ================= FILE: ./src/data/devices-repo.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - LAN Devices Repository
 */

export class DevicesRepository {
  constructor(db) {
    this.db = db;
  }

  upsert(device) {
    return this.db.upsertLanDevice(device);
  }

  getAll() {
    return this.db.getAllLanDevices();
  }

  update(deviceId, fields) {
    return this.db.updateLanDevice(deviceId, fields);
  }

  delete(deviceId) {
    return this.db.deleteLanDevice(deviceId);
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/data/devices-repo.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/data/export-service.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Export & Backup Service
 * Implements CSV Export for Usage statistics (FUNC-02, Scenario 3)
 * and full Database Backup / Restore (Scenario 9).
 */

import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters.js';

export class ExportService {
  constructor(db, appsRepo, hostsRepo, trafficRepo) {
    this.db = db;
    this.appsRepo = appsRepo;
    this.hostsRepo = hostsRepo;
    this.trafficRepo = trafficRepo;
  }

  generateUsageCsv(startTimeSec, endTimeSec, options = {}) {
    const summary = this.trafficRepo.getTotalsForRange(startTimeSec, endTimeSec, options);
    const rows = [];

    // Header
    rows.push(['Category', 'Name / Identifier', 'Detail', 'Inbound Bytes', 'Outbound Bytes', 'Total Bytes', 'Formatted Total']);

    // Applications breakdown
    for (const [appId, data] of summary.appBreakdown.entries()) {
      const app = this.appsRepo.getById(appId);
      const name = app ? app.app_name : `App #${appId}`;
      const path = app ? app.exe_path : '';
      rows.push(['Application', name, path, data.bytes_in, data.bytes_out, data.total, formatBytes(data.total)]);
    }

    // Remote Hosts breakdown
    for (const [hostId, data] of summary.hostBreakdown.entries()) {
      const host = this.hostsRepo.getById(hostId);
      const ip = host ? host.ip_address : `Host #${hostId}`;
      const domain = host ? (host.domain_name || host.country_iso2) : '';
      rows.push(['Remote Host', ip, domain, data.bytes_in, data.bytes_out, data.total, formatBytes(data.total)]);
    }

    // Protocol breakdown
    for (const [protoId, data] of summary.protoBreakdown.entries()) {
      const proto = this.db.tables.traffic_types.get(protoId);
      const name = proto ? proto.service_name : `Proto #${protoId}`;
      rows.push(['Protocol', name, `Port ${proto ? proto.port_number : ''}`, data.bytes_in, data.bytes_out, data.total, formatBytes(data.total)]);
    }

    const csvContent = rows.map(r => r.map(cell => `"${String(cell).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(',')).join('\r\n');
    return csvContent;
  }

  downloadCsv(csvContent, filename = `CachyWire_Usage_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`) {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return csvContent;
    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', filename);
    link.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    return true;
  }

  exportBackup() {
    return this.db.exportDatabaseSnapshot();
  }

  importBackup(backupJson) {
    return this.db.importDatabaseSnapshot(backupJson);
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/data/export-service.js ================= */
