/* ================= FILE: ./src/data/traffic-repo.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Traffic Time-Series Repository
 */

export class TrafficRepository {
  constructor(db) {
    this.db = db;
  }

  recordFlow(flow) {
    this.db.recordTrafficFlow(flow);
  }

  query(params) {
    return this.db.queryTrafficStats(params);
  }

  getTotalsForRange(startTimeSec, endTimeSec, options = {}) {
    const stats = this.db.queryTrafficStats({
      startTimeSec,
      endTimeSec,
      resolutionSec: options.resolutionSec !== undefined ? options.resolutionSec : null,
      appId: options.appId,
      hostId: options.hostId,
      adapterId: options.adapterId,
      isLocalLan: options.isLocalLan
    });

    let totalIn = 0;
    let totalOut = 0;
    const appBreakdown = new Map();
    const hostBreakdown = new Map();
    const protoBreakdown = new Map();

    for (const s of stats) {
      totalIn += s.bytes_in;
      totalOut += s.bytes_out;

      // App aggregation
      const appCurr = appBreakdown.get(s.app_id) || { bytes_in: 0, bytes_out: 0, total: 0 };
      appCurr.bytes_in += s.bytes_in;
      appCurr.bytes_out += s.bytes_out;
      appCurr.total += (s.bytes_in + s.bytes_out);
      appBreakdown.set(s.app_id, appCurr);

      // Host aggregation
      const hostCurr = hostBreakdown.get(s.host_id) || { bytes_in: 0, bytes_out: 0, total: 0 };
      hostCurr.bytes_in += s.bytes_in;
      hostCurr.bytes_out += s.bytes_out;
      hostCurr.total += (s.bytes_in + s.bytes_out);
      hostBreakdown.set(s.host_id, hostCurr);

      // Protocol aggregation
      const protoCurr = protoBreakdown.get(s.proto_id) || { bytes_in: 0, bytes_out: 0, total: 0 };
      protoCurr.bytes_in += s.bytes_in;
      protoCurr.bytes_out += s.bytes_out;
      protoCurr.total += (s.bytes_in + s.bytes_out);
      protoBreakdown.set(s.proto_id, protoCurr);
    }

    return {
      totalIn,
      totalOut,
      totalBytes: totalIn + totalOut,
      appBreakdown,
      hostBreakdown,
      protoBreakdown
    };
  }

  runDownsampling() {
    this.db.runDownsamplingRollup();
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/data/traffic-repo.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src-linux-desktop/cachywire-desktop.c ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire Network Security (v0.1.0-alpha) — Native CachyOS / Arch Linux Desktop Shell
 * Built with GTK3 + WebKit2GTK 4.1 + Ayatana AppIndicator3 (System Tray Next to Clock)
 *
 * Features:
 *  - Native Main Desktop Window (1280x820, min 960x640)
 *  - Persistent System Tray Icon (Ayatana AppIndicator3 / StatusNotifierItem) next to clock
 *  - Automatic background telemetry service management (cachywire-daemon on 127.0.0.1:8080)
 *  - Bi-directional IPC bridge via window.webkit.messageHandlers.cachywire.postMessage()
 */

#define _GNU_SOURCE
#include <gtk/gtk.h>
#include <gio/gio.h>
#include <webkit2/webkit2.h>
#include <libdbusmenu-glib/server.h>
#include <libdbusmenu-gtk/parser.h>
#include <JavaScriptCore/JavaScript.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <signal.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <sys/resource.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <arpa/inet.h>
#include <libgen.h>
#include <limits.h>

static GtkWidget *main_window = NULL;
static GtkWidget *main_webview = NULL;
static DbusmenuServer *tray_menu_server = NULL;
static GtkWidget *tray_gtk_menu = NULL;
static char tray_icon_dir[PATH_MAX + 32] = "";
static pid_t server_pid = -1;
static gboolean is_quitting = FALSE;
static gboolean is_maximized = FALSE;
static char app_root_dir[PATH_MAX] = "/opt/cachywire";

#define GW_PORT 8080
#define GW_MAIN_URL "http://127.0.0.1:8080/index.html"

/* Check if TCP port 8080 on 127.0.0.1 is already accepting connections */
static gboolean is_server_running(void) {
    int sock = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, 0);
    if (sock < 0) return FALSE;

    struct sockaddr_in addr;
    memset(&addr, 0, sizeof(addr));
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(GW_PORT);
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);

    int res = connect(sock, (struct sockaddr *)&addr, sizeof(addr));
    close(sock);
    return (res == 0);
}

/* Resolve the application root directory containing index.html */
static void resolve_app_root(void) {
    const char *env_dir = getenv("CACHYWIRE_APP_DIR");
    if (env_dir && env_dir[0] != '\0') {
        char candidate[PATH_MAX + 32];
        snprintf(candidate, sizeof(candidate), "%s/index.html", env_dir);
        if (access(candidate, R_OK) == 0) {
            snprintf(app_root_dir, sizeof(app_root_dir), "%s", env_dir);
            return;
        }
    }

    char exe_path[PATH_MAX];
    ssize_t len = readlink("/proc/self/exe", exe_path, sizeof(exe_path) - 1);
    if (len > 0) {
        exe_path[len] = '\0';
        char *dir = dirname(exe_path);
        char candidate[PATH_MAX + 32];
        snprintf(candidate, sizeof(candidate), "%s/index.html", dir);
        if (access(candidate, R_OK) == 0) {
            snprintf(app_root_dir, sizeof(app_root_dir), "%s", dir);
            return;
        }
    }

    char cwd[PATH_MAX];
    if (getcwd(cwd, sizeof(cwd)) != NULL) {
        char candidate[PATH_MAX + 32];
        snprintf(candidate, sizeof(candidate), "%s/index.html", cwd);
        if (access(candidate, R_OK) == 0) {
            snprintf(app_root_dir, sizeof(app_root_dir), "%s", cwd);
            return;
        }
    }
}

/* Spawn ultra-lightweight native Rust backend (cachywire-daemon) */
static void ensure_backend_server(void) {
    if (is_server_running()) {
        return;
    }

    pid_t pid = fork();
    if (pid == 0) {
        if (chdir(app_root_dir) != 0) {
            _exit(1);
        }
        setenv("CACHYWIRE_APP_DIR", app_root_dir, 1);
        FILE *f1 = freopen("/tmp/cachywire-backend.log", "a", stdout);
        FILE *f2 = freopen("/tmp/cachywire-backend.log", "a", stderr);
        (void)f1; (void)f2;

        char rust_bin_local[PATH_MAX + 64];
        snprintf(rust_bin_local, sizeof(rust_bin_local), "%s/cachywire-daemon", app_root_dir);
        if (access(rust_bin_local, X_OK) == 0) {
            execl(rust_bin_local, "cachywire-daemon", (char *)NULL);
        }
        const char *xdg = getenv("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR");
        if (xdg && xdg[0] != '\0') {
            char xdg_daemon[PATH_MAX + 64];
            snprintf(xdg_daemon, sizeof(xdg_daemon), "%s/cachywire/cachywire-daemon", xdg);
            if (access(xdg_daemon, X_OK) == 0) {
                execl(xdg_daemon, "cachywire-daemon", (char *)NULL);
            }
        }
        if (access("/opt/cachywire/cachywire-daemon", X_OK) == 0) {
            execl("/opt/cachywire/cachywire-daemon", "cachywire-daemon", (char *)NULL);
        }
        if (access("/tmp/cachywire-bin/cachywire-daemon", X_OK) == 0) {
            execl("/tmp/cachywire-bin/cachywire-daemon", "cachywire-daemon", (char *)NULL);
        }

        fprintf(stderr, "[ERROR] cachywire-daemon not found in %s or /opt/cachywire\n", app_root_dir);
        _exit(1);
    } else if (pid > 0) {
        server_pid = pid;
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            if (is_server_running()) break;
            usleep(100000);
        }
    }
}

/* Cleanly stop spawned backend server on application quit */
static void stop_backend_server(void) {
    if (server_pid > 0) {
        kill(server_pid, SIGTERM);
        // Wait up to 1 second for graceful exit, then force kill if still running
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            usleep(100000);
            int status;
            if (waitpid(server_pid, &status, WNOHANG) > 0) {
                server_pid = -1;
                return;
            }
        }
        kill(server_pid, SIGKILL);
        waitpid(server_pid, NULL, 0);
        server_pid = -1;
    }
}

/* Execute JavaScript in the main window WebView */
static void run_main_js(const char *script) {
    if (main_webview && WEBKIT_IS_WEB_VIEW(main_webview)) {
        webkit_web_view_evaluate_javascript(
            WEBKIT_WEB_VIEW(main_webview),
            script, -1, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL
        );
    }
}

static void on_script_message_received(WebKitUserContentManager *manager,
                                       WebKitJavascriptResult *js_result,
                                       gpointer user_data);

/* Wayland xdg-activation token handed to us by KDE Plasma right before Activate() */
static gchar *pending_activation_token = NULL;

/* Restore and focus the Main Dashboard Window */
static void restore_main_window(void) {
    if (main_window) {
        gtk_widget_show_all(main_window);
        gtk_window_deiconify(GTK_WINDOW(main_window));
        if (pending_activation_token) {
            /* GTK3 on Wayland uses the startup id as the xdg-activation token,
             * which lets KWin raise and focus an already-open window. */
            gtk_window_set_startup_id(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), pending_activation_token);
            g_free(pending_activation_token);
            pending_activation_token = NULL;
        }
        gtk_window_present_with_time(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), GDK_CURRENT_TIME);
    }
}

/* Handle IPC messages from window.webkit.messageHandlers.cachywire.postMessage(cmd) */
static void on_script_message_received(WebKitUserContentManager *manager,
                                       WebKitJavascriptResult *js_result,
                                       gpointer user_data) {
    (void)manager;
    (void)user_data;

    JSCValue *value = webkit_javascript_result_get_js_value(js_result);
    if (!jsc_value_is_string(value)) return;

    char *cmd = jsc_value_to_string(value);
    if (!cmd) return;

    if (strstr(cmd, "restore_main") != NULL) {
        restore_main_window();
    } else if (strstr(cmd, "minimize_window") != NULL) {
        if (main_window) gtk_window_iconify(GTK_WINDOW(main_window));
    } else if (strstr(cmd, "maximize_window") != NULL) {
        if (main_window) {
            if (is_maximized) {
                gtk_window_unmaximize(GTK_WINDOW(main_window));
                is_maximized = FALSE;
            } else {
                gtk_window_maximize(GTK_WINDOW(main_window));
                is_maximized = TRUE;
            }
        }
    } else if (strstr(cmd, "minimize_to_tray") != NULL) {
        if (main_window) {
            gtk_widget_hide(main_window);
        }
    }

    g_free(cmd);
}

/* Intercept window close button (X) to keep CachyWire running in System Tray next to clock */
static gboolean on_main_window_delete_event(GtkWidget *widget, GdkEvent *event, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)event;
    (void)user_data;

    if (is_quitting) {
        return FALSE;
    }

    gtk_widget_hide(widget);
    return TRUE;
}

/* Tray Menu Callbacks */
static void on_tray_open_dashboard(GtkMenuItem *item, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)item; (void)user_data;
    restore_main_window();
}

static void on_tray_clear_all_data(GtkMenuItem *item, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)item; (void)user_data;
    restore_main_window();
    run_main_js("if(window.gwApp){window.gwApp.clearAllRecordedData();}");
}

static void on_tray_fw_click_to_block(GtkMenuItem *item, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)item; (void)user_data;
    restore_main_window();
    run_main_js("if(window.gwApp){window.gwApp.shell.switchView('firewall');window.gwApp.firewallView._setMode('CLICK_TO_BLOCK');}");
}

    restore_main_window();
    run_main_js("if(window.gwApp){window.gwApp.shell.switchView('firewall');window.gwApp.firewallView._setMode('ASK_TO_CONNECT');}");
}

static void on_tray_fw_block_all(GtkMenuItem *item, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)item; (void)user_data;
    restore_main_window();
    run_main_js("if(window.gwApp){window.gwApp.shell.switchView('firewall');window.gwApp.firewallView._setMode('BLOCK_ALL');}");
}

static void on_tray_toggle_incognito(GtkMenuItem *item, gpointer user_data) {

    run_main_js("if(window.gwApp){window.gwApp.toggleIncognito();}");
}

    (void)item; (void)user_data;
    run_main_js("if(window.gwApp){window.gwApp.toggleSnooze();}");
}

static void on_tray_quit(GtkMenuItem *item, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)item; (void)user_data;
    is_quitting = TRUE;
    stop_backend_server();
    gtk_main_quit();
}

/* KDE / Freedesktop StatusNotifierItem XML Introspection (ItemIsMenu = FALSE so Left-Click triggers Activate!) */
static const gchar sni_introspection_xml[] =
    "<node>"
    "  <interface name='org.kde.StatusNotifierItem'>"
    "    <method name='ContextMenu'>"
    "      <arg name='x' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "      <arg name='y' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "    </method>"
    "    <method name='Activate'>"
    "      <arg name='x' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "      <arg name='y' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "    </method>"
    "    <method name='SecondaryActivate'>"
    "      <arg name='x' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "      <arg name='y' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "    </method>"
    "    <method name='Scroll'>"
    "      <arg name='delta' type='i' direction='in'/>"
    "      <arg name='orientation' type='s' direction='in'/>"
    "    </method>"
    "    <method name='ProvideXdgActivationToken'>"
    "      <arg name='token' type='s' direction='in'/>"
    "    </method>"
    "    <property name='Category' type='s' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='Id' type='s' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='Title' type='s' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='Status' type='s' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='WindowId' type='i' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='IconName' type='s' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='IconThemePath' type='s' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='ItemIsMenu' type='b' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='Menu' type='o' access='read'/>"
    "    <property name='ToolTip' type='(sa(iiay)ss)' access='read'/>"
    "  </interface>"
    "</node>";

static void on_sni_method_call(GDBusConnection *conn,
                               const gchar *sender,
                               const gchar *object_path,
                               const gchar *interface_name,
                               const gchar *method_name,
                               GVariant *parameters,
                               GDBusMethodInvocation *invocation,
                               gpointer user_data) {
    (void)conn; (void)sender; (void)object_path; (void)interface_name; (void)parameters; (void)user_data;

    if (g_strcmp0(method_name, "Activate") == 0 || g_strcmp0(method_name, "SecondaryActivate") == 0) {
        restore_main_window();
        g_dbus_method_invocation_return_value(invocation, NULL);
    } else if (g_strcmp0(method_name, "ContextMenu") == 0) {
        if (tray_gtk_menu) {
            gtk_menu_popup_at_pointer(GTK_MENU(tray_gtk_menu), NULL);
        }
        g_dbus_method_invocation_return_value(invocation, NULL);
    } else if (g_strcmp0(method_name, "ProvideXdgActivationToken") == 0) {
        const gchar *token = NULL;
        g_variant_get(parameters, "(&s)", &token);
        g_free(pending_activation_token);
        pending_activation_token = g_strdup(token);
        g_dbus_method_invocation_return_value(invocation, NULL);
    } else if (g_strcmp0(method_name, "Scroll") == 0) {
        g_dbus_method_invocation_return_value(invocation, NULL);
    }
}

static GVariant *on_sni_get_property(GDBusConnection *conn,
                                     const gchar *sender,
                                     const gchar *object_path,
                                     const gchar *interface_name,
                                     const gchar *property_name,
                                     GError **error,
                                     gpointer user_data) {
    (void)conn; (void)sender; (void)object_path; (void)interface_name; (void)error; (void)user_data;

    if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "Category") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_string("ApplicationStatus");
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "Id") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_string("cachywire");
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "Title") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_string("CachyWire Network Monitor");
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "Status") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_string("Active");
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "WindowId") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_int32(0);
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "IconName") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_string("cachywire");
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "IconThemePath") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_string(tray_icon_dir);
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "ItemIsMenu") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_boolean(FALSE);
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "Menu") == 0) {
        return g_variant_new_object_path("/MenuBar");
    } else if (g_strcmp0(property_name, "ToolTip") == 0) {
        GVariantBuilder pixmaps;
        g_variant_builder_init(&pixmaps, G_VARIANT_TYPE("a(iiay)"));
        return g_variant_new("(sa(iiay)ss)", "cachywire", &pixmaps,
                             "CachyWire Network Monitor & Firewall",
                             "Left-click to open CachyWire • Right-click for menu");
    }
    return NULL;
}

static const GDBusInterfaceVTable sni_vtable = {
    on_sni_method_call,
    on_sni_get_property,
    NULL,
    { 0 }
};

static void register_with_sni_watcher(GDBusConnection *conn, const gchar *bus_name) {
    g_dbus_connection_call(
        conn,
        "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "/StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
        "RegisterStatusNotifierItem",
        g_variant_new("(s)", bus_name),
        NULL,
        G_DBUS_CALL_FLAGS_NONE,
        -1,
        NULL,
        NULL,
        NULL
    );
}

static void on_sni_name_acquired(GDBusConnection *conn, const gchar *name, gpointer user_data) {
    (void)user_data;
    register_with_sni_watcher(conn, name);
}

/* Initialize System Tray Icon next to the clock using Native KDE StatusNotifierItem + Dbusmenu */
static void setup_system_tray(const char *icon_dir, const char *icon_file) {
    (void)icon_file;
    if (icon_dir) {
        snprintf(tray_icon_dir, sizeof(tray_icon_dir), "%s", icon_dir);
    }

    tray_gtk_menu = gtk_menu_new();

    GtkWidget *item_status = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("● CachyWire Active — Rust Native Engine");
    gtk_widget_set_sensitive(item_status, FALSE);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_status);

    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), gtk_separator_menu_item_new());

    GtkWidget *item_open = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Open CachyWire Dashboard");
    g_signal_connect(item_open, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_open_dashboard), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_open);

    GtkWidget *item_clear = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Clear All Recorded Data (Reset to 0 B)");
    g_signal_connect(item_clear, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_clear_all_data), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_clear);

    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), gtk_separator_menu_item_new());

    GtkWidget *item_fw_ctb = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Firewall: Click to Block");
    g_signal_connect(item_fw_ctb, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_fw_click_to_block), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_fw_ctb);

    GtkWidget *item_fw_ask = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Firewall: Ask to Connect");
    g_signal_connect(item_fw_ask, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_fw_ask_to_connect), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_fw_ask);

    GtkWidget *item_fw_lock = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Firewall: Block All (Lockdown)");
    g_signal_connect(item_fw_lock, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_fw_block_all), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_fw_lock);

    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), gtk_separator_menu_item_new());

    GtkWidget *item_incog = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Toggle Incognito Mode");
    g_signal_connect(item_incog, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_toggle_incognito), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_incog);

    GtkWidget *item_snooze = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Snooze Alerts (24 Hours)");
    g_signal_connect(item_snooze, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_toggle_snooze), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_snooze);

    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), gtk_separator_menu_item_new());

    GtkWidget *item_quit = gtk_menu_item_new_with_label("Quit CachyWire Completely");
    g_signal_connect(item_quit, "activate", G_CALLBACK(on_tray_quit), NULL);
    gtk_menu_shell_append(GTK_MENU_SHELL(tray_gtk_menu), item_quit);

    gtk_widget_show_all(tray_gtk_menu);

    /* Export right-click menu over D-Bus at /MenuBar */
    tray_menu_server = dbusmenu_server_new("/MenuBar");
    DbusmenuMenuitem *root_item = dbusmenu_gtk_parse_menu_structure(tray_gtk_menu);
    dbusmenu_server_set_root(tray_menu_server, root_item);

    GDBusConnection *bus = g_bus_get_sync(G_BUS_TYPE_SESSION, NULL, NULL);
    if (bus) {
        GDBusNodeInfo *node_info = g_dbus_node_info_new_for_xml(sni_introspection_xml, NULL);
        if (node_info) {
            g_dbus_connection_register_object(
                bus,
                "/StatusNotifierItem",
                node_info->interfaces[0],
                &sni_vtable,
                NULL,
                NULL,
                NULL
            );
            g_dbus_node_info_unref(node_info);
        }

        char bus_name[128];
        snprintf(bus_name, sizeof(bus_name), "org.kde.StatusNotifierItem-%d-1", (int)getpid());
        g_bus_own_name_on_connection(
            bus,
            bus_name,
            G_BUS_NAME_OWNER_FLAGS_NONE,
            on_sni_name_acquired,
            NULL,
            NULL,
            NULL
        );
    }
}

int main(int argc, char *argv[]) {
    /* NVIDIA proprietary driver + WebKit2GTK DMA-BUF renderer leaks GPU sync_file
     * descriptors until the 1024 fd limit is hit, which freezes the WebProcess.
     * Disable the DMA-BUF renderer and raise the soft fd limit as a safety net. */
    setenv("WEBKIT_DISABLE_DMABUF_RENDERER", "1", 0);
    struct rlimit rl;
    if (getrlimit(RLIMIT_NOFILE, &rl) == 0 && rl.rlim_cur < rl.rlim_max) {
        rl.rlim_cur = rl.rlim_max;
        setrlimit(RLIMIT_NOFILE, &rl);
    }

    resolve_app_root();
    ensure_backend_server();

    gtk_init(&argc, &argv);
    g_set_prgname("cachywire");
    g_set_application_name("CachyWire");

    /* Clear WebKit2GTK disk cache on startup so updated UI/JS/CSS modules always load fresh */
    WebKitWebContext *wk_context = webkit_web_context_get_default();
    if (wk_context) {
        webkit_web_context_clear_cache(wk_context);
        webkit_web_context_set_cache_model(wk_context, WEBKIT_CACHE_MODEL_DOCUMENT_VIEWER);
    }

    GtkSettings *gtk_settings = gtk_settings_get_default();
    if (gtk_settings) {
        g_object_set(gtk_settings, "gtk-application-prefer-dark-theme", TRUE, NULL);
    }

    char icon_dir[PATH_MAX + 32];
    char icon_path[PATH_MAX + 64];
    snprintf(icon_dir, sizeof(icon_dir), "%s/dist/icons", app_root_dir);
    snprintf(icon_path, sizeof(icon_path), "%s/dist/icons/icon_512.png", app_root_dir);

    main_window = gtk_window_new(GTK_WINDOW_TOPLEVEL);
    gtk_window_set_title(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), "CachyWire Network Monitor & Firewall (v0.1.0-alpha) — Open Source CachyOS Edition");
    gtk_window_set_default_size(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), 1280, 820);
    gtk_window_set_position(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), GTK_WIN_POS_CENTER);

    GdkGeometry geom;
    geom.min_width = 960;
    geom.min_height = 640;
    gtk_window_set_geometry_hints(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), NULL, &geom, GDK_HINT_MIN_SIZE);

    if (access(icon_path, R_OK) == 0) {
        gtk_window_set_icon_from_file(GTK_WINDOW(main_window), icon_path, NULL);
    }

    g_signal_connect(main_window, "delete-event", G_CALLBACK(on_main_window_delete_event), NULL);

    WebKitUserContentManager *ucm = webkit_user_content_manager_new();
    g_signal_connect(ucm, "script-message-received::cachywire", G_CALLBACK(on_script_message_received), NULL);
    webkit_user_content_manager_register_script_message_handler(ucm, "cachywire");
    // Note: signal and handler registered once only — duplicate removed (Audit: MEDIUM-4)

    main_webview = webkit_web_view_new_with_user_content_manager(ucm);
    WebKitSettings *wk_settings = webkit_web_view_get_settings(WEBKIT_WEB_VIEW(main_webview));
    webkit_settings_set_enable_developer_extras(wk_settings, TRUE);
    webkit_settings_set_enable_smooth_scrolling(wk_settings, TRUE);
    webkit_settings_set_enable_write_console_messages_to_stdout(wk_settings, TRUE);


    GdkRGBA bg = {0.035, 0.055, 0.102, 1.0};
    webkit_web_view_set_background_color(WEBKIT_WEB_VIEW(main_webview), &bg);

    gtk_container_add(GTK_CONTAINER(main_window), main_webview);
    webkit_web_view_load_uri(WEBKIT_WEB_VIEW(main_webview), GW_MAIN_URL);

    setup_system_tray(icon_dir, icon_path);

    gboolean start_minimized = FALSE;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "--minimized") == 0 || strcmp(argv[i], "--tray") == 0) {
            start_minimized = TRUE;
        }
    }

    if (!start_minimized) {
        gtk_widget_show_all(main_window);
    }

    gtk_main();

    stop_backend_server();
    return 0;
}

/* ================= END: ./src-linux-desktop/cachywire-desktop.c ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/alerts-timeline.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Alerts Center View (SCR-06)
 * AUDIT FIXES (SEC-03): alert titles & details are now HTML-escaped,
 * because alert content can contain process-controlled strings.
 */
import { formatDateTime } from '../utils/formatters.js';
import { esc } from '../utils/escape.js';

export class AlertsTimelineView {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.container = options.container || document.getElementById('view-alerts');
    this.alertsRepo = options.alertsRepo;
    this.appsRepo = options.appsRepo;
    this.onAlertClick = options.onAlertClick || (() => {});
    this.onUnreadChange = options.onUnreadChange || (() => {});
    this.activeCategory = 'ALL'; // 'ALL' | 'SECURITY' | 'SYSTEM' | 'NETWORK' | 'DATA_PLAN'
  }

  mount() {
    this.container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="alerts-header-bar">
    <div class="alerts-filter-chips">
    <button class="filter-chip active" data-category="ALL">All</button>
    <button class="filter-chip" data-category="SECURITY">Security</button>
    <button class="filter-chip" data-category="SYSTEM">System</button>
    <button class="filter-chip" data-category="NETWORK">Network</button>
    <button class="filter-chip" data-category="DATA_PLAN">Data Plan</button>
    </div>
    <div class="alerts-actions">
    <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-mark-all-read">Mark all as read</button>
    <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-clear-alerts">Clear</button>
    </div>
    </div>
    <div class="alerts-timeline-container" id="alerts-timeline-feed"></div>
    `;
    this._bindEvents();
    this.refresh();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    this.container.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.container.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
        chip.classList.add('active');
        this.activeCategory = chip.getAttribute('data-category');
        this.refresh();
      });
    });
    const btnMarkAll = this.container.querySelector('#btn-mark-all-read');
    btnMarkAll.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.alertsRepo.markAllAsRead();
      this.refresh();
      this.onUnreadChange();
    });
    const btnClear = this.container.querySelector('#btn-clear-alerts');
    btnClear.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.alertsRepo.clear();
      this.refresh();
      this.onUnreadChange();
    });
  }

  refresh() {
    const feed = this.container.querySelector('#alerts-timeline-feed');
    if (!feed) return;
    const alerts = this.alertsRepo.getAll(this.activeCategory);
    feed.innerHTML = alerts.length === 0
    ? '<div style="padding:48px;text-align:center;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);font-size:14px;">No alerts recorded yet</div>'
    : '';
    const iconMap = {
      FIRST_NETWORK_ACTIVITY: '🌐',
      APP_CHANGED: '⚠️',
      ARP_SPOOF: '🚨',
      WIFI_EVIL_TWIN: '📡',
      HOSTS_CHANGED: '📝',
      DNS_CHANGED: '🔄',
      RDP_CONNECTION: '🖥️',
      PROXY_SETTINGS_CHANGED: '🛡️',
      INTERNET_LOST: '❌',
      INTERNET_RESTORED: '✅',
      NETWORK_ANOMALY: '📈',
      DATA_PLAN_WARNING: '📊',
      DATA_PLAN_EXCEEDED: '🛑',
      NEW_DEVICE_ON_NETWORK: '🔌'
    };
    for (const alert of alerts) {
      const isUnread = alert.is_read === 0;
      const icon = iconMap[alert.alert_type] || '🔔';
      let details = {};
      try {
        details = JSON.parse(alert.details_json);
      } catch (e) {
        details = { raw: alert.details_json };
      }
      const card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = `alert-card severity-${alert.severity} ${isUnread ? 'unread' : ''}`;
      card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="alert-card-left">
      <div class="alert-type-icon">${icon}</div>
      <div class="alert-content">
      <span class="alert-title">${esc(alert.title)}</span>
      <span class="alert-description">${esc(this._formatDetailsDescription(details))}</span>
      </div>
      </div>
      <div class="alert-card-right">
      <span class="alert-timestamp">${formatDateTime(alert.created_ts)}</span>
      ${alert.app_id ? `<button class="pill-btn" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Mute future alerts from this app">Mute App</button>` : ''}
      </div>
      `;
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (isUnread) {
          this.alertsRepo.markAsRead(alert.alert_id);
          card.classList.remove('unread');
          this.onUnreadChange();
        }
      });
      const muteBtn = card.querySelector('button');
      if (muteBtn && alert.app_id) {
        muteBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          this.appsRepo.toggleAlertsMuted(alert.app_id);
          muteBtn.textContent = 'Muted';
        });
      }
      feed.appendChild(card);
    }
  }

  _formatDetailsDescription(details) {
    if (!details) return '';
    const items = [];
    if (details.path) items.push(`Path: ${details.path}`);
    if (details.destination) items.push(`Destination: ${details.destination}`);
    if (details.gatewayIp) items.push(`Gateway: ${details.gatewayIp} (Spoofed MAC: ${details.spoofedMac})`);
    if (details.ssid) items.push(`SSID: ${details.ssid} (Fake BSSID: ${details.fakeBssid})`);
    if (details.formattedUsed) items.push(`Used: ${details.formattedUsed} / Limit: ${details.formattedLimit}`);
    if (details.rate) items.push(`Current Rate: ${(details.rate / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB/s (Z-Score: ${details.zScore})`);
    if (details.description) items.push(details.description);
    return items.length > 0 ? items.join(' | ') : JSON.stringify(details);
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/alerts-timeline.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/firewall-grid.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Firewall Grid View (SCR-04)
 * Comprehensive Security Table matching Section 3.2 (Screen 4) & Figure 2-4
 * Provides 1-click instant blocking (< 200ms), mode switching, Ask-to-Connect prompt test, search, and sorting.
 */

import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters.js';
import { FIREWALL_MODES, FIREWALL_PROFILES } from '../config/default-settings.js';
import { getAppIconSvg } from '../utils/app-icons.js';

export class FirewallGridView {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.container = options.container || document.getElementById('view-firewall');
    this.db = options.db;
    this.appsRepo = options.appsRepo;
    this.trafficRepo = options.trafficRepo;
    this.firewallPolicy = options.firewallPolicy;
    this.securityScoreEngine = options.securityScoreEngine;
    this.virusTotalClient = options.virusTotalClient;
    this.onAppClick = options.onAppClick || (() => {});
    this.onModeChange = options.onModeChange || (() => {});
    this.onTestAskToConnect = options.onTestAskToConnect || (() => {});

    this.searchQuery = '';
    this.sortBy = 'traffic'; // 'name' | 'state' | 'score' | 'traffic'
    this.sortOrder = 'desc'; // 'asc' | 'desc'
  }

  mount() {
    this.container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="firewall-control-bar">
        <div class="firewall-controls-left">
          <div class="master-toggle-group">
            <span class="master-toggle-label">Firewall:</span>
            <div class="toggle-capsule active" id="fw-master-toggle" title="Toggle Firewall Enforcement ON/OFF">
              <div class="toggle-handle"></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="firewall-select-group">
            <span class="select-label">Mode:</span>
            <select class="gw-select" id="select-fw-mode">
              <option value="${FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK}">Click to block</option>
              <option value="${FIREWALL_MODES.ASK_TO_CONNECT}">Ask to connect</option>
              <option value="${FIREWALL_MODES.BLOCK_ALL}">Block all (Lockdown)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="firewall-select-group">
            <span class="select-label">Profile:</span>
            <select class="gw-select" id="select-fw-profile">
              ${FIREWALL_PROFILES.map(p => `<option value="${p.id}">${p.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="firewall-controls-right">
          <div class="search-input-wrapper">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"/></svg>
            <input type="text" class="gw-search-box" id="fw-search-input" placeholder="Search applications...">
          </div>

          <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-test-ask-connect" title="Simulate New Outbound Connection Prompt (SCR-09)">
            🛡️ Test Ask Prompt
          </button>

          <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-scan-all-vt" title="Run VirusTotal Inspection on all apps">
            Scan VT
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="firewall-table-wrapper">
        <div class="firewall-table">
          <div class="table-header">
            <div class="th-cell th-block" data-sort="state">Block</div>
            <div class="th-cell th-app" data-sort="name">Application & Publisher</div>
            <div class="th-cell th-score" data-sort="score">GW Score & Status</div>
            <div class="th-cell th-vt">VirusTotal</div>
            <div class="th-cell th-traffic" data-sort="traffic">Traffic ▾</div>
          </div>

          <div class="table-body" id="fw-table-body"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    this._bindEvents();
    this.refresh();
  }

  _setMode(modeKey) {
    const map = {
      normal: FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK,
      click_to_block: FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK,
      CLICK_TO_BLOCK: FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK,
      ask_to_connect: FIREWALL_MODES.ASK_TO_CONNECT,
      ASK_TO_CONNECT: FIREWALL_MODES.ASK_TO_CONNECT,
      block_all: FIREWALL_MODES.BLOCK_ALL,
      BLOCK_ALL: FIREWALL_MODES.BLOCK_ALL
    };
    const target = map[modeKey] || FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK;
    this.firewallPolicy.setMode(target);
    const selMode = this.container.querySelector('#select-fw-mode');
    if (selMode) selMode.value = target;
    this.refresh();
    this.onModeChange();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    const masterToggle = this.container.querySelector('#fw-master-toggle');
    masterToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const isEnabled = this.firewallPolicy.isEnabled();
      this.firewallPolicy.setEnabled(!isEnabled);
      masterToggle.classList.toggle('active', !isEnabled);
      this.refresh();
      this.onModeChange();
    });

    const selMode = this.container.querySelector('#select-fw-mode');
    selMode.value = this.firewallPolicy.getMode();
    selMode.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      this.firewallPolicy.setMode(e.target.value);
      this.refresh();
      this.onModeChange();
    });

    const selProfile = this.container.querySelector('#select-fw-profile');
    selProfile.value = this.firewallPolicy.getProfile();
    selProfile.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      this.firewallPolicy.setProfile(e.target.value);
      this.refresh();
    });

    const searchInput = this.container.querySelector('#fw-search-input');
    let debounceTimer;
    searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
      debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.searchQuery = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
        this.refresh();
      }, 150);
    });

    this.container.querySelectorAll('.th-cell[data-sort]').forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const field = th.getAttribute('data-sort');
        if (this.sortBy === field) {
          this.sortOrder = this.sortOrder === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
          this.sortBy = field;
          this.sortOrder = 'desc';
        }
        this.refresh();
      });
    });

    const btnTestAsk = this.container.querySelector('#btn-test-ask-connect');
    if (btnTestAsk) {
      btnTestAsk.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.onTestAskToConnect();
      });
    }

    const btnScanAll = this.container.querySelector('#btn-scan-all-vt');
    btnScanAll.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      btnScanAll.textContent = 'Scanning...';
      const apps = this.appsRepo.getAll();
      for (const app of apps) {
        await this.virusTotalClient.scanApplication(app.app_id);
      }
      btnScanAll.textContent = 'Scan VT';
      this.refresh();
    });
  }

  refresh() {
    const tableBody = this.container.querySelector('#fw-table-body');
    if (!tableBody) return;

    const masterToggle = this.container.querySelector('#fw-master-toggle');
    if (masterToggle) {
      masterToggle.classList.toggle('active', this.firewallPolicy.isEnabled());
    }

    const selMode = this.container.querySelector('#select-fw-mode');
    if (selMode) {
      selMode.value = this.firewallPolicy.getMode();
    }

    let apps = this.appsRepo.getAll();

    if (this.searchQuery) {
      apps = apps.filter(a =>
        a.app_name.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery) ||
        a.exe_path.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery) ||
        (a.publisher && a.publisher.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery))
      );
    }

    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const summary = this.trafficRepo.getTotalsForRange(nowSec - (30 * 86400), nowSec);

    const appDataList = apps.map(app => {
      const traffic = summary.appBreakdown.get(app.app_id)?.total || 0;
      return { app, traffic };
    });

    appDataList.sort((a, b) => {
      // Hoist blocked applications to the very top for instant access
      const aBlocked = a.app.firewall_state === 1 ? 1 : 0;
      const bBlocked = b.app.firewall_state === 1 ? 1 : 0;
      if (aBlocked !== bBlocked) {
        return bBlocked - aBlocked;
      }
      let comparison = 0;
      if (this.sortBy === 'traffic') comparison = a.traffic - b.traffic;
      else if (this.sortBy === 'name') comparison = a.app.app_name.localeCompare(b.app.app_name);
      else if (this.sortBy === 'state') comparison = a.app.firewall_state - b.app.firewall_state;
      else if (this.sortBy === 'score') comparison = a.app.security_score - b.app.security_score;
      return this.sortOrder === 'asc' ? comparison : -comparison;
    });

    tableBody.innerHTML = appDataList.length === 0 ? '<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);">No applications match the search criteria</div>' : '';

    const isFwEnabled = this.firewallPolicy.isEnabled();

    for (const { app, traffic } of appDataList) {
      const isBlocked = app.firewall_state === 1;
      const rating = this.securityScoreEngine.getScoreRating(app.security_score);

      let vtClass = 'unknown';
      let vtText = 'Scan';
      if (app.vt_total > 0) {
        vtText = `${app.vt_positives} / ${app.vt_total}`;
        vtClass = app.vt_positives === 0 ? 'clean' : 'infected';
      }

      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = `table-row ${isBlocked ? 'blocked-row' : ''}`;
      row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="td-block">
          <button class="block-toggle-btn ${isBlocked ? 'blocked' : ''}" title="${isBlocked ? 'Unblock Application' : 'Block Application'}" ${!isFwEnabled ? 'disabled' : ''}>
            <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="${isBlocked ? '#FF4757' : 'none'}" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
              <path d="M12 22s8-4 8-10V5l-8-3-8 3v7c0 6 8 10 8 10z"/>
            </svg>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="td-app" title="Click to view full application details">
          <div class="app-vector-icon" style="width:22px;height:22px;flex-shrink:0;">${getAppIconSvg(app.app_name, app.exe_path)}</div>
          <div class="app-details-col">
            <span class="app-primary-name">${app.app_name}</span>
            <span class="app-publisher-name">${app.publisher || 'Unverified Publisher'} ${app.is_signed ? '✓' : ''}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="td-score">
          <span class="score-badge ${rating.cssClass}">
            ${app.security_score}/100 ${rating.label}
          </span>
          ${app.security_score < 40 ? '<span class="anomaly-alert-tag">! ANOMALY DETECTED</span>' : ''}
        </div>

        <div class="td-vt">
          <span class="vt-pill ${vtClass}" title="Click to inspect on VirusTotal">
            ${vtText}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div class="td-traffic mono-num">
          ${formatBytes(traffic)}
        </div>
      `;

      const blockBtn = row.querySelector('.block-toggle-btn');
      blockBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (!isFwEnabled) return;
        this.firewallPolicy.toggleBlock(app.app_id);
        this.refresh();
      });

      const appCol = row.querySelector('.td-app');
      appCol.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.onAppClick(app);
      });

      const vtPill = row.querySelector('.vt-pill');
      vtPill.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        vtPill.textContent = '...';
        await this.virusTotalClient.scanApplication(app.app_id);
        this.refresh();
      });

      tableBody.appendChild(row);
    }
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/firewall-grid.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/lan-scanner.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - LAN Scanner "Things" View (SCR-05)
 * Network device discovery table, OUI manufacturer resolution,
 * real CachyOS /proc/net/arp neighbor discovery, and inline editable device naming.
 *
 * AUDIT FIXES (Batch #2):
 *  - FUNC-06: /api/network/arp returns a bare JSON array — both shapes handled.
 *  - DATA-01: removed fabricated "Samsung-Smart-TV" device injection.
 *  - FUNC-05: live ARP entries now feed the ARP-spoofing watchdog.
 *  - SEC-03: all dynamic strings rendered through esc().
 */
import { formatMacAddress, formatDate } from '../utils/formatters.js';
import { lookupMacVendor } from '../utils/oui-vendors.js';
import { esc } from '../utils/escape.js';

export class LanScannerView {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.container = options.container || document.getElementById('view-network');
    this.devicesRepo = options.devicesRepo;
    this.alertsRepo = options.alertsRepo;
    this.isScanning = false;
    this.alertOnNewDevice = true;
    this.currentSsid = 'CachyOS-LAN (192.168.1.0/24)';
  }

  mount() {
    this.container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="network-header-bar">
    <div class="network-title-group">
    <div class="network-badge-icon">
    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M5 12.55a11 11 0 0 1 14.08 0"/><path d="M1.42 9a16 16 0 0 1 21.16 0"/><path d="M8.53 16.11a6 6 0 0 1 6.95 0"/><line x1="12" y1="20" x2="12.01" y2="20"/></svg>
    </div>
    <div class="network-meta">
    <span class="network-ssid" id="net-ssid-text">${this.currentSsid}</span>
    <span class="network-subnet" id="net-count-text">Scanning subnet...</span>
    </div>
    </div>
    <div class="network-actions-group">
    <label class="device-alert-checkbox-label">
    <input type="checkbox" id="chk-alert-new-device" ${this.alertOnNewDevice ? 'checked' : ''}>
    <span>Alert me when a new unknown device joins</span>
    </label>
    <button class="btn-scan" id="btn-scan-network">
    <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><polyline points="23 4 23 10 17 10"/><polyline points="1 20 1 14 7 14"/><path d="M3.51 9a9 9 0 0 1 14.85-3.36L23 10M1 14l4.64 4.36A9 9 0 0 0 20.49 15"/></svg>
    <span id="scan-btn-label">Scan Network</span>
    </button>
    </div>
    </div>
    <div class="network-table-wrapper">
    <div class="network-table">
    <div class="table-header">
    <div class="th-cell net-th-status">Status</div>
    <div class="th-cell net-th-name">Device Name & Type</div>
    <div class="th-cell net-th-ip">IP Address</div>
    <div class="th-cell net-th-mac">MAC Address</div>
    <div class="th-cell net-th-vendor">Manufacturer (OUI)</div>
    <div class="th-cell net-th-seen">First / Last Seen</div>
    </div>
    <div class="table-body" id="net-table-body"></div>
    </div>
    </div>
    `;
    this._bindEvents();
    this._syncSystemArpTable();
    this.refresh();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    const btnScan = this.container.querySelector('#btn-scan-network');
    btnScan.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.triggerScan();
    });
    const chkAlert = this.container.querySelector('#chk-alert-new-device');
    chkAlert.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      this.alertOnNewDevice = e.target.checked;
    });
  }

  async _syncSystemArpTable() {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch('/api/network/arp');
      if (!resp.ok) return;
      const data = await resp.json();
      // AUDIT FUNC-06: the Rust daemon returns a bare JSON array, not {devices:[...]}
      const devices = Array.isArray(data)
      ? data
      : (data && Array.isArray(data.devices) ? data.devices : []);
      for (const d of devices) {
        if (!d || !d.mac || d.mac === 'UNKNOWN') continue;
        this.devicesRepo.upsert({
          mac_address: d.mac,
          last_ip: d.ip,
          hostname: d.hostname || `Host-${d.ip}`,
          device_type: (d.ip || '').endsWith('.1') ? 'router' : 'desktop',
                                vendor_name: lookupMacVendor(d.mac),
                                is_online: d.is_online !== undefined ? d.is_online : 1
        });
      }
      // AUDIT FUNC-05: feed live kernel ARP data to the spoofing watchdog
      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.securityWatchdog) {
        window.gwApp.securityWatchdog.checkArpTable(devices);
      }
      this.refresh();
    } catch {}
  }

  async triggerScan() {
    if (this.isScanning) return;
    this.isScanning = true;
    const btnScan = this.container.querySelector('#btn-scan-network');
    const btnLabel = this.container.querySelector('#scan-btn-label');
    btnScan.classList.add('scanning');
    btnLabel.textContent = 'Scanning...';
    await this._syncSystemArpTable();
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 900));
    // AUDIT DATA-01: never fabricate devices — report only what the kernel sees.
    this.isScanning = false;
    btnScan.classList.remove('scanning');
    btnLabel.textContent = 'Scan Network';
    this.refresh();
  }

  refresh() {
    const tableBody = this.container.querySelector('#net-table-body');
    const countText = this.container.querySelector('#net-count-text');
    if (!tableBody) return;
    const devices = this.devicesRepo.getAll();
    const onlineCount = devices.filter(d => d.is_online).length;
    countText.textContent = `${onlineCount} of ${devices.length} devices connected`;
    tableBody.innerHTML = devices.length === 0 ? '<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);">No devices detected on local network</div>' : '';
    const deviceIcons = {
      router: '📡',
      desktop: '💻',
      phone: '📱',
      server: '🖧',
      tv: '📺',
      printer: '🖨️',
      generic: '🔌'
    };
    for (const dev of devices) {
      const icon = deviceIcons[dev.device_type] || '🔌';
      const displayName = dev.custom_name || dev.hostname || 'Device';
      const vendor = dev.vendor_name || lookupMacVendor(dev.mac_address);
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'net-row';
      row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="net-th-status" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">
      <span class="status-dot ${dev.is_online ? '' : 'offline'}" title="${dev.is_online ? 'Device Online' : 'Device Offline'}"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="device-name-container">
      <span class="device-type-icon">${icon}</span>
      <input type="text" class="device-name-input" value="${esc(displayName)}" title="Click to edit custom device name" />
      </div>
      <div class="net-ip">${esc(dev.last_ip)}</div>
      <div class="net-mac">${esc(formatMacAddress(dev.mac_address))}</div>
      <div class="net-vendor">${esc(vendor)}</div>
      <div class="net-seen">${esc(formatDate(dev.last_seen_ts))}</div>
      `;
      const input = row.querySelector('.device-name-input');
      input.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        const newName = e.target.value.trim();
        this.devicesRepo.update(dev.device_id, { custom_name: newName });
      });
      tableBody.appendChild(row);
    }
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/lan-scanner.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/mini-viewer.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Floating Mini Viewer Widget (SCR-11)
 * Compact Always-on-top HUD widget displaying live traffic curves and speed.
 */

import { formatSpeed } from '../utils/formatters.js';

export class MiniViewerWidget {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.container = options.container || document.getElementById('mini-viewer-widget');
    this.onRestoreMain = options.onRestoreMain || (() => {});
    this.isOpen = false;
    this.opacity = 0.95;
    this.stayOnTop = true;

    this.samples = [];
    this.maxSamples = 40;
    this.animFrameId = null;
  }

  mount() {
    this.container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="mini-viewer-header" id="mini-drag-handle">
        <span style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;">
          <svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><circle cx="16" cy="16" r="14" fill="none" stroke="#00C2FF" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M6 16 L14 16 L17 10 L20 22 L23 16 L26 16" fill="none" stroke="#00D4B2" stroke-width="3"/></svg>
          CachyWire HUD
        </span>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;">
          <input type="range" id="mini-opacity-slider" min="20" max="100" value="95" style="width:50px;height:4px;" title="Adjust Opacity" />
          <button style="background:transparent;border:none;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);cursor:pointer;" id="mini-close-btn">✕</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mini-viewer-body">
        <canvas id="mini-canvas"></canvas>
        <div class="mini-speeds-overlay">
          <span class="mini-down">↓ <span id="mini-down-val">0 B/s</span></span>
          <span class="mini-up">↑ <span id="mini-up-val">0 B/s</span></span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    this.canvas = this.container.querySelector('#mini-canvas');
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');

    this._bindEvents();
    this._initCanvasResize();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    // Close button
    this.container.querySelector('#mini-close-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.close();
    });

    // Opacity slider
    const slider = this.container.querySelector('#mini-opacity-slider');
    slider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      this.opacity = parseInt(e.target.value, 10) / 100;
      this.container.style.opacity = this.opacity;
    });

    // Double click to restore main UI (FUNC-11, Section 2)
    this.container.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
      this.onRestoreMain();
    });

    // Drag handle
    const handle = this.container.querySelector('#mini-drag-handle');
    let isDragging = false;
    let offsetX = 0;
    let offsetY = 0;

    handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      isDragging = true;
      offsetX = e.clientX - this.container.offsetLeft;
      offsetY = e.clientY - this.container.offsetTop;
      document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
    });

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      if (!isDragging) return;
      this.container.style.left = `${e.clientX - offsetX}px`;
      this.container.style.top = `${e.clientY - offsetY}px`;
      this.container.style.bottom = 'auto';
      this.container.style.right = 'auto';
    });

    window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      isDragging = false;
      document.body.style.userSelect = '';
    });
  }

  _initCanvasResize() {
    const rect = this.canvas.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.canvas.width = rect.width || 260;
    this.canvas.height = rect.height || 100;
  }

  open() {
    this.isOpen = true;
    this.container.classList.add('open');
    this._startRenderLoop();
  }

  close() {
    this.isOpen = false;
    this.container.classList.remove('open');
    if (this.animFrameId) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(this.animFrameId);
    }
  }

  toggle() {
    if (this.isOpen) this.close();
    else this.open();
  }

  pushSample(sample) {
    if (!this.isOpen) return;

    this.samples.push({
      bytes_in: sample.bytes_in || 0,
      bytes_out: sample.bytes_out || 0
    });

    if (this.samples.length > this.maxSamples) {
      this.samples.shift();
    }

    const downVal = this.container.querySelector('#mini-down-val');
    const upVal = this.container.querySelector('#mini-up-val');
    if (downVal && upVal) {
      downVal.textContent = formatSpeed(sample.bytes_in || 0);
      upVal.textContent = formatSpeed(sample.bytes_out || 0);
    }
  }

  _startRenderLoop() {
    const render = () => {
      if (!this.isOpen) return;
      this._draw();
      this.animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);
    };
    this.animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);
  }

  _draw() {
    if (!this.ctx || !this.canvas) return;
    const { width, height } = this.canvas;
    const ctx = this.ctx;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    if (this.samples.length < 2) return;

    let maxVal = 50 * 1024;
    for (const s of this.samples) {
      if (s.bytes_in > maxVal) maxVal = s.bytes_in;
      if (s.bytes_out > maxVal) maxVal = s.bytes_out;
    }
    maxVal *= 1.2;

    const stepX = width / (this.maxSamples - 1);
    const startOffset = width - ((this.samples.length - 1) * stepX);

    // Inbound filled sparkline (#00D4B2)
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(startOffset, height);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.samples.length; i++) {
      const x = startOffset + (i * stepX);
      const y = height - ((this.samples[i].bytes_in / maxVal) * (height - 15));
      ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.lineTo(width, height);
    ctx.closePath();

    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 212, 178, 0.3)';
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.strokeStyle = '#00D4B2';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.8;
    ctx.stroke();

    // Outbound sparkline (#FF9F43)
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < this.samples.length; i++) {
      const x = startOffset + (i * stepX);
      const y = height - ((this.samples[i].bytes_out / maxVal) * (height - 15));
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#FF9F43';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/mini-viewer.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/modals.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Modals Controller
 * Implements:
 * - SCR-07: Application Detail Modal
 * - SCR-08: Remote Host Detail Modal

import { formatBytes, formatDateTime } from '../utils/formatters.js';

export class ModalsController {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.appsRepo = options.appsRepo;
    this.hostsRepo = options.hostsRepo;
    this.trafficRepo = options.trafficRepo;
    this.firewallPolicy = options.firewallPolicy;

  }

        </div>

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SCR-07: Application Detail Modal
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  showAppDetailModal(app) {
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
    if (!backdrop) return;

    const rating = this.securityScoreEngine.getScoreRating(app.security_score);
    const glyph = app.app_name.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();

    backdrop.innerHTML = `
