          </div>
          <button class="dialog-close-btn" id="modal-close-x">✕</button>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-body">
          <table class="meta-table">
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Executable Path</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${app.exe_path}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Publisher</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${app.publisher || 'Unknown'} ${app.is_signed ? '✓ (Authenticode Verified)' : '⚠️ (Unsigned)'}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">File Version</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${app.file_version || '1.0.0.0'}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">SHA-256 Hash</td>
              <td class="meta-val" style="font-size: 11px;">${app.sha256_hash}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">CachyWire Score</td>
              <td class="meta-val">
                <span class="score-badge ${rating.cssClass}">${app.security_score}/100 ${rating.label}</span>
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">VirusTotal Status</td>
              <td class="meta-val">
                ${app.vt_total > 0
                  ? `<span class="vt-pill ${app.vt_positives === 0 ? 'clean' : 'infected'}">${app.vt_positives} / ${app.vt_total} detections</span>`
                  : '<span class="vt-pill unknown">Not yet scanned</span>'}
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Firewall State</td>
              <td class="meta-val" style="color:${app.firewall_state === 1 ? 'var(--gw-danger)' : 'var(--gw-safe)'}; font-weight:700;">
                ${app.firewall_state === 1 ? 'BLOCKED' : 'ALLOWED'}
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">First Seen</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${formatDateTime(app.first_seen_ts)}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Last Activity</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${formatDateTime(app.last_seen_ts)}</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-footer">
          <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-open-file-location">Open File Location</button>
          <button class="pill-btn ${app.firewall_state === 1 ? 'active' : ''}" id="btn-modal-toggle-block">
            ${app.firewall_state === 1 ? 'Unblock Application' : 'Block Application'}
          </button>
          <button class="pill-btn active" id="btn-modal-done">Close</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    backdrop.classList.add('open');

    // Close handlers
    const close = () => {
      backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.trafficView) {
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderGraph();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderScrubber();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.refreshAppsList();
      }
    };
    backdrop.querySelector('#modal-close-x').addEventListener('click', close);
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-modal-done').addEventListener('click', close);

    // Open file location handler (Section 3.2 Screen 1 / 4)
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-open-file-location').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (window.app && window.app.shell) {
        window.app.shell.showToast(`File location: ${app.exe_path}`, 'info');
      }
    });

    // Toggle block button
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-modal-toggle-block').addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.firewallPolicy.toggleBlock(app.app_id);
      if (window.app && window.app.firewallView) {
        window.app.firewallView.refresh();
      }
      this.showAppDetailModal(this.appsRepo.getById(app.app_id));
    });
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SCR-08: Remote Host Detail Modal
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  showHostDetailModal(host) {
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
    if (!backdrop) return;

    backdrop.innerHTML = `
      <div class="gw-dialog" style="width: 520px;">
        <div class="dialog-header">
          <div class="dialog-title">
            <span>🌐</span>
            <span>Remote Host: ${host.domain_name || host.ip_address}</span>
          </div>
          <button class="dialog-close-btn" id="modal-close-x">✕</button>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-body">
          <table class="meta-table">
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">IP Address</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${host.ip_address}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Hostname / Domain</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${host.domain_name || 'No reverse DNS record'}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Country</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${host.country_iso2} (${host.country_iso2 === 'LAN' ? 'Local Network' : 'Global'})</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Organization / ASN</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${host.asn_org || 'Standard Autonomous System'}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">First Contact</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${formatDateTime(host.first_seen_ts)}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Last Seen</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${formatDateTime(host.last_seen_ts)}</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-footer">
          <button class="pill-btn active" id="btn-modal-done">Close</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    backdrop.classList.add('open');
    const close = () => {
      backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.trafficView) {
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderGraph();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderScrubber();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.refreshAppsList();
      }
    };
    backdrop.querySelector('#modal-close-x').addEventListener('click', close);
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-modal-done').addEventListener('click', close);
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SCR-09: Ask to Connect Firewall Prompt
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  showAskToConnectPrompt(app, onDecision) {
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
    if (!backdrop) return;

    backdrop.innerHTML = `
      <div class="gw-dialog ask-connect-prompt">
        <div class="dialog-header" style="background: rgba(0, 194, 255, 0.08);">
          <div class="dialog-title" style="color: var(--gw-accent);">
            <span>🛡️ CachyWire Firewall Alert</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-body">
          <div style="font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--gw-text-primary);">
            New application wants to connect to the Internet:
          </div>

          <div class="prompt-icon-area">
            <span class="app-vector-icon" style="width:36px;height:36px;display:inline-flex;">${getAppIconSvg(app.app_name, app.exe_path)}</span>
            <div>
              <div style="font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--gw-text-primary);">${app.app_name}</div>
              <div style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--gw-text-secondary);">${app.publisher || 'Unverified Publisher'}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <table class="meta-table">
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Path</td>
              <td class="meta-val" style="font-size: 11px;">${app.exe_path}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="meta-label">Digital Signature</td>
              <td class="meta-val">${app.is_signed ? '✓ Valid Authenticode' : '⚠️ Unverified'}</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-footer" style="padding: 16px;">
          <div class="prompt-actions">
            <button class="prompt-btn allow" id="btn-prompt-allow">
              ✓ Allow Connection
            </button>
            <button class="prompt-btn block" id="btn-prompt-block">
              ✕ Block Connection
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    backdrop.classList.add('open');

    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-prompt-allow').addEventListener('click', () => {
      backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      onDecision(true);
    });

    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-prompt-block').addEventListener('click', () => {
      backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      onDecision(false);
    });
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SCR-10: Multi-Tab Settings Dialog
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  showSettingsDialog() {
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
    if (!backdrop) return;

    backdrop.innerHTML = `
      <div class="gw-dialog settings-dialog">
        <div class="dialog-header">
          <div class="dialog-title">
            <span>⚙️</span>
            <span>CachyWire Settings</span>
          </div>
          <button class="dialog-close-btn" id="modal-close-x">✕</button>
        </div>

        <div class="settings-layout">
          <!-- Sidebar Tabs -->
          <div class="settings-tabs-sidebar">
            <button class="settings-tab-btn active" data-tab="general">General</button>
            <button class="settings-tab-btn" data-tab="security">Security Watchdogs</button>
            <button class="settings-tab-btn" data-tab="server">Server & Database</button>
            <button class="settings-tab-btn" data-tab="remote-server">Remote Server</button>
            <button class="settings-tab-btn" data-tab="remote-conns">Remote Connections</button>
            <button class="settings-tab-btn" data-tab="virustotal">VirusTotal</button>
          </div>

          <!-- Tab 1: General -->
          <div class="settings-tab-content active" id="tab-general">
            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Run on CachyOS Startup</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Launch CachyWire automatically when logging into your Linux desktop session</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-startup" ${this.settings.runOnStartup ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row" style="flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Interface Theme & Skins (Premium Unlocked)</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Click any theme below to preview and apply immediately</span>
              </div>
              <div class="gw-pill-group" id="set-theme-group" data-value="${this.settings.theme || 'dark'}" style="display:flex;gap:8px;width:100%;flex-wrap:wrap;">
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${(!this.settings.theme || this.settings.theme === 'dark') ? 'active' : ''}" data-theme-val="dark">Dark Cyber (Default)</button>
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${this.settings.theme === 'light' ? 'active' : ''}" data-theme-val="light">Light Modern</button>
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${this.settings.theme === 'midnight' ? 'active' : ''}" data-theme-val="midnight">Midnight Blue</button>
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${this.settings.theme === 'cyberpunk' ? 'active' : ''}" data-theme-val="cyberpunk">Cyberpunk Neon</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Hide Idle Applications</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Hide applications with zero recent network throughput from tables</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-hide-idle" ${this.settings.hideIdleApps ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Tab 2: Security Watchdogs -->
          <div class="settings-tab-content" id="tab-security">
            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">ARP Spoofing Detection</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Monitor Linux kernel ARP neighbor table (/proc/net/arp) for gateway MAC spoofing</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-wd-arp" ${this.settings.watchdogs.arpSpoofing ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Hosts File Modification Alert</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Detect unauthorized DNS overrides or modifications to /etc/hosts</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-wd-hosts" ${this.settings.watchdogs.hostsFileModification ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">DNS Server Change Monitor</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Notify if /etc/resolv.conf or NetworkManager DNS resolvers are hijacked</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-wd-dns" ${this.settings.watchdogs.dnsChange ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Wi-Fi Evil Twin Warning</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Monitor Linux wireless interfaces (wlan0 / NetworkManager) for rogue SSIDs</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-wd-evil-twin" ${this.settings.watchdogs.wifiEvilTwin ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Application ELF Binary Hash Integrity (BR-02)</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Verify SHA-256 of /usr/bin ELF binaries and alert on modification</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-wd-binary" ${this.settings.watchdogs.binaryIntegrityChange ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Tab 3: Server & Database -->
          <div class="settings-tab-content" id="tab-server">
            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">History Retention Period</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Duration of time-series network activity stored on disk</span>
              </div>
              <div class="gw-pill-group" id="set-retention-group" data-value="${this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays || 30}" style="display:flex;gap:6px;">
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays === 7 ? 'active' : ''}" data-ret-val="7">7d</button>
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${(!this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays || this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays === 30) ? 'active' : ''}" data-ret-val="30">30d</button>
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays === 90 ? 'active' : ''}" data-ret-val="90">90d</button>
                <button type="button" class="pill-btn ${this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays === 365 ? 'active' : ''}" data-ret-val="365">1 Year</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info" style="flex:1;margin-right:12px;">
                <span class="setting-title">Database Storage Path (CachyOS Linux)</span>
                <input type="text" class="gw-select" id="input-db-path" value="${this.settings.server.dbPath || '~/.local/share/cachywire/cachywire_db.json'}" style="width:100%;margin-top:4px;font-family:var(--gw-font-mono);font-size:11px;" />
              </div>
              <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-change-db-path">Apply Path</button>
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Backup & Restore (Scenario 9)</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Export or restore full CachyWire database snapshot</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;">
                <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-backup-db">Backup</button>
                <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-restore-db">Restore</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Clear All Recorded Data & Reset to Zero</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Wipe all recorded traffic, apps, and hosts to start recording fresh from 0 B</span>
              </div>
              <button class="pill-btn" style="border-color:var(--gw-danger);color:var(--gw-danger);font-weight:700;" id="btn-clear-history">🧹 Clear All Data Now</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Tab 4: Remote Server -->
          <div class="settings-tab-content" id="tab-remote-server">
            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Allow Remote Connections</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Listen on TCP port 7010 for remote CachyWire clients (FUNC-10)</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-allow-remote" ${this.settings.server.allowRemoteConnections ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Listening Port</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Default is 7010 (requires TLS encapsulation)</span>
              </div>
              <input type="text" inputmode="numeric" class="gw-select" id="set-remote-port" value="${this.settings.server.listenPort}" style="width:100px;" />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">IP Subnet Whitelist</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Authorized IP ranges permitted to connect</span>
              </div>
              <input type="text" class="gw-select" id="set-remote-whitelist" value="${this.settings.server.allowedIpWhitelist}" style="width:200px;" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Tab 5: Remote Connections -->
          <div class="settings-tab-content" id="tab-remote-conns">
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);margin-bottom:8px;">
              Manage remote Linux servers monitored from this interface:
            </div>
            <div id="remote-servers-list" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;">
              ${this.settings.remoteConnections.map(c => `
                <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px;background:var(--gw-bg-primary);border:1px solid var(--gw-border);border-radius:6px;">
                  <div>
                    <span style="font-weight:700;color:var(--gw-text-primary);">${c.name}</span>
                    <span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);margin-left:8px;">${c.host}:${c.port}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gw-safe);">${c.isCurrent ? '● Active' : 'Connect'}</span>
                </div>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px;align-items:center;">
              <input type="text" class="gw-select" id="input-remote-srv-name" placeholder="Server Name (e.g. CachyOS Node 2)" style="flex:1;" />
              <input type="text" class="gw-select" id="input-remote-srv-host" placeholder="IP / Host (e.g. 192.168.0.50)" style="width:160px;" />
              <button class="pill-btn active" id="btn-add-remote-server">+ Add</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Tab 6: VirusTotal -->
          <div class="settings-tab-content" id="tab-virustotal">
            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">VirusTotal API Key</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Enter free or commercial API key from virustotal.com</span>
              </div>
              <input type="text" class="gw-select" id="set-vt-key" value="${this.settings.virustotal.apiKey}" placeholder="Paste 64-char hex key" style="width:260px;font-family:var(--gw-font-mono);font-size:11px;" />
            </div>

            <div class="setting-row">
              <div class="setting-info">
                <span class="setting-title">Automatic Background Scan</span>
                <span class="setting-desc">Automatically inspect SHA-256 hash of every newly detected Linux binary</span>
              </div>
              <input type="checkbox" class="gw-checkbox" id="set-vt-autoscan" ${this.settings.virustotal.autoScanNewApps ? 'checked' : ''} />
            </div>

            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);background:var(--gw-bg-primary);padding:10px;border-radius:6px;margin-top:12px;">
              By using VirusTotal with CachyWire, you agree to VirusTotal Terms of Service. File hashes are submitted to VirusTotal API v3 over TLS.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-footer">
          <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-settings-cancel">Cancel</button>
          <button class="pill-btn active" id="btn-settings-save">Save Changes</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    backdrop.classList.add('open');

    // Tab switching
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('.settings-tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.settings-tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.settings-tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        const tabName = btn.getAttribute('data-tab');
        backdrop.querySelector(`#tab-${tabName}`).classList.add('active');
      });
    });

    // Instant Theme Pill Switching (no native GTK dropdown popup freeze)
    const themeGroup = backdrop.querySelector('#set-theme-group');
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-val]').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const nextTheme = btn.getAttribute('data-theme-val');
        themeGroup?.setAttribute('data-value', nextTheme);
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-val]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        this.settings.theme = nextTheme;
        if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.shell) {
          window.gwApp.shell.applyTheme(nextTheme);
        } else {
          document.body.className = `theme-${nextTheme}`;
        }
      });
    });

    // Retention Period Pill Switching
    const retGroup = backdrop.querySelector('#set-retention-group');
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ret-val]').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const val = btn.getAttribute('data-ret-val');
        retGroup?.setAttribute('data-value', val);
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ret-val]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
      });
    });

    const close = () => {
      backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.trafficView) {
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderGraph();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderScrubber();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.refreshAppsList();
      }
    };
    backdrop.querySelector('#modal-close-x').addEventListener('click', close);
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-settings-cancel').addEventListener('click', close);

    // Save Changes (Persists all 6 tabs to localStorage & applies immediately)
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-settings-save').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const theme = themeGroup?.getAttribute('data-value') || this.settings.theme || 'dark';
      const startup = backdrop.querySelector('#set-startup').checked;
      const hideIdle = backdrop.querySelector('#set-hide-idle').checked;

      // Tab 2: Security Watchdogs
      this.settings.watchdogs.arpSpoofing = !!backdrop.querySelector('#set-wd-arp')?.checked;
      this.settings.watchdogs.hostsFileModification = !!backdrop.querySelector('#set-wd-hosts')?.checked;
      this.settings.watchdogs.dnsChange = !!backdrop.querySelector('#set-wd-dns')?.checked;
      this.settings.watchdogs.wifiEvilTwin = !!backdrop.querySelector('#set-wd-evil-twin')?.checked;
      this.settings.watchdogs.binaryIntegrityChange = !!backdrop.querySelector('#set-wd-binary')?.checked;

      // Tab 3: Server & Database
      const retentionDays = parseInt(retGroup?.getAttribute('data-value') || '30', 10);
      const customPath = backdrop.querySelector('#input-db-path')?.value?.trim();
      this.settings.server.historyRetentionDays = retentionDays;
      if (customPath) {
        this.settings.server.dbPath = customPath;
        this.exportService.db.dbPath = customPath;
      }

      // Tab 4: Remote Server
      this.settings.server.allowRemoteConnections = !!backdrop.querySelector('#set-allow-remote')?.checked;
      this.settings.server.listenPort = parseInt(backdrop.querySelector('#set-remote-port')?.value || '7010', 10);
      this.settings.server.allowedIpWhitelist = backdrop.querySelector('#set-remote-whitelist')?.value?.trim() || '127.0.0.1';

      // Tab 6: VirusTotal
      const vtKey = backdrop.querySelector('#set-vt-key').value;
      const vtAuto = backdrop.querySelector('#set-vt-autoscan').checked;

      this.settings.theme = theme;
      this.settings.runOnStartup = startup;
      this.settings.hideIdleApps = hideIdle;
      this.settings.virustotal.apiKey = vtKey;
      this.settings.virustotal.autoScanNewApps = vtAuto;
      this.virusTotalClient.setApiKey(vtKey);
      this.virusTotalClient.setAutoScan(vtAuto);

      fetch('/api/system/autostart', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ enabled: startup })
      }).catch(() => {});

      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem('cachywire_cachyos_settings_v3', JSON.stringify(this.settings));
      }

      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.shell) {
        window.gwApp.shell.applyTheme(theme);
        window.gwApp.shell.showToast('✓ تم حفظ جميع الإعدادات وتطبيقها بنجاح', 'info');
      } else {
        document.body.className = '';
        if (theme !== 'light') {
          document.body.classList.add(`theme-${theme}`);
        }
      }

      this.onSettingsUpdated(this.settings);
      close();
    });

    // Apply DB Path inline
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-change-db-path')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const nextPath = backdrop.querySelector('#input-db-path')?.value?.trim();
      if (nextPath) {
        this.settings.server.dbPath = nextPath;
        this.exportService.db.dbPath = nextPath;
        if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
          localStorage.setItem('cachywire_cachyos_settings_v3', JSON.stringify(this.settings));
        }
        if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.shell) {
          window.gwApp.shell.showToast(`✓ تم تحديث مسار قاعدة البيانات: ${nextPath}`, 'info');
        }
      }
    });

    // Add Remote Server inline
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-add-remote-server')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const nameInput = backdrop.querySelector('#input-remote-srv-name');
      const hostInput = backdrop.querySelector('#input-remote-srv-host');
      const name = (nameInput?.value || '').trim() || `CachyOS Node #${this.settings.remoteConnections.length + 1}`;
      const host = (hostInput?.value || '').trim() || '192.168.0.50';

      this.settings.remoteConnections.push({
        id: 'srv_' + Date.now(),
        name,
        host,
        port: 7010,
        isCurrent: false,
        status: 'Connected'
      });
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem('cachywire_cachyos_settings_v3', JSON.stringify(this.settings));
      }
      this.showSettingsDialog();
      const tabBtn = backdrop.querySelector('.settings-tab-btn[data-tab="remote-conns"]');
      if (tabBtn) tabBtn.click();
    });

    // Clear All Data & History Button (No blocking confirm/alert)
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-clear-history').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (window.gwApp && typeof window.gwApp.clearAllRecordedData === 'function') {
        await window.gwApp.clearAllRecordedData();
      } else {
        this.exportService.db.clearAllData();
      }
      close();
    });

    // Backup DB
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-backup-db').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const backup = this.exportService.exportBackup();
      const blob = new Blob([backup], { type: 'application/json' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `CachyWire_DB_Backup_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    });

    // Restore DB
    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-restore-db').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const input = document.createElement('input');
      input.type = 'file';
      input.accept = '.json';
      input.onchange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (event) => {
          try {
            this.exportService.importBackup(event.target.result);
            if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.shell) {
              window.gwApp.shell.showToast('✓ تم استعادة نسخة قاعدة البيانات الاحتياطية بنجاح', 'info');
            }
            close();
          } catch (err) {
            if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.shell) {
              window.gwApp.shell.showToast('⚠️ ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح', 'critical');
            }
          }
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
      };
      input.click();
    });
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Data Plan Settings Modal
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  showDataPlanModal(dataPlanMonitor, onSaved) {
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
    if (!backdrop) return;

    const cfg = dataPlanMonitor.config;
    const limitGb = Math.round(cfg.limitBytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024));

    backdrop.innerHTML = `
      <div class="gw-dialog" style="width: 460px;">
        <div class="dialog-header">
          <div class="dialog-title">
            <span>📊</span>
            <span>Data Plan & Bandwidth Overuse</span>
          </div>
          <button class="dialog-close-btn" id="modal-close-x">✕</button>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-body">
          <div class="setting-row">
            <div class="setting-info">
              <span class="setting-title">Enable Data Plan Monitoring</span>
              <span class="setting-desc">Audit external data against billing cycle</span>
            </div>
            <input type="checkbox" id="dp-enable" ${cfg.enabled ? 'checked' : ''} />
          </div>

          <div class="setting-row">
            <div class="setting-info">
              <span class="setting-title">Plan Limit (Gigabytes)</span>
              <span class="setting-desc">Monthly quota cap</span>
            </div>
            <div class="gw-stepper">
              <button type="button" class="gw-stepper-btn" id="dp-limit-minus">−</button>
              <input type="text" inputmode="numeric" id="dp-limit-gb" value="${limitGb}" />
              <button type="button" class="gw-stepper-btn" id="dp-limit-plus">+</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="setting-row">
            <div class="setting-info">
              <span class="setting-title">Billing Day of Month</span>
              <span class="setting-desc">Day the plan resets (1 - 31)</span>
            </div>
            <div class="gw-stepper">
              <button type="button" class="gw-stepper-btn" id="dp-day-minus">−</button>
              <input type="text" inputmode="numeric" id="dp-bill-day" value="${cfg.billingDayOfMonth}" />
              <button type="button" class="gw-stepper-btn" id="dp-day-plus">+</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="setting-row">
            <div class="setting-info">
              <span class="setting-title">Warning Alert Threshold</span>
              <span class="setting-desc">Alert percentage before quota exhaustion</span>
            </div>
            <select class="gw-select" id="dp-warn-pct">
              <option value="50" ${cfg.warnThresholdPercent === 50 ? 'selected' : ''}>50%</option>
              <option value="75" ${cfg.warnThresholdPercent === 75 ? 'selected' : ''}>75%</option>
              <option value="80" ${cfg.warnThresholdPercent === 80 ? 'selected' : ''}>80% (Default)</option>
              <option value="90" ${cfg.warnThresholdPercent === 90 ? 'selected' : ''}>90%</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="dialog-footer">
          <button class="pill-btn" id="modal-close-btn">Cancel</button>
          <button class="pill-btn active" id="btn-save-plan">Save Plan</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    backdrop.classList.add('open');

    const close = () => {
      backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.trafficView) {
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderGraph();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.renderScrubber();
        window.gwApp.trafficView.refreshAppsList();
      }
    };
    backdrop.querySelector('#modal-close-x').addEventListener('click', close);
    backdrop.querySelector('#modal-close-btn').addEventListener('click', close);

    const limitInput = backdrop.querySelector('#dp-limit-gb');
    const dayInput = backdrop.querySelector('#dp-bill-day');

    backdrop.querySelector('#dp-limit-minus')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const curr = parseInt(limitInput.value, 10) || 100;
      limitInput.value = Math.max(1, curr - 10);
    });
    backdrop.querySelector('#dp-limit-plus')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const curr = parseInt(limitInput.value, 10) || 100;
      limitInput.value = Math.min(10000, curr + 10);
    });
    backdrop.querySelector('#dp-day-minus')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const curr = parseInt(dayInput.value, 10) || 1;
      dayInput.value = Math.max(1, curr - 1);
    });
    backdrop.querySelector('#dp-day-plus')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const curr = parseInt(dayInput.value, 10) || 1;
      dayInput.value = Math.min(31, curr + 1);
    });

    backdrop.querySelector('#btn-save-plan').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const enabled = backdrop.querySelector('#dp-enable').checked;
      const limitGbVal = parseFloat(backdrop.querySelector('#dp-limit-gb').value) || 100;
      const billingDay = parseInt(backdrop.querySelector('#dp-bill-day').value, 10) || 1;
      const warnPct = parseInt(backdrop.querySelector('#dp-warn-pct').value, 10) || 80;

      dataPlanMonitor.updateConfig({
        enabled,
        limitBytes: limitGbVal * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,
        billingDayOfMonth: billingDay,
        warnThresholdPercent: warnPct
      });

      onSaved();
      close();
    });
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/modals.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/shell.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Shell & Navigation Controller (SCR-01)
 * AUDIT FIXES: Snooze pill added to header (SCR-01), showToast now respects
 * Snooze mode + caps concurrent toasts at 3, keyboard shortcuts ignore inputs.
 */
export function postNativeDesktopMessage(action, payload = {}) {
  try {
    if (window.webkit && window.webkit.messageHandlers && window.webkit.messageHandlers.cachywire) {
      window.webkit.messageHandlers.cachywire.postMessage(typeof action === 'string' ? action : JSON.stringify({ action, ...payload }));
      return true;
    }
    if (window.chrome && window.chrome.webview) {
      window.chrome.webview.postMessage(action);
      return true;
    }
  } catch {}
  return false;
}

    <svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M21 12.79A9 9 0 1 1 11.21 3 7 7 0 0 0 21 12.79z"/></svg>
    </button>
    <div class="window-controls">
    <button class="win-btn" id="win-min" title="Minimize Window">─</button>
    <button class="win-btn" id="win-max" title="Maximize / Restore Window">□</button>
    <button class="win-btn close" id="win-close" title="Hide to Background System Tray (Next to Clock)">✕</button>
    </div>
    </div>
    </header>
    <main id="viewport-main">
    <div class="view-container active" id="view-traffic"></div>
    <div class="view-container" id="view-firewall"></div>
    <div class="view-container" id="view-alerts"></div>
    <div class="view-container" id="view-network"></div>
    <div class="view-container" id="view-usage"></div>
    </main>
    <!-- Toast Notifications Container -->
    <div id="toast-container"></div>
    `;
    this._bindEvents();
    this._bindKeyboardShortcuts();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    this.container.querySelectorAll('.header-nav-tab').forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const targetView = tab.getAttribute('data-view');
        this.switchView(targetView);
      });
    });
    const brandMenu = this.container.querySelector('#btn-gw-menu');
    const dropdown = this.container.querySelector('#gw-brand-dropdown');
    brandMenu.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const isVisible = dropdown.style.display === 'block';
      dropdown.style.display = isVisible ? 'none' : 'block';
    });
    document.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (dropdown) dropdown.style.display = 'none';
    });
      this.container.querySelector('#menu-clear-all-data')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onClearAllData());
      this.container.querySelector('#btn-clear-all-data')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onClearAllData());
      this.container.querySelector('#menu-open-settings')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onOpenSettings());
      this.container.querySelector('#menu-open-dataplan')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onOpenDataPlan());
      this.container.querySelector('#menu-toggle-incognito')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onIncognitoToggle());
      this.container.querySelector('#menu-toggle-snooze')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onSnoozeToggle());
      this.container.querySelector('#menu-open-license')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onOpenLicenseInfo());
      dropdown.querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(item => {
        item.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const theme = item.getAttribute('data-theme');
          this.applyTheme(theme);
          this.onThemeChange(theme);
        });
      });
      this.container.querySelector('#btn-theme-toggle')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const nextTheme = this.state.theme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light';
        this.applyTheme(nextTheme);
        this.onThemeChange(nextTheme);
      });
      this.container.querySelector('#pill-firewall-toggle')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onFirewallToggle());
      this.container.querySelector('#pill-incognito-toggle')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onIncognitoToggle());
      this.container.querySelector('#pill-snooze-toggle')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.onSnoozeToggle());
      this.container.querySelector('#win-min')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!postNativeDesktopMessage('minimize_window')) {
          this.showToast('Application minimized', 'info');
        }
      });
      this.container.querySelector('#win-max')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!postNativeDesktopMessage('maximize_window')) {
          if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
            document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().catch(() => {});
          } else {
            document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
          }
        }
      });
      this.container.querySelector('#win-close')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!postNativeDesktopMessage('minimize_to_tray')) {
          this.showToast('CachyWire continues monitoring in background System Tray', 'info');
        }
      });
  }

  _bindKeyboardShortcuts() {
    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      // AUDIT UX: don't hijack Alt+numbers while the user is typing in a field
      const tag = (e.target && e.target.tagName) || '';
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
      if (e.altKey) {
        if (e.key === '1') { e.preventDefault(); this.switchView('traffic'); }
        if (e.key === '2') { e.preventDefault(); this.switchView('firewall'); }
        if (e.key === '3') { e.preventDefault(); this.switchView('usage'); }
        if (e.key === '4') { e.preventDefault(); this.switchView('network'); }
        if (e.key === '5') { e.preventDefault(); this.switchView('alerts'); }
      }
    });
  }

  applyTheme(theme) {
    this.state.theme = theme || 'dark';
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem('gw_cachyos_theme', this.state.theme);
    }
    document.body.className = `theme-${this.state.theme}`;
  }

  switchTab(viewName) {
    this.switchView(viewName);
  }

  switchView(viewName) {
    if (this.currentView === viewName) return;
    this.currentView = viewName;
    this.container.querySelectorAll('.header-nav-tab').forEach(tab => {
      tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.getAttribute('data-view') === viewName);
    });
    this.container.querySelectorAll('.view-container').forEach(view => {
      const isTarget = view.id === `view-${viewName}`;
      view.classList.toggle('active', isTarget);
    });
    this.onViewChange(viewName);
  }

  updateFirewallState(enabled, mode) {
    this.state.firewallEnabled = enabled;
    this.state.firewallMode = mode;
    const pill = this.container.querySelector('#pill-firewall-toggle');
    const text = this.container.querySelector('#text-firewall-status');
    const dot = this.container.querySelector('#dot-firewall');
    if (pill && text && dot) {
      pill.classList.toggle('active', enabled);
      const modeShort = mode === 'BLOCK_ALL' ? ' (Lockdown)' : mode === 'ASK_TO_CONNECT' ? ' (Ask)' : '';
      text.textContent = enabled ? `Firewall: ON${modeShort}` : 'Firewall: OFF';
      dot.style.background = enabled ? 'var(--gw-safe, #10B981)' : 'var(--gw-danger, #EF4444)';
    }
  }

  updateIncognitoState(active) {
    this.state.isIncognito = !!active;
    const pill = this.container.querySelector('#pill-incognito-toggle');
    const text = this.container.querySelector('#text-incognito-status');
    const menuLabel = this.container.querySelector('#menu-incognito-label');
    if (pill) pill.classList.toggle('active', this.state.isIncognito);
    if (text) text.textContent = this.state.isIncognito ? 'Incognito: ON' : 'Incognito';
    if (menuLabel) menuLabel.textContent = `Incognito Mode: ${this.state.isIncognito ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`;
  }

  updateSnoozeState(active) {
    this.state.isSnoozed = !!active;
    const pill = this.container.querySelector('#pill-snooze-toggle');
    const text = this.container.querySelector('#text-snooze-status');
    const menuLabel = this.container.querySelector('#menu-snooze-label');
    if (pill) pill.classList.toggle('active', this.state.isSnoozed);
    if (text) text.textContent = this.state.isSnoozed ? 'Snoozed (24h)' : 'Snooze';
    if (menuLabel) menuLabel.textContent = `Snooze Alerts (24h): ${this.state.isSnoozed ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`;
  }

  updateAlertsCount(count) {
    this.state.unreadAlerts = count;
    const badge = this.container.querySelector('#badge-alerts-count');
    if (badge) {
      badge.textContent = count > 99 ? '99+' : count;
      badge.style.display = count > 0 ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
    }
  }

  showToast(message, type = 'info') {
    // AUDIT FUNC-09: respect Snooze mode (critical alerts still break through)
    if (this.state.isSnoozed && type !== 'critical') return;
    const container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
    if (!container) return;
    // AUDIT UX-06: cap concurrent toasts to avoid alert storms
    while (container.children.length >= 3) container.removeChild(container.firstChild);
    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = `gw-toast ${type}`;
    toast.textContent = message;
    container.appendChild(toast);
    setTimeout(() => toast.classList.add('visible'), 10);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.remove('visible');
      setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
    }, 4000);
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/shell.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/traffic-monitor.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Traffic Monitor View (SCR-02)
 * Pixel-accurate implementation matching CachyWire 3.x reference
 * Features:
 * - Left column switchable between Apps, Traffic Types (Protocols), and Remote Hosts
 * - Right area graph with Wave (Bézier curves), Bar (grouped columns), and Geo (world breakdown) modes
 * - Interactive crosshair hover tooltip showing exact time, download/upload throughput, and active app
 * - Center summary banner with circular arc gauge, live download/upload speed meters, and WAN/LAN progress bar
 * - Dual-handled interactive timeline scrubber to freely select and zoom any duration
 */

import { formatBytes, formatSpeed } from '../utils/formatters.js';
import { getAppIconSvg } from '../utils/app-icons.js';
import { lookupGeoIp } from '../utils/geoip.js';

export class TrafficMonitorView {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.container = options.container || document.getElementById('view-traffic');
    this.db = options.db;
    this.appsRepo = options.appsRepo;
    this.hostsRepo = options.hostsRepo;
    this.trafficRepo = options.trafficRepo;
    this.onAppClick = options.onAppClick || (() => {});
    this.onHostClick = options.onHostClick || (() => {});
    this.onOpenDataPlan = options.onOpenDataPlan || (() => {});

    this.selectedPeriod = '5m';  // Default to 5m live real-time view on CachyOS
    this.selectedAdapter = 'all';
    this.sidebarType = 'apps';   // 'apps' | 'traffic' | 'hosts'
    this.selectedAppId = null;
    this.selectedProtoId = null;
    this.selectedHostId = null;
    this.viewMode = 'wave';      // 'wave' | 'bar' | 'geo'
    this.sortOrder = 'desc';     // 'desc' | 'asc'
    this.searchQuery = '';

    // Scrubber zoom window (0.0 to 1.0)
    this.zoomStart = 0.0;
    this.zoomEnd = 1.0;
    this.isDraggingScrubber = false;
    this.scrubberRafPending = false;
    this._cachedFilteredRecords = null;
    this._cachedFilterKey = '';

    this.lastBuckets = [];
    this.hoverBucketIndex = null;
    this.animFrameId = null;
  }

                </button>
                <button class="mode-btn" data-mode="geo" id="btn-mode-geo" title="Geo / Country Traffic Breakdown">
                  <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="3" y1="12" x2="21" y2="12" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"/><ellipse cx="12" cy="12" rx="4" ry="9" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"/></svg>

    this._bindEvents();
    this._initCanvases();
    this.refreshAppsList(false);
    this.renderGraph();
    this.renderScrubber();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    // Breakdown type selector (Apps / Traffic Types / Hosts)
    const selType = this.container.querySelector('#select-filter-type');
    if (selType) {
      selType.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        this.sidebarType = e.target.value;
        this.selectedAppId = null;
        this.selectedProtoId = null;
        this.selectedHostId = null;

        const colTitle = this.container.querySelector('#sidebar-col-title');
        const masterLabel = this.container.querySelector('#master-row-label');
        if (this.sidebarType === 'apps') {
          if (colTitle) colTitle.textContent = 'App';
          if (masterLabel) masterLabel.textContent = 'All apps';
        } else if (this.sidebarType === 'traffic') {
          if (colTitle) colTitle.textContent = 'Protocol';
          if (masterLabel) masterLabel.textContent = 'All traffic';
        } else {
          if (colTitle) colTitle.textContent = 'Remote Host';
          if (masterLabel) masterLabel.textContent = 'All hosts';
        }

        this.container.querySelector('#row-all-apps')?.classList.add('active');
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
        this.renderGraph();
      });
    }

    // Adapter filter
    const selAdapter = this.container.querySelector('#select-filter-adapter');
    if (selAdapter) {
      selAdapter.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        this.selectedAdapter = e.target.value;
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
        this.renderGraph();
      });
    }

    // Time window select
    const selTime = this.container.querySelector('#select-time-window');
    if (selTime) {
      selTime.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        this.selectedPeriod = e.target.value;
        this.zoomStart = 0.0;
        this.zoomEnd = 1.0;
        this.updateScrubberWindowUI();
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
        this.renderGraph();
        this.renderScrubber();
      });
    }

    // View mode buttons
    this.container.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.container.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        this.viewMode = btn.getAttribute('data-mode');
        this.renderGraph();
      });
    });

    // Master row: click to show all
    const rowAll = this.container.querySelector('#row-all-apps');
    if (rowAll) {
      rowAll.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.selectedAppId = null;
        this.selectedProtoId = null;
        this.selectedHostId = null;
        this.container.querySelectorAll('.apps-table-row').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
        rowAll.classList.add('active');
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
        this.renderGraph();
        this.renderScrubber();
      });
    }

    // Resizable Splitter interaction between Left Apps Sidebar and Right Graph
    const splitter = this.container.querySelector('#traffic-splitter');
    const sidebar = this.container.querySelector('#traffic-apps-sidebar');
    if (splitter && sidebar) {
      // Restore persisted sidebar width
      try {
        const savedW = localStorage.getItem('cachywire_sidebar_width');
        if (savedW) {
          const wNum = parseInt(savedW, 10);
          if (wNum >= 220 && wNum <= 650) {
            sidebar.style.width = `${wNum}px`;
            sidebar.style.minWidth = `${wNum}px`;
          }
        }
      } catch (_) {}

      let isDragging = false;
      let startX = 0;
      let startW = 0;

      const onSplitterMouseMove = (e) => {
        if (!isDragging) return;
        const deltaX = e.clientX - startX;
        const newW = Math.max(220, Math.min(650, startW + deltaX));
        sidebar.style.width = `${newW}px`;
        sidebar.style.minWidth = `${newW}px`;
      };

      const onSplitterMouseUp = () => {
        if (!isDragging) return;
        isDragging = false;
        splitter.classList.remove('dragging');
        document.body.style.cursor = '';
        document.body.style.userSelect = '';
        window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onSplitterMouseMove);
        window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onSplitterMouseUp);
        try {
          const finalW = parseInt(sidebar.style.width, 10);
          if (finalW) localStorage.setItem('cachywire_sidebar_width', finalW);
        } catch (_) {}
        this._resizeCanvases();
        this.renderGraph();
        this.renderScrubber();
      };

      splitter.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        isDragging = true;
        startX = e.clientX;
        startW = sidebar.getBoundingClientRect().width;
        splitter.classList.add('dragging');
        document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
        document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', onSplitterMouseMove, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('mouseup', onSplitterMouseUp);
      });
    }

    // Horizontal Resizer Splitter between Graph Canvas and Summary/Scrubber
    const hSplitter = this.container.querySelector('#traffic-h-splitter');
    const summaryStrip = this.container.querySelector('#traffic-summary-strip');
    if (hSplitter && summaryStrip) {
      try {
        const savedH = localStorage.getItem('cachywire_summary_height');
        if (savedH) {
          const hNum = parseInt(savedH, 10);
          if (hNum >= 68 && hNum <= 150) {
            summaryStrip.style.height = `${hNum}px`;
            summaryStrip.style.minHeight = `${hNum}px`;
          }
        }
      } catch (_) {}

      hSplitter.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const startY = e.clientY;
        const startH = summaryStrip.getBoundingClientRect().height;
        hSplitter.classList.add('dragging');
        document.body.style.cursor = 'row-resize';
        document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';

        const onHMouseMove = (moveEvt) => {
          const deltaY = startY - moveEvt.clientY; // dragging up expands summary
          const newH = Math.max(68, Math.min(160, startH + deltaY));
          summaryStrip.style.height = `${newH}px`;
          summaryStrip.style.minHeight = `${newH}px`;
          this._resizeCanvases();
          this.renderGraph();
          this.renderScrubber();
        };

        const onHMouseUp = () => {
          hSplitter.classList.remove('dragging');
          document.body.style.cursor = '';
          document.body.style.userSelect = '';
          window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onHMouseMove);
          window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onHMouseUp);
          try {
            localStorage.setItem('cachywire_summary_height', parseInt(summaryStrip.style.height, 10));
          } catch (_) {}
        };

        window.addEventListener('mousemove', onHMouseMove, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('mouseup', onHMouseUp);
      });

      hSplitter.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
        summaryStrip.style.height = '74px';
        summaryStrip.style.minHeight = '74px';
        try {
          localStorage.removeItem('cachywire_summary_height');
        } catch (_) {}
        this._resizeCanvases();
        this.renderGraph();
        this.renderScrubber();
      });
    }

    // Center gauge opens Data Plan modal
    this.container.querySelector('#metric-center-gauge')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.onOpenDataPlan();
    });

    const badge = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-selected-badge');
    if (badge) {
      badge.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        this.selectedAppId = null;
        this.selectedProtoId = null;
        this.selectedHostId = null;
        this.container.querySelectorAll('.apps-table-row').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
        this.container.querySelector('#row-all-apps')?.classList.add('active');
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
        this.renderGraph();
        this.renderScrubber();
      });
    }

    // Toggle search row
    const btnSearch = this.container.querySelector('#btn-toggle-search');
    const searchRow = this.container.querySelector('#apps-search-row');
    const searchInput = this.container.querySelector('#apps-search-input');
    const searchClear = this.container.querySelector('#apps-search-clear');

    if (btnSearch && searchRow) {
      btnSearch.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isHidden = searchRow.style.display === 'none';
        searchRow.style.display = isHidden ? 'flex' : 'none';
        btnSearch.classList.toggle('active', isHidden);
        if (isHidden) {
          searchInput.focus();
        } else {
          searchInput.value = '';
          this.searchQuery = '';
          this.refreshAppsList(false);
        }
      });
    }

    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        this.searchQuery = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
      });
    }

    if (searchClear && searchInput) {
      searchClear.addEventListener('click', () => {
        searchInput.value = '';
        this.searchQuery = '';
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
        searchInput.focus();
      });
    }

    // Toggle sort order
    const btnSort = this.container.querySelector('#btn-toggle-sort');
    if (btnSort) {
      btnSort.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.sortOrder = this.sortOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
        this.refreshAppsList(false);
      });
    }

    // Scrubber drag handlers
    this._initScrubberInteractions();

    // Double-click on scrubber resets zoom to full 100% range
    this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-wrapper')?.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
      this.zoomStart = 0.0;
      this.zoomEnd = 1.0;
      this.updateScrubberWindowUI();
      this.renderGraph();
      this.refreshAppsList(false);
    });
  }

  _initCanvases() {
    this.canvas = this.container.querySelector('#traffic-canvas');
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');

    this.scrubberCanvas = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-canvas');
    this.scrubberCtx = this.scrubberCanvas.getContext('2d');

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      this._resizeCanvases();
      this.renderGraph();
      this.renderScrubber();
    });

    const graphContainer = this.container.querySelector('#graph-canvas-container');
    if (graphContainer) resizeObserver.observe(graphContainer);
    const scrubberTrack = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-track-area') || this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-wrapper');
    if (scrubberTrack) resizeObserver.observe(scrubberTrack);

    // Interactive Crosshair Hover Tooltip on Main Graph Canvas
    const tooltip = this.container.querySelector('#canvas-tooltip');
    this.canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      if (this.viewMode === 'geo' || this.lastBuckets.length === 0) {
        if (tooltip) tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }
      const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const rulerW = 72;
      const mouseX = e.clientX - rect.left;
      if (mouseX < rulerW) {
        this.hoverBucketIndex = null;
        if (tooltip) tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        this.renderGraph();
        return;
      }
      const relX = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (mouseX - rulerW) / Math.max(1, rect.width - rulerW)));
      const idx = Math.min(this.lastBuckets.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.round(relX * (this.lastBuckets.length - 1))));
      this.hoverBucketIndex = idx;
      this.renderGraph();

      const b = this.lastBuckets[idx];
      if (b && tooltip) {
        const dt = new Date(b.time);
        const timeStr = dt.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
        let appHeaderHtml = '';
        if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
          const selApp = this.appsRepo.getById(this.selectedAppId);
          if (selApp) {
            appHeaderHtml = `<div style="color:#00D4B2;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:3px;text-overflow:ellipsis;overflow:hidden;white-space:nowrap;">App: ${selApp.app_name}</div>`;
          }
        }
        tooltip.style.display = 'block';
        tooltip.innerHTML = `
          ${appHeaderHtml}
          <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15);padding-bottom:3px;">${timeStr}</div>
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;color:#00D4B2;"><span>↓ Inbound:</span><span class="mono-num">${formatBytes(b.down)}</span></div>
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;color:#FF9F43;"><span>↑ Outbound:</span><span class="mono-num">${formatBytes(b.up)}</span></div>
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;color:#94A3B8;margin-top:2px;font-size:10px;"><span>Total:</span><span class="mono-num">${formatBytes(b.down + b.up)}</span></div>
        `;
        const leftPx = Math.min(rect.width - 175, Math.max(10, e.clientX - rect.left + 12));
        const topPx = Math.min(rect.height - 110, Math.max(10, e.clientY - rect.top - 20));
        tooltip.style.left = `${leftPx}px`;
        tooltip.style.top = `${topPx}px`;
      }
    });

    this.canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
      this.hoverBucketIndex = null;
      if (tooltip) tooltip.style.display = 'none';
      this.renderGraph();
    });

    // Interactive Smooth Wheel Zoom In / Out on Graph Canvas
    this.canvas.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const relX = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - rect.left) / Math.max(1, rect.width)));

      const currentSpan = this.zoomEnd - this.zoomStart;
      const zoomFactor = e.deltaY < 0 ? 0.82 : 1.22;
      const newSpan = Math.max(0.015, Math.min(1.0, currentSpan * zoomFactor));

      const cursorRatio = this.zoomStart + relX * currentSpan;
      let newStart = cursorRatio - relX * newSpan;
      let newEnd = newStart + newSpan;

      if (newStart < 0) {
        newStart = 0;
        newEnd = Math.min(1.0, newSpan);
      }
      if (newEnd > 1.0) {
        newEnd = 1.0;
        newStart = Math.max(0, 1.0 - newSpan);
      }

      this.zoomStart = newStart;
      this.zoomEnd = newEnd;
      this.updateScrubberWindowUI();
      this.renderGraph();
      this.refreshAppsList(false);
      this.renderScrubber();
    }, { passive: false });

    // Double-click on chart canvas resets zoom back to 100% full view
    this.canvas.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
      this.zoomStart = 0.0;
      this.zoomEnd = 1.0;
      this.updateScrubberWindowUI();
      this.renderGraph();
      this.refreshAppsList(false);
      this.renderScrubber();
    });

    this._resizeCanvases();
  }

  _resizeCanvases() {
    if (this.canvas && this.canvas.parentElement) {
      this.canvas.width = this.canvas.parentElement.clientWidth || 800;
      this.canvas.height = this.canvas.parentElement.clientHeight || 340;
    }
    const trackArea = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-track-area') || (this.scrubberCanvas ? this.scrubberCanvas.parentElement : null);
    if (this.scrubberCanvas && trackArea) {
      this.scrubberCanvas.width = trackArea.clientWidth || 800;
      this.scrubberCanvas.height = 44;
    }
    this.updateScrubberWindowUI();
  }

  _getPeriodWindowMs() {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (this.selectedPeriod === '5m') return { startMs: now - 5 * 60 * 1000, endMs: now };
    if (this.selectedPeriod === '1h') return { startMs: now - 1 * 3600 * 1000, endMs: now };
    if (this.selectedPeriod === '3h') return { startMs: now - 3 * 3600 * 1000, endMs: now };
    if (this.selectedPeriod === '12h') return { startMs: now - 12 * 3600 * 1000, endMs: now };
    if (this.selectedPeriod === 'week') return { startMs: now - 7 * 86400 * 1000, endMs: now };
    if (this.selectedPeriod === 'month') return { startMs: now - 30 * 86400 * 1000, endMs: now };
    return { startMs: now - 24 * 3600 * 1000, endMs: now }; // default 24h
  }

  _getFilteredRecords() {
    const mapSize = this.db.tables.traffic_stats ? this.db.tables.traffic_stats.size : 0;
    const nowBucket = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const cacheKey = `${this.selectedPeriod}_${this.selectedAdapter}_${mapSize}_${this.isDraggingScrubber ? 'drag' : nowBucket}`;
    if (this._cachedFilteredRecords && this._cachedFilterKey === cacheKey) {
      return this._cachedFilteredRecords;
    }

    const { startMs, endMs } = this._getPeriodWindowMs();
    const results = [];
    if (this.db.tables.traffic_stats) {
      for (const r of this.db.tables.traffic_stats.values()) {
        const ts = r.timestamp || (r.bucket_ts * 1000);
        if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
        if (ts < startMs || ts > endMs + 5000) continue;
        if (this.selectedAdapter === 'wan' && r.is_local_lan !== 0) continue;
        if (this.selectedAdapter === 'lan' && r.is_local_lan !== 1) continue;
        results.push(r);
      }
    }

    this._cachedFilterKey = cacheKey;
    this._cachedFilteredRecords = results;
    return results;
  }

  _initScrubberInteractions() {
    const trackArea = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-track-area') || this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-wrapper');
    const win = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-window');
    const handleL = this.container.querySelector('#handle-left');
    const handleR = this.container.querySelector('#handle-right');

    if (!trackArea || !win || !handleL || !handleR) return;

    let dragType = null;
    let startX = 0;
    let initialZoomStart = 0;
    let initialZoomEnd = 0;

    const onMouseDown = (e, type) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      e.preventDefault();
      dragType = type;
      this.isDraggingScrubber = true;
      startX = e.clientX;
      initialZoomStart = this.zoomStart;
      initialZoomEnd = this.zoomEnd;
      document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove, { passive: true });
      document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
      window.addEventListener('blur', onMouseUp);
    };

    handleL.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => onMouseDown(e, 'left'));
    handleR.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => onMouseDown(e, 'right'));
    win.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => onMouseDown(e, 'window'));

    const onMouseMove = (e) => {
      if (!dragType) return;
      const rect = trackArea.getBoundingClientRect();
      const deltaX = (e.clientX - startX) / Math.max(1, rect.width);

      if (dragType === 'left') {
        this.zoomStart = Math.max(0, Math.min(initialZoomStart + deltaX, this.zoomEnd - 0.05));
      } else if (dragType === 'right') {
        this.zoomEnd = Math.min(1.0, Math.max(initialZoomEnd + deltaX, this.zoomStart + 0.05));
      } else if (dragType === 'window') {
        const windowSpan = Math.max(0.05, initialZoomEnd - initialZoomStart);
        let newStart = initialZoomStart + deltaX;
        let newEnd = newStart + windowSpan;
        if (newStart < 0) { newStart = 0; newEnd = windowSpan; }
        if (newEnd > 1.0) { newEnd = 1.0; newStart = Math.max(0, 1.0 - windowSpan); }
        this.zoomStart = newStart;
        this.zoomEnd = newEnd;
      }

      this.updateScrubberWindowUI();
      if (!this.scrubberRafPending) {
        this.scrubberRafPending = true;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          this.scrubberRafPending = false;
          this.renderGraph();
        });
      }
    };

    const onMouseUp = () => {
      if (!dragType) return;
      dragType = null;
      this.isDraggingScrubber = false;
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
      window.removeEventListener('blur', onMouseUp);
      this.renderGraph();
      this.refreshAppsList(false);
    };
  }

  updateScrubberWindowUI() {
    const win = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-window');
    if (!win) return;
    const leftPercent = (this.zoomStart * 100).toFixed(2);
    const widthPercent = ((this.zoomEnd - this.zoomStart) * 100).toFixed(2);
    win.style.left = `${leftPercent}%`;
    win.style.width = `${widthPercent}%`;
  }

  refreshAppsList(isLiveUpdate = false) {
    const listBody = this.container.querySelector('#apps-list-body');
    if (!listBody) return;

    const filteredStats = this._getFilteredRecords();

    let totalDown = 0;
    let totalUp = 0;
    let wanTotal = 0;
    let lanTotal = 0;

    const appStats = new Map();
    const protoStats = new Map();
    const hostStats = new Map();

    filteredStats.forEach(r => {
      const bIn = r.bytes_in || 0;
      const bOut = r.bytes_out || 0;
      const bTot = bIn + bOut;

      totalDown += bIn;
      totalUp += bOut;
      if (r.is_local_lan) lanTotal += bTot;
      else wanTotal += bTot;

      const aCurr = appStats.get(r.app_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      aCurr.down += bIn; aCurr.up += bOut; aCurr.total += bTot;
      appStats.set(r.app_id, aCurr);

      const pCurr = protoStats.get(r.proto_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      pCurr.down += bIn; pCurr.up += bOut; pCurr.total += bTot;
      protoStats.set(r.proto_id, pCurr);

      const hCurr = hostStats.get(r.host_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      hCurr.down += bIn; hCurr.up += bOut; hCurr.total += bTot;
      hostStats.set(r.host_id, hCurr);
    });

    // Calculate display metrics for the middle summary banner & arc gauge
    // If an app, protocol, or host is selected, display ONLY that item's metrics!
    let displayDown = totalDown;
    let displayUp = totalUp;
    let displayWan = wanTotal;
    let displayLan = lanTotal;

    if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
      const a = appStats.get(this.selectedAppId) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      displayDown = a.down;
      displayUp = a.up;
      let aWan = 0, aLan = 0;
      filteredStats.forEach(r => {
        if (r.app_id === this.selectedAppId) {
          const bTot = (r.bytes_in || 0) + (r.bytes_out || 0);
          if (r.is_local_lan) aLan += bTot;
          else aWan += bTot;
        }
      });
      displayWan = aWan;
      displayLan = aLan;
    } else if (this.selectedProtoId !== null) {
      const p = protoStats.get(this.selectedProtoId) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      displayDown = p.down;
      displayUp = p.up;
      let pWan = 0, pLan = 0;
      filteredStats.forEach(r => {
        if (r.proto_id === this.selectedProtoId) {
          const bTot = (r.bytes_in || 0) + (r.bytes_out || 0);
          if (r.is_local_lan) pLan += bTot;
          else pWan += bTot;
        }
      });
      displayWan = pWan;
      displayLan = pLan;
    } else if (this.selectedHostId !== null) {
      const h = hostStats.get(this.selectedHostId) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      displayDown = h.down;
      displayUp = h.up;
      displayWan = h.down + h.up;
      displayLan = 0;
    }

    // Update master row in sidebar
    const elTotalDown = this.container.querySelector('#total-down-bytes');
    const elTotalUp = this.container.querySelector('#total-up-bytes');
    if (elTotalDown) elTotalDown.textContent = formatBytes(totalDown);
    if (elTotalUp) elTotalUp.textContent = formatBytes(totalUp);

    const masterSpeedInEl = this.container.querySelector('#master-speed-in');
    const masterSpeedOutEl = this.container.querySelector('#master-speed-out');
    let totSpeedIn = 0, totSpeedOut = 0;
    if (this.appLiveRates && this.appLiveRates.size > 0) {
      for (const s of this.appLiveRates.values()) {
        totSpeedIn += s.in || 0;
        totSpeedOut += s.out || 0;
      }
    }
    if (totSpeedIn === 0 && this._lastSystemIn) totSpeedIn = this._lastSystemIn;
    if (totSpeedOut === 0 && this._lastSystemOut) totSpeedOut = this._lastSystemOut;
    if (masterSpeedInEl) masterSpeedInEl.textContent = formatSpeed(totSpeedIn);
    if (masterSpeedOutEl) masterSpeedOutEl.textContent = formatSpeed(totSpeedOut);

    const masterRow = this.container.querySelector('#row-all-apps');
    if (masterRow) {
      const isAllSelected = this.selectedAppId === null && this.selectedProtoId === null && this.selectedHostId === null;
      masterRow.classList.toggle('active', isAllSelected);
    }

    // Update gauge target badge in dedicated footer slot (stable position, no layout shifting)
    const gaugeTargetBadge = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-selected-badge');
    const badgeLabel = gaugeTargetBadge ? gaugeTargetBadge.querySelector('.badge-label') : null;
    const badgeClear = gaugeTargetBadge ? gaugeTargetBadge.querySelector('.badge-clear') : null;

    if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
      const selApp = this.appsRepo.getById(this.selectedAppId);
      const appName = selApp ? selApp.app_name : 'Selected App';
      if (gaugeTargetBadge) {
        gaugeTargetBadge.classList.add('filtering');
        if (badgeLabel) { badgeLabel.textContent = appName; badgeLabel.title = appName; }
        if (badgeClear) badgeClear.style.display = 'inline-block';
      }
    } else if (this.selectedProtoId !== null) {
      const selProto = this.db.getTrafficTypeById(this.selectedProtoId);
      const protoName = selProto ? selProto.service_name : 'Protocol';
      if (gaugeTargetBadge) {
        gaugeTargetBadge.classList.add('filtering');
        if (badgeLabel) { badgeLabel.textContent = protoName; badgeLabel.title = protoName; }
        if (badgeClear) badgeClear.style.display = 'inline-block';
      }
    } else if (this.selectedHostId !== null) {
      const selHost = this.hostsRepo.getById(this.selectedHostId);
      const hostName = selHost ? (selHost.host_name || selHost.ip_address) : 'Host';
      if (gaugeTargetBadge) {
        gaugeTargetBadge.classList.add('filtering');
        if (badgeLabel) { badgeLabel.textContent = hostName; badgeLabel.title = hostName; }
        if (badgeClear) badgeClear.style.display = 'inline-block';
      }
    } else {
      if (gaugeTargetBadge) {
        gaugeTargetBadge.classList.remove('filtering');
        if (badgeLabel) { badgeLabel.textContent = 'TOTAL TRAFFIC'; badgeLabel.title = 'Total Traffic'; }
        if (badgeClear) badgeClear.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }

    // Update summary banner & semi-oval gauge with the filtered values
    const bDownTot = this.container.querySelector('#banner-down-total');
    const bUpTot = this.container.querySelector('#banner-up-total');
    const bGauge = this.container.querySelector('#banner-gauge-total');
    const bWan = this.container.querySelector('#banner-wan-val');
    const bLan = this.container.querySelector('#banner-lan-val');
    const bWanBar = this.container.querySelector('#banner-wan-bar');

    if (bDownTot) bDownTot.textContent = formatBytes(displayDown);
    if (bUpTot) bUpTot.textContent = formatBytes(displayUp);
    if (bGauge) bGauge.textContent = formatBytes(displayDown + displayUp);
    if (bWan) bWan.textContent = formatBytes(displayWan);
    if (bLan) bLan.textContent = formatBytes(displayLan);
    if (bWanBar) {
      const wanPct = (displayWan + displayLan) > 0 ? Math.max(5, Math.min(100, Math.round((displayWan / (displayWan + displayLan)) * 100))) : 98;
      bWanBar.style.width = `${wanPct}%`;
    }

    // Dynamically update semi-oval arc & dual split bar proportions
    const combinedBytes = displayDown + displayUp;
    const downRatio = combinedBytes > 0 ? Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.96, displayDown / combinedBytes)) : 0.5;
    const downPct = Math.round(downRatio * 100);
    const upPct = 100 - downPct;

    const elArcDown = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-arc-down');
    const elArcUp = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-arc-up');
    const elPctDown = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-pct-down');
    const elPctUp = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-pct-up');
    const elBarDown = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-bar-down');
    const elBarUp = this.container.querySelector('#gauge-bar-up');

    if (elArcDown) {
      elArcDown.setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', `${downPct} 100`);
      elArcDown.setAttribute('stroke-dashoffset', '0');
    }
    if (elArcUp) {
      elArcUp.setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', `${upPct} 100`);
      elArcUp.setAttribute('stroke-dashoffset', `-${downPct}`);
    }
    if (elPctDown) elPctDown.textContent = `↓ ${downPct}%`;
    if (elPctUp) elPctUp.textContent = `${upPct}% ↑`;
    if (elBarDown) elBarDown.style.width = `${downPct}%`;
    if (elBarUp) elBarUp.style.width = `${upPct}%`;

    // When doing live 1Hz update, update existing rows in-place to PREVENT jitter & jumping!
    if (isLiveUpdate && listBody.children.length > 0) {
      if (this.sidebarType === 'apps') {
        const apps = this.appsRepo.getAll();
        apps.forEach(app => {
          const stat = appStats.get(app.app_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
          const row = listBody.querySelector(`[data-app-id="${app.app_id}"]`);
          const rateInfo = this.appLiveRates ? this.appLiveRates.get(app.app_id) : null;
          const curIn = rateInfo ? rateInfo.in : 0;
          const curOut = rateInfo ? rateInfo.out : 0;
          const hasLive = (curIn > 0 || curOut > 0);

          if (row) {
            const speedEl = row.querySelector('.col-speed');
            const downEl = row.querySelector('.col-down');
            const upEl = row.querySelector('.col-up');
            if (speedEl) {
              speedEl.className = `col-speed mono-num ${hasLive ? 'live' : ''}`;
              speedEl.innerHTML = `
                <span class="speed-sub-row speed-out"><span style="color:#FF9F43;font-size:9px;margin-right:2px;">↑</span>${formatSpeed(curOut)}</span>
                <span class="speed-sub-row speed-in"><span style="color:#00D4B2;font-size:9px;margin-right:2px;">↓</span>${formatSpeed(curIn)}</span>
              `;
            }
            if (downEl) downEl.textContent = formatBytes(stat.down);
            if (upEl) upEl.textContent = formatBytes(stat.up);
          } else if (stat.total > 0 || hasLive) {
            const newRow = document.createElement('div');
            newRow.className = `apps-table-row ${this.selectedAppId === app.app_id ? 'active' : ''}`;
            newRow.setAttribute('data-app-id', String(app.app_id));
            const iconSvg = getAppIconSvg(app.app_name, app.exe_path);
            newRow.innerHTML = `
              <div class="app-info-cell">
                <div class="app-vector-icon">${iconSvg}</div>
                <span class="app-label" title="Click to filter chart | Double-click for details: ${app.exe_path || ''}">${app.app_name}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="col-speed mono-num ${hasLive ? 'live' : ''}">
                <span class="speed-sub-row speed-out"><span style="color:#FF9F43;font-size:9px;margin-right:2px;">↑</span>${formatSpeed(curOut)}</span>
                <span class="speed-sub-row speed-in"><span style="color:#00D4B2;font-size:9px;margin-right:2px;">↓</span>${formatSpeed(curIn)}</span>
              </div>
              <span class="col-down mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.down)}</span>
              <span class="col-up mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.up)}</span>
            `;
