            newRow.addEventListener('click', () => {
              this.selectedAppId = (this.selectedAppId === app.app_id) ? null : app.app_id;
              this.refreshAppsList(false);
              this.renderGraph();
              this.renderScrubber();
            });
            newRow.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              this.onAppClick(app);
            });
            listBody.appendChild(newRow);
          }
        });
        return;
      }
    }

    listBody.innerHTML = '';

    if (this.sidebarType === 'apps') {
      let apps = this.appsRepo.getAll();
      if (window.gwApp && window.gwApp.settings && window.gwApp.settings.hideIdleApps) {
        apps = apps.filter(a => (appStats.get(a.app_id)?.total || 0) > 0);
      }
      apps.sort((a, b) => {
        const aBytes = appStats.get(a.app_id)?.total || 0;
        const bBytes = appStats.get(b.app_id)?.total || 0;
        return this.sortOrder === 'desc' ? bBytes - aBytes : aBytes - bBytes;
      });
      if (this.searchQuery) {
        apps = apps.filter(a => a.app_name.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery));
      }

      apps.forEach(app => {
        const stat = appStats.get(app.app_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = `apps-table-row ${this.selectedAppId === app.app_id ? 'active' : ''}`;
        row.setAttribute('data-app-id', String(app.app_id));
        const iconSvg = getAppIconSvg(app.app_name, app.exe_path);

        const rateInfo = this.appLiveRates ? this.appLiveRates.get(app.app_id) : null;
        const curIn = rateInfo ? rateInfo.in : 0;
        const curOut = rateInfo ? rateInfo.out : 0;
        const hasLive = (curIn > 0 || curOut > 0);

        row.innerHTML = `
          <div class="app-info-cell">
            <div class="app-vector-icon">${iconSvg}</div>
            <span class="app-label" title="Click to filter chart | Double-click for details: ${app.exe_path || ''}">${app.app_name}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="col-speed mono-num ${hasLive ? 'live' : ''}">
            <span class="speed-sub-row speed-out"><span style="color:#FF9F43;font-size:9px;margin-right:2px;">↑</span>${formatSpeed(curOut)}</span>
            <span class="speed-sub-row speed-in"><span style="color:#00D4B2;font-size:9px;margin-right:2px;">↓</span>${formatSpeed(curIn)}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="col-down mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.down)}</span>
          <span class="col-up mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.up)}</span>
        `;

        row.addEventListener('click', () => {
          this.selectedAppId = (this.selectedAppId === app.app_id) ? null : app.app_id;
          this.refreshAppsList(false);
          this.renderGraph();
          this.renderScrubber();
        });

        row.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          this.onAppClick(app);
        });

        listBody.appendChild(row);
      });
    } else if (this.sidebarType === 'traffic') {
      let protos = this.db.getAllTrafficTypes();
      protos.sort((a, b) => {
        const aBytes = protoStats.get(a.proto_id)?.total || 0;
        const bBytes = protoStats.get(b.proto_id)?.total || 0;
        return this.sortOrder === 'desc' ? bBytes - aBytes : aBytes - bBytes;
      });
      if (this.searchQuery) {
        protos = protos.filter(p => p.service_name.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery));
      }

      protos.forEach(proto => {
        const stat = protoStats.get(proto.proto_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = `apps-table-row ${this.selectedProtoId === proto.proto_id ? 'active' : ''}`;
        row.innerHTML = `
          <div class="app-info-cell">
            <span style="font-weight:700;color:var(--gw-accent);width:20px;text-align:center;">#</span>
            <span class="app-label">${proto.service_name}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="col-speed mono-num">-</div>
          <span class="col-down mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.down)}</span>
          <span class="col-up mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.up)}</span>
        `;
        row.addEventListener('click', () => {
          this.selectedProtoId = (this.selectedProtoId === proto.proto_id) ? null : proto.proto_id;
          this.refreshAppsList(false);
          this.renderGraph();
          this.renderScrubber();
        });
        listBody.appendChild(row);
      });
    } else if (this.sidebarType === 'hosts') {
      let hosts = this.hostsRepo.getAll();
      hosts.sort((a, b) => {
        const aBytes = hostStats.get(a.host_id)?.total || 0;
        const bBytes = hostStats.get(b.host_id)?.total || 0;
        return this.sortOrder === 'desc' ? bBytes - aBytes : aBytes - bBytes;
      });
      if (this.searchQuery) {
        hosts = hosts.filter(h =>
          (h.domain_name && h.domain_name.toLowerCase().includes(this.searchQuery)) ||
          h.ip_address.includes(this.searchQuery)
        );
      }

      hosts.forEach(host => {
        const stat = hostStats.get(host.host_id) || { down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
        const geo = lookupGeoIp(host.ip_address);
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = `apps-table-row ${this.selectedHostId === host.host_id ? 'active' : ''}`;
        row.innerHTML = `
          <div class="app-info-cell">
            <span class="host-flag-icon">${geo.flag}</span>
            <span class="app-label" title="${host.ip_address}">${host.domain_name || host.ip_address}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="col-speed mono-num">-</div>
          <span class="col-down mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.down)}</span>
          <span class="col-up mono-num">${formatBytes(stat.up)}</span>
        `;
        row.addEventListener('click', () => {
          this.selectedHostId = (this.selectedHostId === host.host_id) ? null : host.host_id;
          this.refreshAppsList(false);
          this.renderGraph();
          this.renderScrubber();
        });
        row.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          this.onHostClick(host);
        });
        listBody.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
  }

  renderGraph() {
    if (!this.canvas || !this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const w = this.canvas.width;
    const h = this.canvas.height;
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    let records = this._getFilteredRecords();
    if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
      records = records.filter(r => r.app_id === this.selectedAppId);
    }
    if (this.selectedProtoId !== null) {
      records = records.filter(r => r.proto_id === this.selectedProtoId);
    }
    if (this.selectedHostId !== null) {
      records = records.filter(r => r.host_id === this.selectedHostId);
    }

    // If Geo Mode is active, render Geographic Country Breakdown Canvas
    if (this.viewMode === 'geo') {
      this._renderGeoView(ctx, w, h, records);
      return;
    }

    records.sort((a, b) => (a.timestamp || a.bucket_ts * 1000) - (b.timestamp || b.bucket_ts * 1000));

    const { startMs: periodStart, endMs: periodEnd } = this._getPeriodWindowMs();
    const minTime = records.length > 0 ? Math.min(periodStart, records[0].timestamp || (records[0].bucket_ts * 1000)) : periodStart;
    const maxTime = records.length > 0 ? Math.max(periodEnd, records[records.length - 1].timestamp || (records[records.length - 1].bucket_ts * 1000)) : periodEnd;
    const totalTimeSpan = Math.max(maxTime - minTime, 60000);

    const visibleStart = minTime + (totalTimeSpan * this.zoomStart);
    const visibleEnd = minTime + (totalTimeSpan * this.zoomEnd);
    const visibleSpan = Math.max(visibleEnd - visibleStart, 10000);

    const visibleRecords = records.filter(r => {
      const ts = r.timestamp || (r.bucket_ts * 1000);
      return ts >= visibleStart && ts <= visibleEnd;
    });

    const numBuckets = 60;
    const bucketDuration = Math.max(1, visibleSpan / numBuckets);
    const buckets = new Array(numBuckets);
    for (let i = 0; i < numBuckets; i++) {
      const bStart = visibleStart + (i * bucketDuration);
      buckets[i] = { time: bStart + (bucketDuration / 2), down: 0, up: 0 };
    }

    for (let j = 0; j < visibleRecords.length; j++) {
      const r = visibleRecords[j];
      const ts = r.timestamp || (r.bucket_ts * 1000);
      const idx = Math.min(numBuckets - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor((ts - visibleStart) / bucketDuration)));
      buckets[idx].down += (r.bytes_in || 0);
      buckets[idx].up += (r.bytes_out || 0);
    }

    this.lastBuckets = buckets;

    let maxBytes = 64 * 1024; // 64 KB minimum scale for crisp live sensitivity
    buckets.forEach(b => {
      if (b.down > maxBytes) maxBytes = b.down;
      if (b.up > maxBytes) maxBytes = b.up;
    });
    maxBytes = maxBytes * 1.25;

    const rulerW = 72;
    const bucketDurationSec = Math.max(0.1, bucketDuration / 1000);
    const peakRate = maxBytes / bucketDurationSec;

    const labelScale = this.container.querySelector('#label-max-scale');
    if (labelScale) labelScale.textContent = formatSpeed(peakRate);

    // 1. Draw subtle horizontal & vertical time gridlines
    const numGridLines = 5;
    const isDarkTheme = !document.body.classList.contains('theme-light');
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.font = '600 11px "JetBrains Mono", monospace';

    // Vertical time grid lines
    for (let g = 0; g <= numGridLines; g++) {
      const gx = rulerW + ((w - rulerW) / numGridLines) * g;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(gx, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(gx, h - 26);
      ctx.strokeStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.14)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22)';
      ctx.stroke();

      const gTime = new Date(visibleStart + (visibleSpan / numGridLines) * g);
      const timeStr = gTime.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
      ctx.fillStyle = isDarkTheme ? '#CBD5E1' : '#475569';
      ctx.fillText(`${timeStr}`, gx + 8, h - 9);
    }

    // 2. Speed Ruler on the Left Margin
    ctx.fillStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.88)' : 'rgba(241, 245, 249, 0.92)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, rulerW, h - 26);

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(rulerW, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(rulerW, h - 26);
    ctx.strokeStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.32)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.42)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
    ctx.stroke();

    const speedTicks = [
      { ratio: 1.0, y: 16 },
      { ratio: 0.75, y: 16 + (h - 42) * 0.25 },
      { ratio: 0.50, y: 16 + (h - 42) * 0.50 },
      { ratio: 0.25, y: 16 + (h - 42) * 0.75 },
      { ratio: 0.0, y: h - 26 }
    ];

    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.font = '600 10px "JetBrains Mono", monospace';

    for (const t of speedTicks) {
      const val = peakRate * t.ratio;
      const label = t.ratio === 0 ? '0 B/s' : formatSpeed(val);

      // Horizontal grid line extending across the chart
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rulerW, t.y);
      ctx.lineTo(w, t.y);
      ctx.strokeStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.15)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();

      // Tick mark on the ruler
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rulerW - 6, t.y);
      ctx.lineTo(rulerW, t.y);
      ctx.strokeStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.45)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.6)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
      ctx.stroke();

      // Speed unit text
      ctx.fillStyle = t.ratio === 1.0 ? '#00D4B2' : (isDarkTheme ? '#94A3B8' : '#475569');
      ctx.fillText(label, rulerW - 8, t.y);
    }

    // Minor tick marks
    const minorRatios = [0.875, 0.625, 0.375, 0.125];
    for (const mr of minorRatios) {
      const my = 16 + (h - 42) * (1 - mr);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rulerW - 3, my);
      ctx.lineTo(rulerW, my);
      ctx.strokeStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.35)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';

    if (this.viewMode === 'bar') {
      const groupW = (w - rulerW) / buckets.length;
      const barW = Math.max(2, (groupW - 4) / 2);
      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {
        const gx = rulerW + i * groupW + 2;
        const downH = (buckets[i].down / maxBytes) * (h - 42);
        const upH = (buckets[i].up / maxBytes) * (h - 42);

        ctx.fillStyle = '#00D4B2';
        ctx.fillRect(gx, (h - 26) - downH, barW, downH);

        ctx.fillStyle = '#FF9F43';
        ctx.fillRect(gx + barW + 1, (h - 26) - upH, barW, upH);
      }
    } else {
      // Authentic CachyWire Wave Area Curve Mode (Turquoise-Teal Download + Warm Amber Upload)
      const downGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h - 26);
      downGradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0, 212, 178, 0.54)');
      downGradient.addColorStop(0.65, 'rgba(0, 212, 178, 0.18)');
      downGradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 212, 178, 0.01)');

      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rulerW, h - 26);

      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {
        const bx = rulerW + (i / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
        const by = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i].down / maxBytes) * (h - 42));

        if (i === 0) {
          ctx.lineTo(bx, by);
        } else {
          const prevX = rulerW + ((i - 1) / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
          const prevY = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i - 1].down / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
          const cpx = (prevX + bx) / 2;
          ctx.bezierCurveTo(cpx, prevY, cpx, by, bx, by);
        }
      }

      ctx.lineTo(w, h - 26);
      ctx.lineTo(rulerW, h - 26);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle = downGradient;
      ctx.fill();

      // Signature Turquoise-Teal top stroke for Download
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {
        const bx = rulerW + (i / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
        const by = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i].down / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
        if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(bx, by);
        else {
          const prevX = rulerW + ((i - 1) / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
          const prevY = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i - 1].down / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
          const cpx = (prevX + bx) / 2;
          ctx.bezierCurveTo(cpx, prevY, cpx, by, bx, by);
        }
      }
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#00D4B2';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.3;
      ctx.stroke();

      // Warm Amber-Orange Area + Stroke for Upload
      const upGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h - 26);
      upGradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 159, 67, 0.50)');
      upGradient.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(255, 159, 67, 0.15)');
      upGradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 159, 67, 0.01)');

      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rulerW, h - 26);

      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {
        const bx = rulerW + (i / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
        const by = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i].up / maxBytes) * (h - 42));

        if (i === 0) {
          ctx.lineTo(bx, by);
        } else {
          const prevX = rulerW + ((i - 1) / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
          const prevY = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i - 1].up / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
          const cpx = (prevX + bx) / 2;
          ctx.bezierCurveTo(cpx, prevY, cpx, by, bx, by);
        }
      }

      ctx.lineTo(w, h - 26);
      ctx.lineTo(rulerW, h - 26);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle = upGradient;
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {
        const bx = rulerW + (i / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
        const by = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i].up / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
        if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(bx, by);
        else {
          const prevX = rulerW + ((i - 1) / (buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
          const prevY = (h - 26) - ((buckets[i - 1].up / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
          const cpx = (prevX + bx) / 2;
          ctx.bezierCurveTo(cpx, prevY, cpx, by, bx, by);
        }
      }
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#FF9F43';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    // Draw interactive crosshair when hovering
    if (this.hoverBucketIndex !== null && buckets[this.hoverBucketIndex]) {
      const idx = this.hoverBucketIndex;
      const cx = rulerW + (idx / Math.max(1, buckets.length - 1)) * (w - rulerW);
      const downY = (h - 26) - ((buckets[idx].down / maxBytes) * (h - 42));
      const upY = (h - 26) - ((buckets[idx].up / maxBytes) * (h - 42));

      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#00D4B2';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
      ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(cx, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(cx, h - 26);
      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#00D4B2';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, downY, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.fillStyle = '#FF9F43';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, upY, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  _renderGeoView(ctx, w, h, records) {
    const isDarkTheme = !document.body.classList.contains('theme-light');
    const countryTotals = new Map();
    records.forEach(r => {
      const host = this.hostsRepo.getById(r.host_id);
      const geo = lookupGeoIp(host ? host.ip_address : '142.250.185.14');
      const key = geo.country;
      const curr = countryTotals.get(key) || { flag: geo.flag, iso2: geo.iso2, down: 0, up: 0, total: 0 };
      curr.down += (r.bytes_in || 0);
      curr.up += (r.bytes_out || 0);
      curr.total += ((r.bytes_in || 0) + (r.bytes_out || 0));
      countryTotals.set(key, curr);
    });

    const sorted = Array.from(countryTotals.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1].total - a[1].total);
    const maxTotal = sorted.length > 0 ? Math.max(1, sorted[0][1].total) : 1;

    ctx.fillStyle = isDarkTheme ? '#94A3B8' : '#64748B';
    ctx.font = '700 13px "Plus Jakarta Sans", sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('Global Network Traffic Distribution by Country (GeoIP)', 24, 32);

    let y = 62;
    for (const [country, data] of sorted.slice(0, 7)) {
      const barMaxW = Math.max(120, w - 340);
      const barW = Math.max(8, (data.total / maxTotal) * barMaxW);

      ctx.font = '600 13px "Plus Jakarta Sans", sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = isDarkTheme ? '#F8FAFC' : '#1E293B';
      ctx.fillText(`${data.flag}  ${country} (${data.iso2})`, 24, y + 15);

      ctx.fillStyle = isDarkTheme ? 'rgba(148,163,184,0.12)' : '#E2E8F0';
      ctx.fillRect(220, y + 2, barMaxW, 18);

      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(220, 0, 220 + barW, 0);
      grad.addColorStop(0, '#00D4B2');
      grad.addColorStop(1, '#FF9F43');
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      ctx.fillRect(220, y + 2, barW, 18);

      ctx.fillStyle = isDarkTheme ? '#00D4B2' : '#00A88F';
      ctx.font = '700 12px "JetBrains Mono", monospace';
      ctx.fillText(formatBytes(data.total), 230 + barMaxW, y + 15);

      y += 36;
    }
  }

  renderScrubber() {
    if (!this.scrubberCanvas || !this.scrubberCtx) return;
    const ctx = this.scrubberCtx;
    const w = this.scrubberCanvas.width;
    const h = this.scrubberCanvas.height;
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Update scrubber time ticks dynamically
    const ticksEl = this.container.querySelector('#scrubber-ticks');
    if (ticksEl) {
      const { startMs, endMs } = this._getPeriodWindowMs();
      const span = endMs - startMs;
      const labels = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const dt = new Date(startMs + (span * (i / 9)));
        labels.push(`<span>${dt.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}</span>`);
      }
      ticksEl.innerHTML = labels.join('');
    }

    let records = this._getFilteredRecords();
    if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
      records = records.filter(r => r.app_id === this.selectedAppId);
    }
    if (this.selectedProtoId !== null) {
      records = records.filter(r => r.proto_id === this.selectedProtoId);
    }
    if (this.selectedHostId !== null) {
      records = records.filter(r => r.host_id === this.selectedHostId);
    }
    records.sort((a, b) => (a.timestamp || a.bucket_ts * 1000) - (b.timestamp || b.bucket_ts * 1000));
    if (records.length === 0) return;

    const numPoints = 72;
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(records.length / numPoints));
    let maxVal = 64 * 1024;
    for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i += step) {
      const v = (records[i].bytes_in || 0) + (records[i].bytes_out || 0);
      if (v > maxVal) maxVal = v;
    }

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, h);

    for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i += step) {
      const x = (i / Math.max(1, records.length - 1)) * w;
      const y = h - (((records[i].bytes_in || 0) + (records[i].bytes_out || 0)) / maxVal * (h - 6));
      ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }

    ctx.lineTo(w, h);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 212, 178, 0.24)';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#00D4B2';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  pushRealtimeSample(sample) {
    if (sample && sample.appRates) {
      this.appLiveRates = sample.appRates;
    }
    this._lastSystemIn = sample ? (sample.bytes_in || 0) : 0;
    this._lastSystemOut = sample ? (sample.bytes_out || 0) : 0;

    const downSpeedEl = this.container.querySelector('#banner-down-speed');
    const upSpeedEl = this.container.querySelector('#banner-up-speed');

    let liveDown = sample ? (sample.bytes_in || 0) : 0;
    let liveUp = sample ? (sample.bytes_out || 0) : 0;

    if (this.selectedAppId !== null && this.appLiveRates) {
      const aRate = this.appLiveRates.get(this.selectedAppId);
      liveDown = aRate ? (aRate.in || 0) : 0;
      liveUp = aRate ? (aRate.out || 0) : 0;
    }

    if (downSpeedEl) {
      downSpeedEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #00D4B2;">↓</span> ${formatSpeed(liveDown)}`;
    }
    if (upSpeedEl) {
      upSpeedEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #FF9F43;">↑</span> ${formatSpeed(liveUp)}`;
    }

    // Do not interrupt active timeline scrubber dragging
    if (this.isDraggingScrubber) return;

    this.refreshAppsList(true);
    this.renderGraph();
    this.renderScrubber();
  }

  destroy() {
    if (this.animFrameId) cancelAnimationFrame(this.animFrameId);
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/traffic-monitor.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/presentation/usage-view.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Usage & Hardware Resources View (SCR-03)
 * Implements 3-column synchronized cross-filtering breakdown,
 * Total Bandwidth banner with download/upload gauge, CachyOS live hardware telemetry, and CSV Export.
 */

import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters.js';
import { getAppIconSvg } from '../utils/app-icons.js';
import { lookupGeoIp } from '../utils/geoip.js';

export class UsageView {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.container = options.container || document.getElementById('view-usage');
    this.db = options.db;
    this.appsRepo = options.appsRepo;
    this.hostsRepo = options.hostsRepo;
    this.trafficRepo = options.trafficRepo;
    this.exportService = options.exportService;
    this.onAppDoubleClick = options.onAppDoubleClick || (() => {});
    this.onHostDoubleClick = options.onHostDoubleClick || (() => {});
    this.onOpenDataPlan = options.onOpenDataPlan || (() => {});

    this.selectedRange = 'Month'; // 'Today' | 'Week' | 'Month'
    this.trafficFilter = 'ALL';   // 'ALL' | 'EXTERNAL' | 'LOCAL'
    this.selectedAppId = null;    // For cross-filtering drill-down
    this.selectedCountryIso2 = null; // Filter hosts by country if selected
    this.selectedCountryName = null;
  }

  mount() {
    this.container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="usage-banner">
        <div class="banner-left">
          <div class="total-stat-box">
            <span class="total-stat-label">Total Bandwidth</span>
            <span class="total-stat-value mono-num" id="usage-total-stat">0 B</span>
          </div>

          <div class="usage-split-visual">
            <div class="split-labels">
              <span class="down"><span style="color:var(--gw-inbound);">↓</span> <span id="usage-down-stat">0 B</span></span>
              <span class="up"><span style="color:var(--gw-outbound);">↑</span> <span id="usage-up-stat">0 B</span></span>
            </div>
            <div class="split-bar-track">
              <div class="split-bar-inbound" id="usage-split-in" style="width: 50%;"></div>
              <div class="split-bar-outbound" id="usage-split-out" style="width: 50%;"></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- CachyOS Real-Time Hardware Telemetry Strip -->
          <div class="hw-telemetry-pills" id="hw-telemetry-pills" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-left:12px;">
            <div class="status-pill" title="Operating System & Kernel">
              <span>🐧</span>
              <span id="hw-os-kernel">CachyOS Linux</span>
            </div>
            <div class="status-pill" title="Live CPU Utilization">
              <span>⚡ CPU:</span>
              <span class="mono-num" id="hw-cpu-load">12%</span>
            </div>
            <div class="status-pill" title="Live Memory Utilization">
              <span>🧠 RAM:</span>
              <span class="mono-num" id="hw-ram-load">--</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="banner-right">
          <select class="gw-select" id="select-usage-range">
            <option value="Today">Today</option>
            <option value="Week">This Week</option>
            <option value="Month" selected>This Month</option>
          </select>

          <select class="gw-select" id="select-usage-type">
            <option value="ALL">All Traffic</option>
            <option value="EXTERNAL">External / Internet Only</option>
            <option value="LOCAL">Local / LAN Only</option>
          </select>

          <button class="pill-btn" id="btn-export-csv" title="Export Usage Data to CSV (FUNC-02)">
            Export CSV
          </button>

          <button class="pill-btn active" id="btn-data-plan-modal" title="Configure Bandwidth Overuse & Data Plan (BR-06)">
            Data Plan
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="usage-columns-container" id="usage-columns-container">
        <!-- 1. Applications Column -->
        <div class="usage-column" id="usage-col-apps">
          <div class="usage-column-header">
            <span>Applications (Click to Filter)</span>
            <span class="mono-num" id="col-apps-total">0 B</span>
          </div>
          <div class="usage-column-list" id="col-apps-list"></div>
        </div>

        <!-- Splitter 1 -->
        <div class="usage-col-splitter" title="Drag to resize columns, double-click to reset"></div>

        <!-- 2. Countries Column (الدول) -->
        <div class="usage-column" id="usage-col-countries">
          <div class="usage-column-header">
            <span id="col-countries-title">Countries (الدول)</span>
            <span class="mono-num" id="col-countries-total">0 B</span>
          </div>
          <div class="usage-column-list" id="col-countries-list"></div>
        </div>

        <!-- Splitter 2 -->
        <div class="usage-col-splitter" title="Drag to resize columns, double-click to reset"></div>

        <!-- 3. Remote Hosts Column -->
        <div class="usage-column" id="usage-col-hosts">
          <div class="usage-column-header">
            <span id="col-hosts-title">Remote Hosts</span>
            <span class="mono-num" id="col-hosts-total">0 B</span>
          </div>
          <div class="usage-column-list" id="col-hosts-list"></div>
        </div>

        <!-- Splitter 3 -->
        <div class="usage-col-splitter" title="Drag to resize columns, double-click to reset"></div>

        <!-- 4. Traffic Types Column -->
        <div class="usage-column" id="usage-col-proto">
          <div class="usage-column-header">
            <span>Traffic Types</span>
            <span class="mono-num" id="col-proto-total">0 B</span>
          </div>
          <div class="usage-column-list" id="col-proto-list"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    this._bindEvents();
    this._initColumnSplitters();
    this.refresh();
  }

  _bindEvents() {
    const selRange = this.container.querySelector('#select-usage-range');
    selRange.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      this.selectedRange = e.target.value;
      this.refresh();
    });

    const selType = this.container.querySelector('#select-usage-type');
    selType.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      this.trafficFilter = e.target.value;
      this.refresh();
    });

    const btnExport = this.container.querySelector('#btn-export-csv');
    btnExport.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const { startSec, endSec } = this._getRangeSeconds();
      const csv = this.exportService.generateUsageCsv(startSec, endSec, {
        isLocalLan: this.trafficFilter === 'LOCAL' ? 1 : this.trafficFilter === 'EXTERNAL' ? 0 : null
      });
      this.exportService.downloadCsv(csv, `CachyWire_Usage_${this.selectedRange}.csv`);
    });

    const btnDataPlan = this.container.querySelector('#btn-data-plan-modal');
    btnDataPlan.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.onOpenDataPlan();
    });
  }

  _initColumnSplitters() {
    const container = this.container.querySelector('#usage-columns-container');
    if (!container) return;
    const splitters = container.querySelectorAll('.usage-col-splitter');
    const columns = container.querySelectorAll('.usage-column');

    // Restore saved widths if any
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('cachywire_usage_col_widths');
      if (saved) {
        const widths = JSON.parse(saved);
        if (Array.isArray(widths) && widths.length === columns.length) {
          columns.forEach((col, idx) => {
            if (widths[idx] >= 110) {
              col.style.flex = 'none';
              col.style.width = `${widths[idx]}px`;
            }
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    splitters.forEach((splitter, index) => {
      splitter.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const leftCol = columns[index];
        const rightCol = columns[index + 1];
        if (!leftCol || !rightCol) return;

        const startX = e.clientX;
        const startLeftW = leftCol.getBoundingClientRect().width;
        const startRightW = rightCol.getBoundingClientRect().width;

        splitter.classList.add('dragging');
        document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
        document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';

        const onMouseMove = (moveEvt) => {
          const deltaX = moveEvt.clientX - startX;
          const minW = 120;
          let newLeftW = startLeftW + deltaX;
          let newRightW = startRightW - deltaX;

          if (newLeftW < minW) {
            newLeftW = minW;
            newRightW = startLeftW + startRightW - minW;
          } else if (newRightW < minW) {
            newRightW = minW;
            newLeftW = startLeftW + startRightW - minW;
          }

          leftCol.style.flex = 'none';
          leftCol.style.width = `${newLeftW}px`;
          rightCol.style.flex = 'none';
          rightCol.style.width = `${newRightW}px`;
        };

        const onMouseUp = () => {
          splitter.classList.remove('dragging');
          document.body.style.cursor = '';
          document.body.style.userSelect = '';
          window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
          window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);

          try {
            const widths = Array.from(columns).map(col => Math.round(col.getBoundingClientRect().width));
            localStorage.setItem('cachywire_usage_col_widths', JSON.stringify(widths));
          } catch (_) {}
        };

        window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
      });

      // Double-click splitter to reset all columns to equal width
      splitter.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
        columns.forEach(col => {
          col.style.flex = '1 1 0';
          col.style.width = '';
        });
        try {
          localStorage.removeItem('cachywire_usage_col_widths');
        } catch (_) {}
      });
    });
  }

  updateHardwareTelemetry(sysStatus) {
    if (!sysStatus) return;
    const osEl = this.container.querySelector('#hw-os-kernel');
    const cpuEl = this.container.querySelector('#hw-cpu-load');
    const ramEl = this.container.querySelector('#hw-ram-load');
    if (osEl) osEl.textContent = `${sysStatus.osName || 'CachyOS'} (${sysStatus.kernel || ''})`;
    if (cpuEl) cpuEl.textContent = `${sysStatus.cpuPercent || 0}%`;
    if (ramEl) {
      ramEl.textContent = `${formatBytes(sysStatus.memUsedBytes || 0)} / ${formatBytes(sysStatus.memTotalBytes || 0)} (${sysStatus.memPercent || 0}%)`;
    }
  }

  _getRangeSeconds() {
    const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    let startSec = nowSec - 86400;

    if (this.selectedRange === 'Week') {
      startSec = nowSec - (7 * 86400);
    } else if (this.selectedRange === 'Month') {
      startSec = nowSec - (30 * 86400);
    }
    return { startSec, endSec: nowSec };
  }

  refresh() {
    const { startSec, endSec } = this._getRangeSeconds();
    const isLocalLan = this.trafficFilter === 'LOCAL' ? 1 : this.trafficFilter === 'EXTERNAL' ? 0 : null;

    const globalSummary = this.trafficRepo.getTotalsForRange(startSec, endSec, { isLocalLan });

    const statTotal = this.container.querySelector('#usage-total-stat');
    const statDown = this.container.querySelector('#usage-down-stat');
    const statUp = this.container.querySelector('#usage-up-stat');
    const splitIn = this.container.querySelector('#usage-split-in');
    const splitOut = this.container.querySelector('#usage-split-out');

    statTotal.textContent = formatBytes(globalSummary.totalBytes);
    statDown.textContent = formatBytes(globalSummary.totalIn);
    statUp.textContent = formatBytes(globalSummary.totalOut);

    const downPercent = globalSummary.totalBytes > 0 ? (globalSummary.totalIn / globalSummary.totalBytes) * 100 : 50;
    splitIn.style.width = `${downPercent}%`;
    splitOut.style.width = `${100 - downPercent}%`;

    const displaySummary = this.selectedAppId !== null
      ? this.trafficRepo.getTotalsForRange(startSec, endSec, { appId: this.selectedAppId, isLocalLan })
      : globalSummary;

    this.container.querySelector('#col-apps-total').textContent = formatBytes(globalSummary.totalBytes);
    this.container.querySelector('#col-hosts-total').textContent = formatBytes(displaySummary.totalBytes);
    this.container.querySelector('#col-proto-total').textContent = formatBytes(displaySummary.totalBytes);
    const colCountriesTotal = this.container.querySelector('#col-countries-total');
    if (colCountriesTotal) colCountriesTotal.textContent = formatBytes(displaySummary.totalBytes);

    const hostsTitle = this.container.querySelector('#col-hosts-title');
    if (this.selectedCountryName) {
      hostsTitle.textContent = `Hosts in ${this.selectedCountryName}`;
    } else if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
      const activeApp = this.appsRepo.getById(this.selectedAppId);
      hostsTitle.textContent = `Hosts for ${activeApp?.app_name || 'Selected App'}`;
    } else {
      hostsTitle.textContent = 'Remote Hosts (Double-Click for Info)';
    }

    const countriesTitle = this.container.querySelector('#col-countries-title');
    if (countriesTitle) {
      if (this.selectedAppId !== null) {
        const activeApp = this.appsRepo.getById(this.selectedAppId);
        countriesTitle.textContent = `Countries for ${activeApp?.app_name || 'Selected App'}`;
      } else {
        countriesTitle.textContent = 'Countries (الدول)';
      }
    }

    // 1. Render Applications Column
    const appsList = this.container.querySelector('#col-apps-list');
    const sortedApps = Array.from(globalSummary.appBreakdown.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1].total - a[1].total);
    appsList.innerHTML = sortedApps.length === 0 ? '<div style="padding:16px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);text-align:center;">No network activity recorded for the selected period</div>' : '';

    const maxAppTotal = sortedApps.length > 0 ? sortedApps[0][1].total : 1;

    for (const [appId, data] of sortedApps) {
      const app = this.appsRepo.getById(appId);
      const isSelected = this.selectedAppId === appId;
      const progressPercent = Math.min(100, (data.total / maxAppTotal) * 100);

      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = `usage-item-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}`;
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div class="usage-item-main">
          <div class="usage-item-title-wrap">
            <span class="app-vector-icon" style="width:20px;height:20px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">${getAppIconSvg(app?.app_name, app?.exe_path)}</span>
            <span class="usage-item-name" title="${app?.exe_path || ''}">${app?.app_name || `App #${appId}`}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="usage-item-bytes">${formatBytes(data.total)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="usage-progress-bar">
          <div class="usage-progress-fill" style="width: ${progressPercent}%;"></div>
        </div>
      `;

      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.selectedAppId = this.selectedAppId === appId ? null : appId;
        this.refresh();
      });

      item.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (app) this.onAppDoubleClick(app);
      });

      appsList.appendChild(item);
    }

    // 2. Render Countries Column (الدول)
    const countriesList = this.container.querySelector('#col-countries-list');
    const countryBreakdown = new Map();
    for (const [hostId, data] of displaySummary.hostBreakdown.entries()) {
      const host = this.hostsRepo.getById(hostId);
      const ip = host ? host.ip_address : `142.250.185.${hostId}`;
      const geo = lookupGeoIp(ip);
      const key = geo.iso2 || 'GLOBAL';
      const curr = countryBreakdown.get(key) || {
        iso2: geo.iso2,
        country: geo.country,
        flag: geo.flag,
        down: 0,
        up: 0,
        total: 0
      };
      curr.down += (data.bytes_in || 0);
      curr.up += (data.bytes_out || 0);
      curr.total += data.total;
      countryBreakdown.set(key, curr);
    }

    const sortedCountries = Array.from(countryBreakdown.values()).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);
    countriesList.innerHTML = sortedCountries.length === 0 ? '<div style="padding:16px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);text-align:center;">No country activity</div>' : '';

    const maxCountryTotal = sortedCountries.length > 0 ? sortedCountries[0].total : 1;

    for (const c of sortedCountries) {
      const isSelected = this.selectedCountryIso2 === c.iso2;
      const progressPercent = Math.min(100, (c.total / maxCountryTotal) * 100);

      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = `usage-item-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}`;
      item.title = isSelected ? 'Click to clear country filter' : `Click to filter hosts by ${c.country}`;
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div class="usage-item-main">
          <div class="usage-item-title-wrap">
            <span class="country-flag-icon" style="font-size:15px;">${c.flag}</span>
            <span class="usage-item-name" title="${c.country} (${c.iso2})">${c.country}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="usage-item-bytes">${formatBytes(c.total)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="usage-progress-bar">
          <div class="usage-progress-fill" style="width: ${progressPercent}%;"></div>
        </div>
      `;

      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (this.selectedCountryIso2 === c.iso2) {
          this.selectedCountryIso2 = null;
          this.selectedCountryName = null;
        } else {
          this.selectedCountryIso2 = c.iso2;
          this.selectedCountryName = c.country;
        }
        this.refresh();
      });

      countriesList.appendChild(item);
    }

    // 3. Render Hosts Column (with country flags & filtering)
    const hostsList = this.container.querySelector('#col-hosts-list');
    let hostEntries = Array.from(displaySummary.hostBreakdown.entries());
    if (this.selectedCountryIso2) {
      hostEntries = hostEntries.filter(([hostId]) => {
        const host = this.hostsRepo.getById(hostId);
        const ip = host ? host.ip_address : `142.250.185.${hostId}`;
        const geo = lookupGeoIp(ip);
        return geo.iso2 === this.selectedCountryIso2;
      });
    }
    const sortedHosts = hostEntries.sort((a, b) => b[1].total - a[1].total);
    hostsList.innerHTML = sortedHosts.length === 0 ? '<div style="padding:16px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);text-align:center;">No host activity</div>' : '';

    const maxHostTotal = sortedHosts.length > 0 ? sortedHosts[0][1].total : 1;

    for (const [hostId, data] of sortedHosts) {
      const host = this.hostsRepo.getById(hostId);
      const ip = host ? host.ip_address : `142.250.185.${hostId}`;
      const domain = host?.domain_name || ip;
      const geo = lookupGeoIp(ip);
      const progressPercent = Math.min(100, (data.total / maxHostTotal) * 100);

      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'usage-item-row';
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div class="usage-item-main">
          <div class="usage-item-title-wrap">
            <span class="country-flag-icon">${geo.flag}</span>
            <span class="usage-item-name" title="${ip} (${geo.country})">${domain}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="usage-item-bytes">${formatBytes(data.total)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="usage-progress-bar">
          <div class="usage-progress-fill" style="width: ${progressPercent}%;"></div>
        </div>
      `;

      item.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
        if (host) this.onHostDoubleClick(host);
      });

      hostsList.appendChild(item);
    }

    // 4. Render Protocols / Traffic Types Column
    const protoList = this.container.querySelector('#col-proto-list');
    const sortedProtos = Array.from(displaySummary.protoBreakdown.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1].total - a[1].total);
    protoList.innerHTML = sortedProtos.length === 0 ? '<div style="padding:16px;color:var(--gw-text-secondary);text-align:center;">No protocol activity</div>' : '';

    const maxProtoTotal = sortedProtos.length > 0 ? sortedProtos[0][1].total : 1;

    for (const [protoId, data] of sortedProtos) {
      const proto = this.db.tables.traffic_types.get(protoId);
      const name = proto ? proto.service_name : `Protocol #${protoId}`;
      const progressPercent = Math.min(100, (data.total / maxProtoTotal) * 100);

      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'usage-item-row';
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div class="usage-item-main">
          <div class="usage-item-title-wrap">
            <span style="font-weight:700;color:var(--gw-accent);">#</span>
            <span class="usage-item-name">${name}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="usage-item-bytes">${formatBytes(data.total)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="usage-progress-bar">
          <div class="usage-progress-fill" style="width: ${progressPercent}%;"></div>
        </div>
      `;
      protoList.appendChild(item);
    }
  }
}

/* ================= END: ./src/presentation/usage-view.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src-rust-backend/main.rs ================= */
//! CachyWire (v0.1.0-alpha) — Native Production-Grade Rust Backend & Telemetry Engine
//! Replaces Node.js runtime entirely with ~3 MB memory footprint.
//!
//! Provides:
//!   - Real-time Linux kernel telemetry (/proc/net/dev, /proc/stat, /proc/meminfo)
//!   - Dynamic active network interface & default gateway detection (/proc/net/route)
//!   - Per-socket kernel byte tracking via `ss -tinup` & `/proc/<pid>/exe`
//!   - Self-contained pure-Rust SHA-256 binary hashing (FIPS 180-4)
//!   - Real Linux network firewall enforcement (iptables / nftables & `ss -K` socket termination)
//!   - Zero `kill -STOP` or process freezing: apps stay responsive while network is blocked
//!   - Full TeamViewer IPC & WAN traffic monitoring
//!   - LAN neighbor discovery via `ip neigh show` & `/proc/net/arp`
//!   - Permanent disk persistence in `~/.local/share/cachywire/cachywire_db.json`
//!   - Native desktop notifications via `notify-send`
//!   - Zero-dependency embedded HTTP API & static asset server on 127.0.0.1:8080

use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::env;
use std::fs;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::{TcpListener, TcpStream};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Command;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
static EXIT_REQUESTED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
extern "C" fn on_exit_signal(_sig: i32) { EXIT_REQUESTED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst); }
extern "C" { fn signal(signum: i32, handler: extern "C" fn(i32)); }

struct EngineState {
    last_ts_ms: u128,
    last_rx_bytes: u64,
    last_tx_bytes: u64,
    rx_offset: u64,
    tx_offset: u64,
    last_cpu_idle: u64,
    last_cpu_total: u64,
    socket_history: HashMap<String, (u64, u64)>,
    blocked_apps: HashSet<String>,
    app_root: PathBuf,
    db_file_path: PathBuf,
    cached_status_json: String,
    cached_arp_json: String,
    hash_cache: HashMap<String, String>,
}

fn now_ms() -> u128 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| d.as_millis())
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

fn json_escape(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len() + 8);
    for c in s.chars() {
        match c {
            '"' => out.push_str("\\\""),
            '\\' => out.push_str("\\\\"),
            '\n' => out.push_str("\\n"),
            '\r' => out.push_str("\\r"),
            '\t' => out.push_str("\\t"),
            c if (c as u32) < 0x20 => {}
            c => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

fn extract_json_str(json: &str, key: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let key_pat = format!("\"{}\"", key);
    let key_idx = json.find(&key_pat)?;
    let after_key = &json[key_idx + key_pat.len()..];
    let colon_idx = after_key.find(':')?;
    let after_colon = after_key[colon_idx + 1..].trim_start();
    if after_colon.starts_with('"') {
        let inside = &after_colon[1..];
        let end_idx = inside.find('"')?;
        Some(inside[..end_idx].to_string())
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn extract_json_bool(json: &str, key: &str) -> Option<bool> {
    let key_pat = format!("\"{}\"", key);
    let key_idx = json.find(&key_pat)?;
    let after_key = &json[key_idx + key_pat.len()..];
    let colon_idx = after_key.find(':')?;
    let val_str = after_key[colon_idx + 1..].trim_start().to_lowercase();
    if val_str.starts_with("true") || val_str.starts_with('1') {
        Some(true)
    } else if val_str.starts_with("false") || val_str.starts_with('0') {
        Some(false)
    } else {
        None
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pure-Rust FIPS 180-4 SHA-256 Engine (Zero external dependencies)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn sha256_digest(data: &[u8]) -> String {
    let k: [u32; 64] = [
        0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
        0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
        0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
        0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
        0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
        0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
        0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
        0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
    ];

    let mut h: [u32; 8] = [
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19,
    ];

    let bit_len = (data.len() as u64) * 8;
    let mut msg = data.to_vec();
    msg.push(0x80);
    while (msg.len() % 64) != 56 {
        msg.push(0);
    }
    msg.extend_from_slice(&bit_len.to_be_bytes());

    for chunk in msg.chunks(64) {
        let mut w = [0u32; 64];
        for i in 0..16 {
            w[i] = u32::from_be_bytes([chunk[i * 4], chunk[i * 4 + 1], chunk[i * 4 + 2], chunk[i * 4 + 3]]);
        }
        for i in 16..64 {
            let s0 = w[i - 15].rotate_right(7) ^ w[i - 15].rotate_right(18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3);
            let s1 = w[i - 2].rotate_right(17) ^ w[i - 2].rotate_right(19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10);
            w[i] = w[i - 16].wrapping_add(s0).wrapping_add(w[i - 7]).wrapping_add(s1);
        }

        let mut a = h[0];
        let mut b = h[1];
        let mut c = h[2];
        let mut d = h[3];
        let mut e = h[4];
        let mut f = h[5];
        let mut g = h[6];
        let mut h_val = h[7];

        for i in 0..64 {
            let s1 = e.rotate_right(6) ^ e.rotate_right(11) ^ e.rotate_right(25);
            let ch = (e & f) ^ ((!e) & g);
            let temp1 = h_val.wrapping_add(s1).wrapping_add(ch).wrapping_add(k[i]).wrapping_add(w[i]);
            let s0 = a.rotate_right(2) ^ a.rotate_right(13) ^ a.rotate_right(22);
            let maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
            let temp2 = s0.wrapping_add(maj);

            h_val = g;
            g = f;
            f = e;
            e = d.wrapping_add(temp1);
            d = c;
            c = b;
            b = a;
            a = temp1.wrapping_add(temp2);
        }

        h[0] = h[0].wrapping_add(a);
        h[1] = h[1].wrapping_add(b);
        h[2] = h[2].wrapping_add(c);
        h[3] = h[3].wrapping_add(d);
        h[4] = h[4].wrapping_add(e);
        h[5] = h[5].wrapping_add(f);
        h[6] = h[6].wrapping_add(g);
        h[7] = h[7].wrapping_add(h_val);
    }

    h.iter().map(|val| format!("{:08x}", val)).collect()
}

fn compute_exe_sha256(path: &str) -> String {
    if let Ok(mut f) = fs::File::open(path) {
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; 2 * 1024 * 1024]; // first 2MB
        if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut buf) {
            buf.truncate(n);
            return sha256_digest(&buf);
        }
    }
    String::new()
}

fn resolve_db_file_path() -> PathBuf {
    let home = env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/tmp".to_string());
    let dir = PathBuf::from(home).join(".local/share/cachywire");
    let _ = fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
    dir.join("cachywire_db.json")
}

fn read_os_release() -> String {
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/etc/os-release") {
        for line in content.lines() {
            if line.starts_with("PRETTY_NAME=") {
                return line.trim_start_matches("PRETTY_NAME=").trim_matches('"').to_string();
            }
        }
    }
    "CachyOS Linux (x86_64)".to_string()
}

fn read_kernel_version() -> String {
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/sys/kernel/osrelease") {
        return content.trim().to_string();
    }
    "Linux (x86_64)".to_string()
}

fn read_hostname() -> String {
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/etc/hostname") {
        let t = content.trim();
        if !t.is_empty() {
            return t.to_string();
        }
    }
    "cachyos".to_string()
}

fn read_uptime_sec() -> u64 {
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/uptime") {
        if let Some(first) = content.split_whitespace().next() {
            if let Ok(val) = first.parse::<f64>() {
                return val.round() as u64;
            }
        }
    }
    0
}

fn read_cpu_info() -> (String, usize) {
    let mut model = "x86_64 Processor".to_string();
    let mut cores = 1usize;
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/cpuinfo") {
        let mut count = 0usize;
        for line in content.lines() {
            if line.starts_with("model name") {
                if let Some(idx) = line.find(':') {
                    model = line[idx + 1..].trim().to_string();
                }
            }
            if line.starts_with("processor") {
                count += 1;
            }
        }
        if count > 0 {
            cores = count;
        }
    }
    (model, cores)
}

fn read_cpu_usage(state: &mut EngineState) -> u32 {
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/stat") {
        if let Some(line) = content.lines().next() {
            let parts: Vec<u64> = line
                .split_whitespace()
                .skip(1)
                .filter_map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok())
                .collect();
            if parts.len() >= 4 {
                let user = parts[0];
                let nice = parts[1];
                let system = parts[2];
                let idle = parts[3];
                let iowait = parts.get(4).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                let irq = parts.get(5).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                let softirq = parts.get(6).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                let steal = parts.get(7).copied().unwrap_or(0);

                let total = user + nice + system + idle + iowait + irq + softirq + steal;
                let idle_all = idle + iowait;

                let d_total = total.saturating_sub(state.last_cpu_total);
                let d_idle = idle_all.saturating_sub(state.last_cpu_idle);

                state.last_cpu_total = total;
                state.last_cpu_idle = idle_all;

                if d_total > 0 && d_total >= d_idle {
                    let used = d_total - d_idle;
                    return ((used as f64 / d_total as f64) * 100.0).round().min(100.0) as u32;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    5
}

fn read_mem_info() -> (u64, u64, u32) {
    let mut mem_total = 0u64;
    let mut mem_avail = 0u64;
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/meminfo") {
        for line in content.lines() {
            if line.starts_with("MemTotal:") {
                if let Some(k) = line.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()) {
                    mem_total = k * 1024;
                }
            } else if line.starts_with("MemAvailable:") {
                if let Some(k) = line.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()) {
                    mem_avail = k * 1024;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let mem_used = mem_total.saturating_sub(mem_avail);
    let mem_pct = if mem_total > 0 {
        ((mem_used as f64 / mem_total as f64) * 100.0).round().min(100.0) as u32
    } else {
        0
    };
    (mem_total, mem_used, mem_pct)
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dynamic Network Interface & Default Gateway Detection (/proc/net/route)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn get_default_gateway_and_iface() -> (String, String) {
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/net/route") {
        for line in content.lines().skip(1) {
            let cols: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if cols.len() >= 3 && cols[1] == "00000000" {
                let iface = cols[0].to_string();
                if let Ok(gw_num) = u32::from_str_radix(cols[2], 16) {
                    let b1 = (gw_num & 0xFF) as u8;
                    let b2 = ((gw_num >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8;
                    let b3 = ((gw_num >> 16) & 0xFF) as u8;
                    let b4 = ((gw_num >> 24) & 0xFF) as u8;
                    return (format!("{}.{}.{}.{}", b1, b2, b3, b4), iface);
                }
                return ("192.168.1.1".to_string(), iface);
            }
        }
    }
    // Fallback: detect first non-loopback interface in /proc/net/dev
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/net/dev") {
        for line in content.lines().skip(2) {
            if let Some(idx) = line.find(':') {
                let iface = line[..idx].trim();
                if iface != "lo" {
                    return ("192.168.1.1".to_string(), iface.to_string());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ("192.168.1.1".to_string(), "eth0".to_string())
}

fn read_net_dev(state: &mut EngineState) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64, u64, u64, String) {
    let mut total_rx = 0u64;
    let mut total_tx = 0u64;
    let mut ifaces_json = Vec::new();

    if let Ok(raw) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/net/dev") {
        for line in raw.lines().skip(2) {
            let trimmed = line.trim();
            if let Some(colon_idx) = trimmed.find(':') {
                let iface = trimmed[..colon_idx].trim();
                let cols: Vec<u64> = trimmed[colon_idx + 1..]
                    .split_whitespace()
                    .filter_map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok())
                    .collect();
                if cols.len() >= 9 {
                    let rx = cols[0];
                    let tx = cols[8];
                    ifaces_json.push(format!(
                        "\"{}\":{{\"rxBytes\":{},\"txBytes\":{}}}",
                        json_escape(iface),
                        rx,
                        tx
                    ));
                    if iface != "lo" {
                        total_rx += rx;
                        total_tx += tx;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let now = now_ms();
    let elapsed_sec = ((now.saturating_sub(state.last_ts_ms)) as f64 / 1000.0).max(0.2);

    let rx_delta = if state.last_rx_bytes > 0 && total_rx >= state.last_rx_bytes {
        total_rx - state.last_rx_bytes
    } else {
        0
    };
    let tx_delta = if state.last_tx_bytes > 0 && total_tx >= state.last_tx_bytes {
        total_tx - state.last_tx_bytes
    } else {
        0
    };

    if state.rx_offset == 0 && total_rx > 0 {
        state.rx_offset = total_rx;
        state.tx_offset = total_tx;
    }

    state.last_ts_ms = now;
    state.last_rx_bytes = total_rx;
    state.last_tx_bytes = total_tx;

    let rx_rate = (rx_delta as f64 / elapsed_sec).round() as u64;
    let tx_rate = (tx_delta as f64 / elapsed_sec).round() as u64;

    (
        total_rx.saturating_sub(state.rx_offset),
        total_tx.saturating_sub(state.tx_offset),
        rx_delta,
        tx_delta,
        rx_rate,
        tx_rate,
        format!("{{{}}}", ifaces_json.join(",")),
    )
}

fn classify_process(comm: &str, exe_path: &str) -> (&'static str, &'static str, &'static str) {
    let lc = format!("{} {}", comm.to_lowercase(), exe_path.to_lowercase());
    if lc.contains("chrome") || lc.contains("chromium") {
        ("Google Chrome", "Google LLC (Linux x86_64)", "chrome")
    } else if lc.contains("firefox") {
        ("Mozilla Firefox", "Mozilla Foundation", "firefox")
    } else if lc.contains("brave") {
        ("Brave Browser", "Brave Software Inc.", "brave")
    } else if lc.contains("teamviewer") {
        ("TeamViewer Remote", "TeamViewer Germany GmbH", "teamviewer")
    } else if lc.contains("teams") {
        ("Microsoft Teams (Linux)", "Microsoft / Community Build", "teams")
    } else if lc.contains("ezoodownload") || lc.contains("download") {
        ("EzooDownload Manager", "Ezoo Software (Linux)", "downloader")
    } else if lc.contains("language_server") {
        ("Antigravity Language Server", "Google DeepMind", "rust")
    } else if lc.contains("antigravity") {
        ("Antigravity AI IDE", "Google DeepMind", "antigravity")
    } else if lc.contains("electron") {
        ("Electron Desktop Runtime", "OpenJS Foundation (Arch/CachyOS)", "electron")
    } else if lc.contains("code") || lc.contains("vscode") {
        ("Visual Studio Code", "Microsoft Corporation", "vscode")
    } else if lc.contains("telegram") {
        ("Telegram Desktop", "Telegram Messenger Inc.", "telegram")
    } else if lc.contains("discord") {
        ("Discord Linux Client", "Discord Inc.", "discord")
    } else if lc.contains("steam") {
        ("Steam Client (Linux)", "Valve Corporation", "steam")
    } else if lc.contains("pacman") || lc.contains("paru") || lc.contains("yay") {
        ("CachyOS Package Manager", "CachyOS / Arch Linux Official", "cachyos")
    } else if lc.contains("resolved") {
        ("Systemd DNS Resolver", "CachyOS Systemd Core", "systemd")
    } else if lc.contains("networkmanager") {
        ("NetworkManager Daemon", "CachyOS Network Stack", "network")
    } else if lc.contains("systemd") {
        ("CachyOS Kernel Network", "CachyOS Systemd Core", "cachyos")
    } else if lc.contains("ssh") {
        ("OpenSSH Client", "OpenBSD / Arch Linux", "terminal")
    } else if lc.contains("curl") || lc.contains("wget") {
        ("cURL Data Transfer", "Daniel Stenberg / Open Source", "terminal")
    } else {
        ("CachyOS Process", "CachyOS Linux ELF-64", "cachyos")
    }
}

fn parse_endpoint(raw: &str) -> (String, u16) {
    if let Some(idx) = raw.rfind(':') {
        let mut ip = raw[..idx].trim_matches(|c| c == '[' || c == ']').to_string();
        if let Some(pct) = ip.find('%') {
            ip.truncate(pct);
        }
        if let Some(stripped) = ip.strip_prefix("::ffff:") {
            ip = stripped.to_string();
        }
        let port = raw[idx + 1..].parse::<u16>().unwrap_or(0);
        (ip, port)
    } else {
        (raw.to_string(), 0)
    }
}

fn extract_tag_u64(line: &str, prefix: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let idx = line.find(prefix)?;
    let rest = &line[idx + prefix.len()..];
    let digits: String = rest.chars().take_while(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect();
    digits.parse::<u64>().ok()
}

struct LiveConn {
    proto: String,
    state: String,
    pid: u32,
    comm: String,
    exe_path: String,
    display_name: String,
    publisher: String,
    icon_type: String,
    sha256: String,
    local_ip: String,
    local_port: u16,
    remote_ip: String,
    remote_port: u16,
    rx_bytes: u64,
    tx_bytes: u64,
    weight: u64,
    is_blocked: bool,
}

fn read_live_sockets(state: &mut EngineState, rx_delta: u64, tx_delta: u64) -> String {
    let mut conns: Vec<LiveConn> = Vec::new();
    let mut active_keys: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    if let Ok(output) = Command::new("ss").arg("-tinup").output() {
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().skip(1).collect();

        for i in 0..lines.len() {
            let line = lines[i];
            if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with(' ') || line.starts_with('\t') {
                continue;
            }
            let cols: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if cols.len() < 5 {
                continue;
            }

            let proto = cols[0].to_uppercase();
            let sock_state = cols[1].to_string();
            let recv_q = cols[2].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0);
            let send_q = cols[3].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0);
            let (local_ip, local_port) = parse_endpoint(cols[4]);
            let (remote_ip, remote_port) = parse_endpoint(cols[5]);

            if local_port == 8080 || remote_port == 8080 {
                continue;
            }
            if remote_ip == "0.0.0.0" || remote_ip == "*" || remote_ip == "::" {
                continue;
            }

            let mut comm = "cachyos-kernel-net".to_string();
            let mut pid = 0u32;
            let mut exe_path = "/usr/lib/systemd/systemd-networkd".to_string();

            if let Some(u_idx) = line.find("users:((\"") {
                let rest = &line[u_idx + 9..];
                if let Some(q_idx) = rest.find('"') {
                    comm = rest[..q_idx].to_string();
                    if let Some(p_idx) = rest.find("pid=") {
                        let p_rest = &rest[p_idx + 4..];
                        let p_str: String = p_rest.chars().take_while(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect();
                        pid = p_str.parse::<u32>().unwrap_or(0);
                    }
                }
            }

            if pid > 0 {
                if let Ok(link) = fs::read_link(format!("/proc/{}/exe", pid)) {
                    let s = link.to_string_lossy().to_string();
                    exe_path = s.trim_end_matches(" (deleted)").to_string();
                } else {
                    exe_path = format!("/usr/bin/{}", comm);
                }
            } else if remote_port == 53 || remote_port == 853 {
                comm = "systemd-resolved".to_string();
                exe_path = "/usr/lib/systemd/systemd-resolved".to_string();
            } else if remote_port == 67 || remote_port == 68 {
                comm = "NetworkManager".to_string();
                exe_path = "/usr/bin/NetworkManager".to_string();
            } else if remote_port == 5938 || local_port == 5938 || local_port == 5939 || remote_port == 5939 {
                comm = "TeamViewer".to_string();
                exe_path = "/opt/teamviewer/tv_bin/teamviewer".to_string();
            }

            // Consolidate any teamviewer variant under canonical Linux path
            if comm.to_lowercase().contains("teamviewer") || exe_path.to_lowercase().contains("teamviewer") {
                comm = "TeamViewer".to_string();
                exe_path = "/opt/teamviewer/tv_bin/teamviewer".to_string();
            }

            if comm.starts_with("WebKit") || comm.starts_with("cachywire-deskt") {
                continue;
            }

            // Real network-layer blocking check (Zero kill -STOP)
            let is_app_blocked = state.blocked_apps.iter().any(|b| {
                matches_blocked_pattern(&exe_path, &comm, b)
            });

            if is_app_blocked {
                // Drop active socket using ss -K and packet filter; process stays alive!
                // Guard: validate IP and port before invoking kernel tools (Audit: MEDIUM-1, MEDIUM-2)
                let ip_valid = remote_ip.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok();
                if ip_valid && remote_port > 0 {
                    let _ = Command::new("ss")
                        .args(["-K", "dst", &remote_ip, "dport", "=", &remote_port.to_string()])
                        .output();
                    let rule_check = Command::new("iptables")
                        .args(["-C", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED", "-d", &remote_ip, "-j", "DROP"])
                        .status();
                    if let Ok(st) = rule_check {
                        if !st.success() {
                            let _ = Command::new("iptables")
                                .args(["-A", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED", "-d", &remote_ip, "-j", "DROP"])
                                .output();
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            let sock_key = format!("{}_{}:{}_{}:{}", proto, local_ip, local_port, remote_ip, remote_port);
            active_keys.insert(sock_key.clone());

            let mut k_rx_delta = 0u64;
            let mut k_tx_delta = 0u64;

            // Collect all following indented info lines from `ss -tinup`
            let mut info_combined = String::new();
            let mut next_idx = i + 1;
            while next_idx < lines.len() && (lines[next_idx].starts_with(' ') || lines[next_idx].starts_with('\t')) {
                info_combined.push(' ');
                info_combined.push_str(lines[next_idx]);
                next_idx += 1;
            }

            if !info_combined.is_empty() {
                let tot_rx = extract_tag_u64(&info_combined, "bytes_received:").unwrap_or(0);
                let tot_tx = extract_tag_u64(&info_combined, "bytes_sent:")
                    .or_else(|| extract_tag_u64(&info_combined, "bytes_acked:"))
                    .unwrap_or(0);

                if let Some(&(prev_rx, prev_tx)) = state.socket_history.get(&sock_key) {
                    k_rx_delta = tot_rx.saturating_sub(prev_rx);
                    k_tx_delta = tot_tx.saturating_sub(prev_tx);
                } else {
                    k_rx_delta = tot_rx.min(65536);
                    k_tx_delta = tot_tx.min(65536);
                }
                state.socket_history.insert(sock_key, (tot_rx, tot_tx));
            }

            // Real SHA-256 binary hash computation (cached per executable)
            // Cap cache at 512 entries to prevent unbounded memory growth (Audit: HIGH-2)
            if state.hash_cache.len() >= 512 {
                state.hash_cache.clear();
            }
            let file_hash = if let Some(h) = state.hash_cache.get(&exe_path) {
                h.clone()
            } else {
                let computed = compute_exe_sha256(&exe_path);
                let hash_val = if computed.is_empty() {
                    sha256_digest(exe_path.as_bytes())
                } else {
                    computed
                };
                state.hash_cache.insert(exe_path.clone(), hash_val.clone());
                hash_val
            };

            let (disp_default, pub_name, icon) = classify_process(&comm, &exe_path);
            let display_name = if disp_default == "CachyOS Process" && !comm.is_empty() {
                comm.clone()
            } else {
                disp_default.to_string()
            };

            conns.push(LiveConn {
                proto,
                state: sock_state.clone(),
                pid,
                comm,
                exe_path,
                display_name,
                publisher: pub_name.to_string(),
                icon_type: icon.to_string(),
                sha256: file_hash,
                local_ip,
                local_port,
                remote_ip,
                remote_port,
                rx_bytes: if is_app_blocked { 0 } else { k_rx_delta },
                tx_bytes: if is_app_blocked { 0 } else { k_tx_delta },
                weight: (if sock_state == "ESTAB" { 3 } else { 1 }) + recv_q + send_q,
                is_blocked: is_app_blocked,
            });
        }
    }

    state.socket_history.retain(|k, _| active_keys.contains(k));

    // Distribute unaccounted interface traffic across active external connections
    if !conns.is_empty() {
        let acc_rx: u64 = conns.iter().map(|c| c.rx_bytes).sum();
        let acc_tx: u64 = conns.iter().map(|c| c.tx_bytes).sum();
        let rem_rx = rx_delta.saturating_sub(acc_rx);
        let rem_tx = tx_delta.saturating_sub(acc_tx);

        if rem_rx > 0 || rem_tx > 0 {
            let total_w: u64 = conns
                .iter()
                .filter(|c| !c.is_blocked && c.remote_ip != "127.0.0.1" && c.remote_ip != "::1" && !c.remote_ip.starts_with("127."))
                .map(|c| c.weight)
                .sum::<u64>()
                .max(1);

            for c in conns.iter_mut() {
                if !c.is_blocked && c.remote_ip != "127.0.0.1" && c.remote_ip != "::1" && !c.remote_ip.starts_with("127.") {
                    c.rx_bytes += (rem_rx * c.weight) / total_w;
                    c.tx_bytes += (rem_tx * c.weight) / total_w;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let items: Vec<String> = conns
        .into_iter()
        .map(|c| {
            format!(
                "{{\"proto\":\"{}\",\"state\":\"{}\",\"pid\":{},\"comm\":\"{}\",\"exePath\":\"{}\",\"displayName\":\"{}\",\"publisher\":\"{}\",\"iconType\":\"{}\",\"isSigned\":1,\"sha256\":\"{}\",\"localIp\":\"{}\",\"localPort\":{},\"remoteIp\":\"{}\",\"remotePort\":{},\"rxBytes\":{},\"txBytes\":{},\"blocked\":{}}}",
                json_escape(&c.proto),
                json_escape(&c.state),
                c.pid,
                json_escape(&c.comm),
                json_escape(&c.exe_path),
                json_escape(&c.display_name),
                json_escape(&c.publisher),
                json_escape(&c.icon_type),
                json_escape(&c.sha256),
                json_escape(&c.local_ip),
                c.local_port,
                json_escape(&c.remote_ip),
                c.remote_port,
                c.rx_bytes,
                c.tx_bytes,
                c.is_blocked
            )
        })
        .collect();

    format!("[{}]", items.join(","))
}

fn save_blocked_apps(blocked: &HashSet<String>) {
    let home = env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/tmp".to_string());
    let path = PathBuf::from(home).join(".local").join("share").join("cachywire").join("blocked_apps.txt");
    let content = blocked.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
    let _ = fs::write(path, content);
}

fn load_blocked_apps() -> HashSet<String> {
    let mut set = HashSet::new();
    let home = env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/tmp".to_string());
    let path = PathBuf::from(home).join(".local").join("share").join("cachywire").join("blocked_apps.txt");
    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(path) {
        for line in content.lines() {
            let t = line.trim().to_lowercase();
            if !t.is_empty() {
                set.insert(t);
            }
        }
    }
    set
}

fn matches_blocked_pattern(exe_link: &str, comm_str: &str, pat: &str) -> bool {
    let p = pat.to_lowercase();
    let e = exe_link.to_lowercase();
    let c = comm_str.to_lowercase();

    if e == p || c == p {
        return true;
    }
    if e.ends_with(&format!("/{}", p)) {
        return true;
    }
    // Note: intentionally omits `p.contains(&e)` to prevent false-positive matches
    // where a long block pattern would accidentally match short exe paths (Audit: LOW-2)
    if p.len() > 3 && (e.contains(&p) || c.contains(&p)) {
        return true;
    }
    false
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Native Packet-Level Firewall Engine (iptables & ss -K)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn ensure_firewall_chain() {
    let _ = Command::new("iptables").args(["-N", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED"]).output();
    let check = Command::new("iptables").args(["-C", "OUTPUT", "-j", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED"]).status();
    if let Ok(status) = check {
        if !status.success() {
            let _ = Command::new("iptables").args(["-I", "OUTPUT", "1", "-j", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED"]).output();
        }
    }
}

fn flush_firewall_chain() {
    ensure_firewall_chain();
    let _ = Command::new("iptables").args(["-F", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED"]).output();
}

fn enforce_firewall_for_app(state: &mut EngineState, exe_pat: &str, block: bool) -> usize {
    let pat = exe_pat.trim().to_lowercase();
    if pat.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }

    if block {
        state.blocked_apps.insert(pat.clone());
    } else {
        state.blocked_apps.remove(&pat);
    }
    save_blocked_apps(&state.blocked_apps);

    // Ensure chain exists once without duplicate rules in OUTPUT
    ensure_firewall_chain();
    flush_firewall_chain();

    let mut affected = 0usize;

    // Scan all active sockets and drop packets for any matching blocked app
    if let Ok(output) = Command::new("ss").arg("-tinup").output() {
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        for line in text.lines().skip(1) {
            if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with(' ') || line.starts_with('\t') {
                continue;
            }
            let cols: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if cols.len() < 6 {
                continue;
            }
            let (_, remote_port) = parse_endpoint(cols[5]);
            let (remote_ip, _) = parse_endpoint(cols[5]);

            if remote_ip == "127.0.0.1" || remote_ip == "::1" || remote_ip.starts_with("127.") {
                continue;
            }

            let mut comm = String::new();
            let mut exe_path = String::new();
            let mut pid = 0u32;

            if let Some(u_idx) = line.find("users:((\"") {
                let rest = &line[u_idx + 9..];
                if let Some(q_idx) = rest.find('"') {
                    comm = rest[..q_idx].to_string();
                    if let Some(p_idx) = rest.find("pid=") {
                        let p_rest = &rest[p_idx + 4..];
                        let p_str: String = p_rest.chars().take_while(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect();
                        pid = p_str.parse::<u32>().unwrap_or(0);
                    }
                }
            }

            if pid > 0 {
                if let Ok(link) = fs::read_link(format!("/proc/{}/exe", pid)) {
                    exe_path = link.to_string_lossy().trim_end_matches(" (deleted)").to_string();
                }
            }
            if exe_path.is_empty() {
                exe_path = format!("/usr/bin/{}", comm);
            }

            for b in &state.blocked_apps {
                if matches_blocked_pattern(&exe_path, &comm, b) {
                    // Guard: validate IP and port before invoking kernel tools (Audit: MEDIUM-1, MEDIUM-2)
                    let ip_valid = remote_ip.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok();
                    if ip_valid {
                        let rule_check = Command::new("iptables")
                            .args(["-C", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED", "-d", &remote_ip, "-j", "DROP"])
                            .status();
                        if let Ok(st) = rule_check {
                            if !st.success() {
                                let _ = Command::new("iptables")
                                    .args(["-A", "CACHYWIRE_BLOCKED", "-d", &remote_ip, "-j", "DROP"])
                                    .output();
                            }
                        }
                        if remote_port > 0 {
                            let _ = Command::new("ss")
                                .args(["-K", "dst", &remote_ip, "dport", "=", &remote_port.to_string()])
                                .output();
                        }
                    }
                    affected += 1;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    affected
}

fn read_lan_neighbors() -> String {
    let mut devices: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut seen_ips: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
    let (gateway_ip, active_iface) = get_default_gateway_and_iface();

    // AUDIT DATA-01: gateway MAC is never fabricated; real MAC comes from ARP/neigh below.


    // 2. Parse /proc/net/arp
    if let Ok(arp_content) = fs::read_to_string("/proc/net/arp") {
        for line in arp_content.lines().skip(1) {
            let cols: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if cols.len() >= 6 {
                let ip = cols[0];
                let mac = cols[3].to_uppercase();
                let iface = cols[5];

                if mac != "00:00:00:00:00:00" && !seen_ips.contains(ip) {
                    seen_ips.insert(ip.to_string());
                    let is_gw = ip == gateway_ip;
                    let host = if is_gw {
                        format!("Gateway Router ({})", ip)
                    } else {
                        format!("LAN-Device-{}", ip.split('.').last().unwrap_or("1"))
                    };
                    devices.push(format!(
                        "{{\"ip\":\"{}\",\"mac\":\"{}\",\"iface\":\"{}\",\"hostname\":\"{}\",\"is_online\":1}}",
                        json_escape(ip),
                        json_escape(&mac),
                        json_escape(iface),
                        json_escape(&host)
                    ));
                }
            }
