        }
    }

    // 3. `ip neigh show`
    if let Ok(out) = Command::new("ip").args(["neigh", "show"]).output() {
        let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
        for line in s.lines() {
            let cols: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if cols.len() >= 5 && cols[0].contains('.') {
                let ip = cols[0];
                if seen_ips.contains(ip) {
                    continue;
                }
                if let Some(ll_pos) = cols.iter().position(|&x| x == "lladdr") {
                    if ll_pos + 1 < cols.len() {
                        let mac = cols[ll_pos + 1].to_uppercase();
                        let iface = if cols.len() > 2 { cols[2] } else { &active_iface };
                        let state = cols.last().copied().unwrap_or("REACHABLE");
                        let is_online = if state == "FAILED" { 0 } else { 1 };
                        let is_gw = ip == gateway_ip;
                        let host = if is_gw {
                            format!("Gateway Router ({})", ip)
                        } else {
                            format!("LAN-Device-{}", ip.split('.').last().unwrap_or("1"))
                        };
                        seen_ips.insert(ip.to_string());
                        devices.push(format!(
                            "{{\"ip\":\"{}\",\"mac\":\"{}\",\"iface\":\"{}\",\"hostname\":\"{}\",\"is_online\":{}}}",
                            json_escape(ip),
                            json_escape(&mac),
                            json_escape(iface),
                            json_escape(&host),
                            is_online
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if !seen_ips.contains(&gateway_ip) {
        devices.insert(0, format!(
            "{{\"ip\":\"{}\",\"mac\":\"{}\",\"iface\":\"{}\",\"hostname\":\"{}\",\"is_online\":1}}",
            json_escape(&gateway_ip),
            "UNKNOWN",
            json_escape(&active_iface),
            format!("Default Gateway ({})", gateway_ip)
        ));
    }
    format!("[{}]", devices.join(","))
}

fn mime_type(path: &Path) -> &'static str {
    match path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("") {
        "html" => "text/html; charset=utf-8",
        "css" => "text/css; charset=utf-8",
        "js" | "mjs" => "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
        "json" => "application/json; charset=utf-8",
        "png" => "image/png",
        "jpg" | "jpeg" => "image/jpeg",
        "svg" => "image/svg+xml",
        "ico" => "image/x-icon",
        "wasm" => "application/wasm",
        _ => "application/octet-stream",
    }
}

fn send_response(stream: &mut TcpStream, status: &str, content_type: &str, body: &[u8]) {
    let header = format!(
        "HTTP/1.1 {}\r\nContent-Type: {}\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nX-Content-Type-Options: nosniff\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n",
        status,
        content_type,
        body.len()
    );
    let _ = stream.write_all(header.as_bytes());
    let _ = stream.write_all(body);
    let _ = stream.flush();
}

fn handle_client(mut stream: TcpStream, state: Arc<Mutex<EngineState>>) {
    let mut buf = [0u8; 8192];
    let n = match stream.read(&mut buf) {
        Ok(0) | Err(_) => return,
        Ok(bytes) => bytes,
    };

    let req_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf[..n]);
    let first_line = match req_str.lines().next() {
        Some(l) => l,
        None => return,
    };
    let parts: Vec<&str> = first_line.split_whitespace().collect();
    if parts.len() < 2 {
        return;
    }
    let method = parts[0];
    let mut req_path = parts[1];
    if let Some(idx) = req_path.find('?') {
        req_path = &req_path[..idx];
    }

    if method == "OPTIONS" {
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "text/plain", b"OK");
        return;
    }

    // Extract POST request body
    let mut body_bytes = Vec::new();
    if method == "POST" {
        let mut content_length = 0usize;
        for line in req_str.lines() {
            if line.to_lowercase().starts_with("content-length:") {
                if let Some(val) = line.split(':').nth(1) {
                    content_length = val.trim().parse::<usize>().unwrap_or(0);
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(pos) = req_str.find("\r\n\r\n") {
            let initial_body = &buf[pos + 4..n];
            body_bytes.extend_from_slice(initial_body);
        }
        let content_length = content_length.min(64 * 1024 * 1024); while body_bytes.len() < content_length {
            let mut more = [0u8; 8192];
            match stream.read(&mut more) {
                Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
                Ok(c) => body_bytes.extend_from_slice(&more[..c]),
            }
        }
    }

    if req_path == "/api/system/status" && method == "GET" {
        let json = { state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).cached_status_json.clone() };
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", json.as_bytes());
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/network/arp" && method == "GET" {
        let json = { state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).cached_arp_json.clone() };
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", json.as_bytes());
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/database/state" && method == "GET" {
        let db_path = { state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).db_file_path.clone() };
        if let Ok(content) = fs::read(&db_path) {
            send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", &content);
        } else {
            send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"applications\":{},\"traffic_flows\":[],\"remote_hosts\":{},\"traffic_types\":{},\"lan_devices\":{},\"alerts\":[],\"blocked_apps\":[]}");
        }
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/database/state" && method == "POST" {
        let db_path = { state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).db_file_path.clone() };
        let looks_like_json = body_bytes.first() == Some(&b'{') && body_bytes.windows(14).any(|w| w == b"\"applications\"");
if !looks_like_json { send_response(&mut stream, "400 Bad Request", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"ok\":false}"); return; }
let tmp_path = db_path.with_extension("json.tmp");
if fs::write(&tmp_path, &body_bytes).is_err() || fs::rename(&tmp_path, &db_path).is_err() { send_response(&mut stream, "500 Internal Server Error", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"ok\":false}"); return; }
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"ok\":true}");
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/database/reset" && method == "POST" {
        let mut st = state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
        let db_path = st.db_file_path.clone();
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&db_path);
        st.rx_offset = 0;
        st.tx_offset = 0;
        st.socket_history.clear();
        st.blocked_apps.clear();
        flush_firewall_chain();
        save_blocked_apps(&st.blocked_apps);
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"ok\":true,\"cleared\":true}");
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/firewall/enforce" && method == "POST" {
        let body_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&body_bytes);
        let exe_pat = extract_json_str(&body_str, "exePath").unwrap_or_default();
        let block = extract_json_bool(&body_str, "blocked").unwrap_or(false);
        let unblock_all = extract_json_bool(&body_str, "unblockAll").unwrap_or(false);

        let mut st = state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
        if unblock_all {
            st.blocked_apps.clear();
            flush_firewall_chain();
            save_blocked_apps(&st.blocked_apps);
            send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"ok\":true,\"unblockedAll\":true}");
            return;
        }

        let affected = enforce_firewall_for_app(&mut st, &exe_pat, block);
        let resp = format!("{{\"ok\":true,\"exePath\":\"{}\",\"blocked\":{},\"affectedProcesses\":{}}}", json_escape(&exe_pat), block, affected);
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", resp.as_bytes());
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/notify" && method == "POST" {
        let body_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&body_bytes);
        let title = extract_json_str(&body_str, "title").unwrap_or_else(|| "CachyWire Security".to_string());
        let message = extract_json_str(&body_str, "message").unwrap_or_default();

        // Sanitize notify-send arguments: strip null bytes and control chars, cap at 256 chars (Audit: HIGH-1)
        let sanitize = |s: &str| -> String {
            s.chars()
                .map(|c| if c == '\n' || c == '\r' || c == '\t' { ' ' } else { c })
                .filter(|&c| c != '\0' && (c as u32) >= 0x20)
                .take(256)
                .collect()
        };
        let _ = Command::new("notify-send")
            .args(["-a", "CachyWire", "-i", "cachywire", &sanitize(&title), &sanitize(&message)])
            .output();
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", b"{\"ok\":true}");
        return;
    }

    if req_path == "/api/system/autostart" && method == "POST" {
        let body_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&body_bytes);
        let enabled = extract_json_bool(&body_str, "enabled").unwrap_or(false);
        let home = env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/tmp".to_string());
        let autostart_dir = PathBuf::from(&home).join(".config").join("autostart");
        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(&autostart_dir);
        let desktop_file = autostart_dir.join("cachywire.desktop");

        if enabled {
            let content = "[Desktop Entry]\nType=Application\nVersion=1.0\nName=CachyWire Network Monitor\nComment=Start CachyWire Network Monitor & Firewall on CachyOS login\nExec=/usr/bin/cachywire --minimized\nIcon=cachywire\nTerminal=false\nCategories=Network;Security;Monitor;System;\nStartupNotify=false\nX-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=true\n";
            let _ = fs::write(&desktop_file, content);
        } else {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&desktop_file);
        }
        let resp = format!("{{\"ok\":true,\"autostart\":{}}}", enabled);
        send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", "application/json; charset=utf-8", resp.as_bytes());
        return;
    }

    // Static frontend file serving with Path Traversal protection via canonicalize() (Audit: HIGH-3)
    let app_root = { state.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).app_root.clone() };
    let rel_path = if req_path == "/" { "index.html" } else { req_path.trim_start_matches('/') };

    // Quick pre-check for obvious traversal attempts before hitting the filesystem
    if rel_path.contains("..") || rel_path.contains('\0') {
        send_response(&mut stream, "403 Forbidden", "text/plain", b"Forbidden");
        return;
    }

    let file_path = app_root.join(rel_path);
    // Canonicalize resolves symlinks and `..` segments; verify target is a file inside app_root
    match file_path.canonicalize() {
        Ok(canonical) => {
            if canonical.starts_with(&app_root) && canonical.is_file() {
                if let Ok(content) = fs::read(&canonical) {
                    send_response(&mut stream, "200 OK", mime_type(&canonical), &content);
                } else {
                    send_response(&mut stream, "403 Forbidden", "text/plain", b"Forbidden");
                }
            } else {
                send_response(&mut stream, "403 Forbidden", "text/plain", b"Forbidden");
            }
        }
        Err(ref e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            send_response(&mut stream, "404 Not Found", "text/plain", b"404 Not Found");
        }
        Err(_) => {
            send_response(&mut stream, "403 Forbidden", "text/plain", b"Forbidden");
        }
    }
}

fn build_status_snapshot(
    st: &mut EngineState,
    os_name: &str,
    kernel_ver: &str,
    host_name: &str,
    cpu_model: &str,
    cpu_cores: usize,
    default_iface: &str,
) -> String {
    let (rx_tot, tx_tot, rx_delta, tx_delta, rx_rate, tx_rate, ifaces_json) = read_net_dev(st);
    let cpu_pct = read_cpu_usage(st);
    let (mem_tot, mem_used, mem_pct) = read_mem_info();
    let live_conns = read_live_sockets(st, rx_delta, tx_delta);

    format!(
        "{{\"engine\":\"CachyWire Rust Native 0.1.0\",\"dbPath\":\"{}\",\"osName\":\"{}\",\"kernel\":\"{}\",\"arch\":\"x86_64\",\"hostname\":\"{}\",\"uptimeSec\":{},\"cpuModel\":\"{}\",\"cpuCores\":{},\"cpuPercent\":{},\"memTotalBytes\":{},\"memUsedBytes\":{},\"memPercent\":{},\"network\":{{\"defaultInterface\":\"{}\",\"rxBytesTotal\":{},\"txBytesTotal\":{},\"rxDeltaBytes\":{},\"txDeltaBytes\":{},\"rxBytesPerSec\":{},\"txBytesPerSec\":{},\"interfaces\":{}}},\"liveConnections\":{}}}",
        json_escape(&st.db_file_path.to_string_lossy()),
        json_escape(os_name),
        json_escape(kernel_ver),
        json_escape(host_name),
        read_uptime_sec(),
        json_escape(cpu_model),
        cpu_cores,
        cpu_pct,
        mem_tot,
        mem_used,
        mem_pct,
        json_escape(default_iface),
        rx_tot,
        tx_tot,
        rx_delta,
        tx_delta,
        rx_rate,
        tx_rate,
        ifaces_json,
        live_conns
    )
}

fn main() {
    let raw_root = env::var("CACHYWIRE_APP_DIR")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| env::current_dir().unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(".")));
    let app_root = raw_root.canonicalize().unwrap_or(raw_root);

    let os_name = read_os_release();
    let kernel_ver = read_kernel_version();
    let host_name = read_hostname();
    let (cpu_model, cpu_cores) = read_cpu_info();
    let (_, default_iface) = get_default_gateway_and_iface();

    let mut initial_state = EngineState {
        last_ts_ms: now_ms(),
        last_rx_bytes: 0,
        last_tx_bytes: 0,
        rx_offset: 0,
        tx_offset: 0,
        last_cpu_idle: 0,
        last_cpu_total: 0,
        socket_history: HashMap::new(),
        blocked_apps: load_blocked_apps(),
        app_root,
        db_file_path: resolve_db_file_path(),
        cached_status_json: String::new(),
        cached_arp_json: read_lan_neighbors(),
        hash_cache: HashMap::new(),
    };

    let _ = read_net_dev(&mut initial_state);
    let _ = read_cpu_usage(&mut initial_state);
    initial_state.cached_status_json = build_status_snapshot(
        &mut initial_state,
        &os_name,
        &kernel_ver,
        &host_name,
        &cpu_model,
        cpu_cores,
        &default_iface,
    );

    let shared_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(initial_state));

    // Background 1Hz kernel telemetry & socket polling thread (zero HTTP blocking)
    let poll_state = Arc::clone(&shared_state);
    let os_name_bg = os_name.clone();
    let kernel_ver_bg = kernel_ver.clone();
    let host_name_bg = host_name.clone();
    let cpu_model_bg = cpu_model.clone();
    let default_iface_bg = default_iface.clone();

    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut tick: u64 = 0;
        loop {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(950));
        if EXIT_REQUESTED.load(Ordering::SeqCst) { flush_firewall_chain(); std::process::exit(0); }
            tick += 1;
            let arp_opt = if tick % 10 == 0 { Some(read_lan_neighbors()) } else { None };
            if let Ok(mut st) = poll_state.lock() {
                st.cached_status_json = build_status_snapshot(
                    &mut st,
                    &os_name_bg,
                    &kernel_ver_bg,
                    &host_name_bg,
                    &cpu_model_bg,
                    cpu_cores,
                    &default_iface_bg,
                );
                if let Some(arp) = arp_opt {
                    st.cached_arp_json = arp;
                }
            }
        }
    });

    unsafe { signal(2, on_exit_signal); signal(15, on_exit_signal); }
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:8080").expect("Failed to bind 127.0.0.1:8080");
    println!("[CachyWire Rust Engine] Listening on http://127.0.0.1:8080 (~3MB RAM, Native Kernel Telemetry)");

    for stream in listener.incoming().flatten() {
        let st_clone = Arc::clone(&shared_state);
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let _ = stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)));
            let _ = stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)));
            handle_client(stream, st_clone);
        });
    }
}

/* ================= END: ./src-rust-backend/main.rs ================= */

export function getAppIconSvg(appName = "", exePath = "") {
  const norm = (appName + " " + exePath).toLowerCase();
  if (norm.includes("language_server") || norm.includes("rust") || norm.includes("cargo")) return APP_ICONS.rust;
  if (norm.includes("antigravity") || norm.includes("agy")) return APP_ICONS.antigravity;
  if (norm.includes("chrome") || norm.includes("chromium")) return APP_ICONS.chrome;
  if (norm.includes("firefox") || norm.includes("librewolf")) return APP_ICONS.firefox;
  if (norm.includes("edge") || norm.includes("webview2")) return APP_ICONS.edge;
  if (norm.includes("brave")) return APP_ICONS.brave;
  if (norm.includes("teams")) return APP_ICONS.teams;
  if (norm.includes("teamviewer") || norm.includes("anydesk")) return APP_ICONS.teamviewer;
  if (norm.includes("ezoodownload") || norm.includes("download") || norm.includes("aria2") || norm.includes("qbittorrent")) return APP_ICONS.downloader;
  if (norm.includes("electron")) return APP_ICONS.electron;
  if (norm.includes("code") || norm.includes("vscode")) return APP_ICONS.vscode;
  if (norm.includes("telegram")) return APP_ICONS.telegram;
  if (norm.includes("discord")) return APP_ICONS.discord;
  if (norm.includes("steam") || norm.includes("lutris")) return APP_ICONS.steam;
  if (norm.includes("ssh") || norm.includes("fish") || norm.includes("bash") || norm.includes("alacritty") || norm.includes("kitty") || norm.includes("konsole")) return APP_ICONS.terminal;
  if (norm.includes("resolved") || norm.includes("dns") || norm.includes("systemd")) return APP_ICONS.systemd;
  if (norm.includes("networkmanager") || norm.includes("avahi") || norm.includes("wpa_supplicant")) return APP_ICONS.network;
  if (norm.includes("pacman") || norm.includes("paru") || norm.includes("yay") || norm.includes("cachyos") || norm.includes("arch")) return APP_ICONS.cachyos;
  if (norm.includes("node")) return APP_ICONS.node;
  if (norm.includes("outlook") || norm.includes("thunderbird")) return APP_ICONS.outlook;
  if (norm.includes("word") || norm.includes("winword")) return APP_ICONS.word;
  if (norm.includes("excel")) return APP_ICONS.excel;
  if (norm.includes("bitdefender") || norm.includes("bdservicehost")) return APP_ICONS.bitdefender;
  if (norm.includes("updater_temp") || norm.includes("suspicious") || norm.includes("malware")) return APP_ICONS.warning_app;
  if (norm.includes("svchost") || norm.includes("host process")) return APP_ICONS.windows;
  if (norm.includes("prism")) return APP_ICONS.prism;
  if (norm.includes("yt-dlp") || norm.includes("youtube")) return APP_ICONS.ytdlp;
  return APP_ICONS.cachyos;
}

/* ================= END: ./src/utils/app-icons.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/utils/crypto.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Cryptography & Binary Hashing
 * AUDIT CRYPTO-01: the old "pure JS fallback" was a non-cryptographic hashCode
 * padded with 'a'. A security product must NEVER silently degrade to a fake
 * hash — we now fail loudly instead.
 */
export async function computeSha256(input) {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.crypto && window.crypto.subtle) {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const data = typeof input === 'string' ? encoder.encode(input) : input;
    const hashBuffer = await window.crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
    const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
    return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  }
  // Node.js / CLI environment
  const cryptoModule = await import('crypto');
  if (cryptoModule && cryptoModule.createHash) {
    return cryptoModule.createHash('sha256').update(input).digest('hex');
  }
  throw new Error('computeSha256: no secure SHA-256 provider available in this environment');
}

export function generateRandomToken(bytesLen = 16) {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.crypto && window.crypto.getRandomValues) {
    const array = new Uint8Array(bytesLen);
    window.crypto.getRandomValues(array);
    return Array.from(array, b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  }
  throw new Error('generateRandomToken: window.crypto.getRandomValues unavailable');
}

/* ================= END: ./src/utils/crypto.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/utils/escape.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - HTML Sanitizer / Escaper (SEC-03)
 * Prevents DOM-based XSS when rendering process-controlled or network-derived strings.
 */
export function esc(str) {
  if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

export function escapeHtml(str) {
  return esc(str);
}

/* ================= END: ./src/utils/escape.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/utils/formatters.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Formatters & Display Helpers
 * Implements high-precision units, tabular numeric formatting, and date display
 */

export function formatBytes(bytes, decimals = 1) {
  if (bytes === 0 || bytes === null || bytes === undefined || isNaN(bytes)) return '0 B';
  const abs = Math.abs(bytes);
  if (abs < 1024) return `${Math.round(bytes)} B`;
  const k = 1024;
  const dm = decimals < 0 ? 0 : decimals;
  const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB', 'PB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(abs) / Math.log(k));
  const safeI = Math.min(i, sizes.length - 1);
  const formatted = (bytes / Math.pow(k, safeI)).toFixed(dm);
  return `${formatted} ${sizes[safeI]}`;
}

export function formatSpeed(bytesPerSec, decimals = 1) {
  return `${formatBytes(bytesPerSec, decimals)}/s`;
}

export function formatBitsSpeed(bytesPerSec, decimals = 1) {
  if (!bytesPerSec || bytesPerSec === 0) return '0 bps';
  const bits = bytesPerSec * 8;
  const k = 1000;
  const dm = decimals < 0 ? 0 : decimals;
  const sizes = ['bps', 'Kbps', 'Mbps', 'Gbps'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bits) / Math.log(k));
  const safeI = Math.min(i, sizes.length - 1);
  const formatted = (bits / Math.pow(k, safeI)).toFixed(dm);
  return `${formatted} ${sizes[safeI]}`;
}

export function formatTimestamp(tsMs) {
  if (!tsMs) return '--:--:--';
  const d = new Date(tsMs);
  return d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit', hour12: false });
}

export function formatDate(tsMs) {
  if (!tsMs) return 'Unknown';
  const d = new Date(tsMs);
  return d.toLocaleDateString([], { year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
}

export function formatDateTime(tsMs) {
  if (!tsMs) return 'Unknown';
  const d = new Date(tsMs);
  return `${formatDate(tsMs)} ${formatTimestamp(tsMs)}`;
}

export function formatMacAddress(mac) {
  if (!mac) return '--:--:--:--:--:--';
  return mac.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-F0-9]/g, '').replace(/(..)(?=.)/g, '$1:');
}

/* ================= END: ./src/utils/formatters.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/utils/geoip.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Offline GeoIP & Country Resolution
 * AUDIT DATA-01: the old deterministic pseudo-hash invented a country for any
 * unmapped IP (presented as fact). We now return an explicit UNKNOWN bucket
 * for anything outside the curated well-known prefix table. Honesty first.
 */
const CURATED_PREFIXES = [
  { prefix: '142.250.', iso2: 'US', name: 'United States' },
{ prefix: '172.217.', iso2: 'US', name: 'United States' },
{ prefix: '185.199.', iso2: 'DE', name: 'Germany' },
{ prefix: '52.',      iso2: 'US', name: 'United States' },
{ prefix: '54.',      iso2: 'US', name: 'United States' },
{ prefix: '13.',      iso2: 'US', name: 'United States' },
{ prefix: '104.',     iso2: 'US', name: 'United States' },
{ prefix: '151.101.', iso2: 'GB', name: 'United Kingdom' },
{ prefix: '162.158.', iso2: 'FR', name: 'France' },
{ prefix: '193.186.', iso2: 'EG', name: 'Egypt' },
{ prefix: '156.208.', iso2: 'EG', name: 'Egypt' },
{ prefix: '103.',     iso2: 'SG', name: 'Singapore' },
{ prefix: '114.119.', iso2: 'CN', name: 'China' },
{ prefix: '133.',     iso2: 'JP', name: 'Japan' },
{ prefix: '177.',     iso2: 'BR', name: 'Brazil' },
{ prefix: '139.162.', iso2: 'DE', name: 'Germany' },
{ prefix: '198.51.',  iso2: 'CA', name: 'Canada' },
{ prefix: '203.0.',   iso2: 'AU', name: 'Australia' }
];

const ISO_TO_FLAG = {
  US: '🇺🇸', DE: '🇩🇪', EG: '🇪🇬', GB: '🇬🇧', FR: '🇫🇷', SG: '🇸🇬',
  CN: '🇨🇳', JP: '🇯🇵', BR: '🇧🇷', CA: '🇨🇦', AU: '🇦🇺', NL: '🇳🇱',
  IN: '🇮', GLOBAL: '🌐'
};

export function lookupGeoIp(ipAddress) {
  if (!ipAddress) return { iso2: 'UNKNOWN', country: 'Unknown', flag: '🌐', known: false };
  // Loopback / LAN (BR-03 ranges)
  if (
    ipAddress.startsWith('127.') ||
    ipAddress.startsWith('10.') ||
    ipAddress.startsWith('192.168.') ||
    ipAddress.startsWith('169.254.') ||
    ipAddress === '::1' ||
    ipAddress.startsWith('fe80:')
  ) {
    return { iso2: 'LAN', country: 'Local Network', flag: '🏠', known: true };
  }
  for (const entry of CURATED_PREFIXES) {
    if (ipAddress.startsWith(entry.prefix)) {
      return { iso2: entry.iso2, country: entry.name, flag: ISO_TO_FLAG[entry.iso2] || '🌐', known: true };
    }
  }
  // AUDIT DATA-01: no fabrication. Unmapped IPs get an honest UNKNOWN bucket.
  return { iso2: 'UNKNOWN', country: 'Unknown (no offline GeoIP entry)', flag: '🌐', known: false };
}

/* ================= END: ./src/utils/geoip.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./src/utils/oui-vendors.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - MAC OUI Vendor Database Lookup
 * Resolves MAC prefixes to device hardware manufacturers (FUNC-06, MOD-SVC-05)
 */

const OUI_TABLE = {
  '00:1A:2B': 'Cisco Systems',
  '00:50:56': 'VMware, Inc.',
  '00:0C:29': 'VMware, Inc.',
  '08:00:27': 'Oracle VirtualBox',
  'B8:27:EB': 'Raspberry Pi Foundation',
  'DC:A6:32': 'Raspberry Pi Foundation',
  'F0:18:98': 'Apple, Inc.',
  '3C:06:30': 'Apple, Inc.',
  '70:4F:57': 'Huawei Technologies',
  '48:8A:D2': 'Huawei Technologies',
  'AC:D1:B8': 'Samsung Electronics',
  '18:65:90': 'Samsung Electronics',
  'FC:EC:DA': 'Ubiquiti Networks',
  '24:A4:3C': 'Ubiquiti Networks',
  '00:15:5D': 'Microsoft Corporation (Hyper-V)',
  'E4:5F:01': 'Raspberry Pi Foundation',
  'D8:3A:DD': 'Intel Corporate',
  '00:E0:4C': 'Realtek Semiconductor'
};

export function lookupMacVendor(macAddress) {
  if (!macAddress) return 'Unknown Hardware';
  const cleanMac = macAddress.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-F0-9]/g, '');
  if (cleanMac.length < 6) return 'Generic Device';
  const prefix = `${cleanMac.substring(0, 2)}:${cleanMac.substring(2, 4)}:${cleanMac.substring(4, 6)}`;
  return OUI_TABLE[prefix] || 'Standard Network Adapter';
}

/* ================= END: ./src/utils/oui-vendors.js ================= */

/* ================= FILE: ./tests/run-all-tests.js ================= */
/**
 * CachyWire 0.1.0-alpha - Automated Test Suite
 * Tests Core Business Logic, Database, Firewall, and Security Engines
 */

import { CachyWireDatabase } from '../src/data/db.js';
import { ApplicationsRepository } from '../src/data/applications-repo.js';
import { HostsRepository } from '../src/data/hosts-repo.js';
import { TrafficRepository } from '../src/data/traffic-repo.js';
import { FirewallPolicyController } from '../src/business/firewall-policy.js';
import { SecurityScoreEngine } from '../src/business/security-score.js';
import { esc } from '../src/utils/escape.js';
import { FIREWALL_MODES } from '../src/config/default-settings.js';

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;

function assert(condition, message) {
  if (condition) {
    console.log(`  ✓ ${message}`);
    passed++;
  } else {
    console.error(`  ✗ FAIL: ${message}`);
    failed++;
  }
}

async function runTests() {
  console.log('[*] Running CachyWire Test Suite...\n');

  // Test 1: HTML Escaping (SEC-03)
  console.log('1. Security: HTML Escaper (SEC-03)');
  assert(esc('<script>alert(1)</script>') === '&lt;script&gt;alert(1)&lt;/script&gt;', 'Sanitizes script tags');
  assert(esc('image" onerror="alert(1)') === 'image&quot; onerror=&quot;alert(1)', 'Sanitizes attribute injection');
  assert(esc(null) === '' && esc(undefined) === '', 'Handles null and undefined cleanly');

  // Test 2: Database Initialization & CRUD
  console.log('\n2. Data Layer: CachyWireDatabase');
  const db = new CachyWireDatabase({ storageKey: 'test_db_key' });
  await db.initialize();
  const app = db.registerApplication({
    exe_path: '/usr/bin/cachyos-updater',
    app_name: 'CachyOS Updater',
    publisher: 'CachyOS Team',
    is_signed: 1
  });
  assert(app && app.app_id > 0, 'Registers new application with auto-increment ID');
  assert(db.getApplicationByPath('/usr/bin/cachyos-updater')?.app_id === app.app_id, 'Indexes application by lowercase path (BR-01)');

  const host = db.getOrCreateRemoteHost('1.1.1.1', 'one.one.one.one', 'US', 'Cloudflare');
  assert(host && host.ip_address === '1.1.1.1', 'Creates remote host with IP and ASN');

  // Test 3: Traffic Stats Bucketing & Rollup
  console.log('\n3. Data Layer: Traffic Repository & Downsampling');
  const trafficRepo = new TrafficRepository(db);
  trafficRepo.recordFlow({
    timestamp: Date.now(),
    adapter_id: 'eno1',
    app_id: app.app_id,
    host_id: 1,
    proto_id: 1,
    bytes_in: 1024,
    bytes_out: 512
  });
  const flows = db.queryTrafficStats({ resolutionSec: 1 });
  assert(flows.length > 0 && flows[0].bytes_in === 1024, 'Records live traffic flow correctly');

  // Test 4: Firewall Policy Controller
  console.log('\n4. Business Logic: Firewall Policy & Critical Exemptions (BR-05)');
  const appsRepo = new ApplicationsRepository(db);
  const alertsRepo = { create: () => ({ alert_id: 1 }) };
  const fw = new FirewallPolicyController(appsRepo, alertsRepo, { enabled: true, mode: FIREWALL_MODES.CLICK_TO_BLOCK });

  assert(!fw.isApplicationBlocked(app.exe_path), 'Default application is allowed');
  fw.toggleBlock(app.app_id);
  assert(fw.isApplicationBlocked(app.exe_path), 'Toggling block marks application blocked');

  // Lockdown mode with DHCP exemption
  fw.setMode(FIREWALL_MODES.BLOCK_ALL);
  assert(fw.isApplicationBlocked(app.exe_path), 'General apps blocked in BLOCK_ALL');
  assert(!fw.isApplicationBlocked('/usr/sbin/dhcpcd', 67, 17), 'DHCP UDP 67 exempt in BLOCK_ALL (BR-05)');
  assert(!fw.isApplicationBlocked('/usr/bin/local-ipc', 8080, 6, '127.0.0.1'), 'Loopback exempt in BLOCK_ALL (BR-05)');

  // Test 5: Security Score Engine
  console.log('\n5. Business Logic: Security Score Engine');
  const scoreEngine = new SecurityScoreEngine(appsRepo, alertsRepo);
  const safeScore = scoreEngine.calculateSecurityScore(app);
  assert(safeScore >= 80, `Trusted signed system app receives high score (${safeScore})`);

  const tmpApp = db.registerApplication({
    exe_path: '/tmp/evil_payload.sh',
    app_name: 'evil_payload.sh',
    is_signed: 0
  });
  const tmpScore = scoreEngine.calculateSecurityScore(tmpApp);
  assert(tmpScore < safeScore, `Execution from /tmp receives security penalty (${tmpScore} < ${safeScore})`);

  // Summary
  console.log(`\n========================================`);
  console.log(`Tests Finished: ${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
  console.log(`========================================`);

  if (failed > 0) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runTests().catch(err => {
  console.error('Fatal test error:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

/* ================= END: ./tests/run-all-tests.js ================= */
